```
1	# Advancements + Statistics
2	
3	## Context
4	
5	Monecraft (v0.11.0, save v12) is now content-rich — 7 gear tiers, 11 enchantments,
6	9 potions/effects, an anvil/grindstone/enchanting loop, farming, breeding, fishing,
7	a boss — but **goal-poor**: the only objective is "beat the Dragon Lord," and new
8	players have no guided path through the systems. After three consecutive
9	combat/gear releases (#35/#36/#37), this adds the missing **progression meta**:
10	
11	- **Statistics** — running counters (blocks mined, mobs killed, distance walked,
12	  deaths, items crafted/enchanted, potions drunk, fish caught, time played …).
13	- **Advancements** — a tight, flat set of ~15 achievements that unlock when the
14	  player engages each major system, with an unlock toast + chime.
15	
16	Both **persist in the save and survive death** (like `xp`, not `effects`), surface
17	in a dedicated **`L`-key overlay** (two tabs), and add **zero binary assets** (icons
18	reuse existing item/block sprites). User-confirmed scope: **L-key overlay**, **tight
19	~15 flat set**.
20	
21	## Design
22	
23	### Engine — one chokepoint, declarative registry
24	
25	Every gameplay event already funnels through `GameEngine.emit()`
26	(`lib/game/engine/GameEngine.ts` ~L668; 29 event types in `lib/game/engine/state.ts`).
27	Hook a single observer there rather than editing 15 system files:
28	
29	```
30	private emit = (event) => {
31	  this.events.push(event);
32	  if (event.type !== "advancementUnlocked") this.observeProgress(event); // guard recursion
33	};
34	private observeProgress(event) {
35	  recordEvent(this.state, event);                       // bump stat counters
36	  for (const id of evaluateAdvancements(this.state))    // locked-only, threshold met
37	    { this.state.advancements.add(id); this.emit({ type:"advancementUnlocked", id, name }); }
38	}
39	```
40	
41	New module **`lib/game/engine/systems/advancements.ts`**:
42	- `recordEvent(state, event)` — maps each event type to stat increments (e.g.
43	  `blockBroken`→`blocks_mined` + a per-ore counter for ore blocks; `mobDied`→
44	  `hostiles_killed` when the kind is hostile, via `MOB_TEMPLATES[kind].hostile`;
45	  `enchanted`→`items_enchanted`; `drankPotion`→`potions_drunk`; `fishingCaught`→
46	  `fish_caught`; `mobBred`→`animals_bred`; `bossDefeated`→`boss_defeated`;
47	  `crafted`→`items_crafted` + `crafted_<recipeId>`; `died`→`deaths`; `jumped`→`jumps`).
48	- `STATS` — display metadata for the Statistics tab (id → label).
49	- `ADVANCEMENTS` — **declarative** registry: `{ id, title, description, icon, category,
50	  stat, threshold }`. Unlock is uniformly `state.stats.get(stat) >= threshold`, so
51	  `evaluateAdvancements(state)` just iterates the not-yet-unlocked entries — trivially
52	  unit-testable. `icon` is an existing item/block id rendered via `itemIconUrl`
53	  (`lib/ui/sprites.ts`) — e.g. Diamonds!→`diamond_ore`, Enchanter→`enchanting_table`,
54	  Dragon Slayer→`dragon_heart` — **zero new assets**.
55	- Tick-driven display stats (`distance_walked` from `move.horizontalDistance`,
56	  `play_time` from `dt`) increment directly in `GameEngine.step()`; no advancement
57	  depends on them, so they stay out of the unlock path.
58	
59	**Proposed ~15 advancements** (tunable; all map to detectable signals):
60	Getting Wood (mine a log), Stone Age (mine stone), Tool Up (craft a pickaxe),
61	Hot Topic (craft a furnace), Iron-ish (mine sliver ore), Diamonds! (mine diamond
62	ore), Take Aim (fire a bow — `bowFired`), Monster Hunter (kill a hostile), Two by
63	Two (breed animals), Time to Farm (harvest mature wheat — `blockBroken`
64	WheatStage3), Enchanter (enchant an item), Local Brewery (drink a potion), Sleep
65	Tight (`sleepStarted`), Hired Help (trade with a villager — `crafted` w/ a
66	`station:"villager"` recipe), Gone Fishing (`fishingCaught`), Dragon Slayer
67	(`bossDefeated`).
68	
69	### New `crafted` event (small, broadly useful)
70	
71	There is no craft event today. Add `{ type:"crafted"; recipeId:string }` to the
72	`GameEvent` union and emit it once in the `craft` command handler
73	(`GameEngine.dispatch`). Unlocks craft/brew/trade advancements + an `items_crafted`
74	stat; the audio director can ignore it.
75	
76	### Persistence — save v12 → v13 (additive, death-proof)
77	
78	Mirror the `xp` pattern exactly (`lib/game/save.ts` `restoreXp`; serialized in
79	`GameEngine.serialize`; restored in the constructor; **not** touched by
80	`clearEffects`):
81	- `GameState` += `stats: Map<string,number>`, `advancements: Set<string>`.
82	- `SaveData` (`lib/game/types.ts`) += `stats?: Array<{id:string;value:number}>`,
83	  `advancements?: string[]`; bump to `version: 13`.
84	- `save.ts`: `serializeStats`/`restoreStats` + `restoreAdvancements` (finite ≥0
85	  values, string ids; mirror `serializeEffects`/`restoreEffects` at L334–353),
86	  `migrateSaveV12toV13` pure bump, extend the `readSave` v1→…→v13 chain.
87	  `SAVE_KEY` unchanged; **`WORLDGEN_VERSION` stays 8**.
88	
89	### UI — dedicated `L`-key overlay
90	
91	- Engine flag: `GameState.advancementsOpen` + project `advancementsOpen:boolean`
92	  and a low-churn `advancementsUnlocked:number` (count) into `GameSnapshot`
93	  (both primitives — safe under the per-key `!==` compare in `refreshSnapshot`).
94	- `Command` += `{ type:"toggleAdvancements" }`; `GameEngine.dispatch` toggles the
95	  flag (mirror `toggleInventory`).
96	- Input: `KeyL` handler in `lib/game/input/inputController.ts` (mirror the `KeyI`
97	  block — dispatch, clear keys, exit pointer lock).
98	- `GameApi` (`lib/game/engine/state.ts`) += `advancementState(): { stats:
99	  Array<{id,value}>; unlocked: string[] }` so the panel reads detail **on render**
100	  via `snapshot.api` — avoids per-frame snapshot churn; the panel re-renders
101	  whenever the snapshot changes (frequent during play) and on every unlock.
102	- New `components/game/AdvancementsPanel.tsx`, rendered in
103	  `components/MinecraftGame.tsx` when `snapshot.advancementsOpen` (mirror the
104	  `inventoryOpen` backdrop+panel block). Two tabs (PauseMenu tab pattern): an
105	  **Advancements** grid grouped by category with locked/unlocked styling (mirror
106	  the recipe-book grid in `InventoryPanel.tsx` + `PixelImg`/`ItemIcon`) and a
107	  **Statistics** list. CSS in `app/hud.css` mirroring `.pause-overlay`/`.recipe-*`.
108	- Toast: listen for `advancementUnlocked` in the `useMinecraftGame` event-drain
109	  loop (~L293–329) → `flashMessage(\`Advancement Unlocked: ${event.name}\`)`
110	  (the `pickedUp` pattern). Audio: a chime in `lib/game/audio/audioDirector.ts`.
111	
112	## Files
113	
114	- New: `lib/game/engine/systems/advancements.ts` (recordEvent, STATS,
115	  ADVANCEMENTS, evaluateAdvancements) + `.test.ts`;
116	  `components/game/AdvancementsPanel.tsx` + `.test.tsx`.
117	- Engine: `lib/game/engine/state.ts` (GameState/GameSnapshot/GameEvent/GameApi),
118	  `lib/game/engine/commands.ts`, `lib/game/engine/GameEngine.ts` (emit observer,
119	  step tick-stats, dispatch toggle + `crafted` emit, serialize/restore, snapshot).
120	- Save: `lib/game/types.ts`, `lib/game/save.ts` (v13 + migration + (de)serialize).
121	- Input/UI: `lib/game/input/inputController.ts`, `components/MinecraftGame.tsx`,
122	  `lib/game/useMinecraftGame.ts` (toast), `app/hud.css`,
123	  `lib/game/audio/audioDirector.ts`.
124	- Reuse: `itemIconUrl` (`lib/ui/sprites.ts`), `PixelImg`/`ItemIcon`, the recipe-book
125	  grid + PauseMenu tab patterns, `MOB_TEMPLATES` (`lib/game/mobs.ts`) for hostility.
126	
127	### Commit slices (branch `feat/advancements`)
128	
129	1. `feat(stats): track gameplay statistics` — stats Map + recordEvent + step
130	   tick-stats + the `crafted` event + save v13 persistence of `stats`; tests.
131	2. `feat(advancements): registry + unlock tracking` — ADVANCEMENTS, the emit
132	   observer, `advancementUnlocked` event, `advancements` Set persistence; tests.
133	3. `feat(ui): advancements & statistics overlay (L key)` — flag/command/KeyL,
134	   GameApi accessor, AdvancementsPanel + tabs + CSS, toast + chime; component/e2e.
135	4. `docs` — reference (advancements + stats tables), manual (open with L; what
136	   unlocks), save-format (v13), adding-content (new "An advancement / a stat"
137	   section — the extension pattern), testing.md coverage, CHANGELOG `[Unreleased]`.
138	
139	## Verification
140	
141	- Full CI `verify` gate green (`lint`, `typecheck`, `format:check`, `bun test`,
142	  `build`) + `bun run test:e2e` (renderer/shell touched).
143	- Engine tests (`advancements.test.ts`): `recordEvent` bumps the right counter per
144	  event (incl. per-ore + hostile-only kill); `evaluateAdvancements` unlocks exactly
145	  at the threshold, never twice, and is order-independent; the `crafted` event
146	  drives `items_crafted`/trade unlock.
147	- `GameEngine.test.ts`: a headless run that breaks an ore / kills a hostile / enchants
148	  / drinks a potion / defeats the boss emits `advancementUnlocked` once each and the
149	  stats reflect it; **a full save round-trip preserves stats + advancements and they
150	  survive a death** (assert not cleared, unlike effects).
151	- `save.test.ts`: `migrateSaveV12toV13` pure bump; `serializeStats`/`restoreStats`/
152	  `restoreAdvancements` round-trip + drop garbage; the v1→…→v13 chain. Update the
153	  e2e save-version assertion 12 → 13.
154	- Component test (`AdvancementsPanel.test.tsx`): renders locked vs unlocked entries,
155	  switches tabs, shows stat values. e2e: press `L` → overlay opens (pointer lock
156	  released) → close.
157	- Manual pass (not headless-verifiable): the overlay layout/icons and the unlock
158	  toast + chime.

id: toolu_01KkeQFQBZsTaZgUGUspR6F6
```
1	import { renderSpritePixels, SPRITE_SIZE } from "./spritePixels";
2	import { renderHudIconPixels, type HudIconName } from "./hudPixels";
3	import { renderSkinPortraitPixels } from "./skinPortrait";
4	import { getSkinPreset, type SkinId } from "@/lib/game/playerSkins";
5	
6	/**
7	 * DOM side of the sprite system: turns pure pixel buffers into cached data
8	 * URLs for <img> tags. happy-dom has no 2D canvas context, so everything
9	 * degrades to a transparent pixel there — components stay testable while the
10	 * pixel logic itself is covered by the pure-module tests.
11	 */
12	
13	export const TRANSPARENT_PIXEL = "data:image/gif;base64,R0lGODlhAQABAIAAAAAAAP///yH5BAEAAAAALAAAAAABAAEAAAIBRAA7";
14	
15	const cache = new Map<string, string>();
16	
17	export function spriteDataUrl(key: string, pixels: () => Uint8ClampedArray): string {
18	  const hit = cache.get(key);
19	  if (hit) return hit;
20	
21	  let url = TRANSPARENT_PIXEL;
22	  if (typeof document !== "undefined") {
23	    const canvas = document.createElement("canvas");
24	    canvas.width = SPRITE_SIZE;
25	    canvas.height = SPRITE_SIZE;
26	    const ctx = canvas.getContext("2d");
27	    if (ctx) {
28	      // Copy into a fresh buffer: ImageData requires a non-shared ArrayBuffer.
29	      ctx.putImageData(new ImageData(new Uint8ClampedArray(pixels()), SPRITE_SIZE, SPRITE_SIZE), 0, 0);
30	      url = canvas.toDataURL();
31	    }
32	  }
33	  cache.set(key, url);
34	  return url;
35	}
36	
37	/** Data URL for an inventory item's 16x16 icon. */
38	export function itemIconUrl(itemId: string): string {
39	  return spriteDataUrl(`item:${itemId}`, () => renderSpritePixels(itemId));
40	}
41	
42	/** Data URL for a HUD icon (hearts, drumsticks, armor). */
43	export function hudIconUrl(name: HudIconName): string {
44	  return spriteDataUrl(`hud:${name}`, () => renderHudIconPixels(name));
45	}
46	
47	/** Data URL for a skin preset's 16x16 bust portrait (pause-menu picker). */
48	export function skinPortraitUrl(skinId: SkinId): string {
49	  return spriteDataUrl(`skin:${skinId}`, () => renderSkinPortraitPixels(getSkinPreset(skinId).palette));
50	}
51	
```

116	/**
117	 * Body-center height above the ground for a mob kind. Mirrors the geometry
118	 * math in createMobModel so the headless simulation needs no Three.js meshes.
119	 */
120	export function mobHalfHeight(kind: MobKind): number {
121	  const bodyHeight = MOB_TEMPLATES[kind].modelArgs[5][1];
122	  const legHeight = Math.max(0.3, bodyHeight * 0.56);
123	  return Math.max(bodyHeight, legHeight) * 0.5 + 0.2;
124	}
125	
126	/** Builds the Three.js model for a mob kind (renderer side). */
127	export function createMobModelForKind(kind: MobKind) {
128	  return createMobModel(...MOB_TEMPLATES[kind].modelArgs);
129	}

I now have everything I need. Key confirmations and refinements:

- **`itemIconUrl(id)` → `renderSpritePixels(id)`** is a single renderer; blocks held in the hotbar render through it, so block-id icons work — but each chosen id must be one `renderSpritePixels` handles (verify per-icon during build; fall back to a guaranteed item id).
- **No `hostile` on `MobTemplate`** — I'll add a `HOSTILE_MOB_KINDS` set (`zombie, skeleton, spider, creeper, boss`) as the single source of truth in `lib/game/mobs.ts` and read it in `recordEvent`.
- **`crafted` event** carries `recipeId`; `recordEvent` resolves the recipe's station (mirroring the craft handler's own `RECIPES.find`) to drive the villager-trade advancement.

The plan is detailed, user-confirmed, and now validated against the actual code. I'll write the final implementation plan.

> AGENT

The plan is written and validated against the codebase. Here's the summary before I hand it to you for approval:

**What's being built:** A progression-meta layer for Monecraft — persistent **Statistics** counters and a tight **~15-advancement** flat set, surfaced in an `L`-key overlay with two tabs, an unlock toast + chime, and zero new binary assets (icons reuse existing sprites). Both persist in the save and survive death like `xp`.

**Architecture:** One observer hooked into the single `emit` chokepoint (no editing 15 system files) + a declarative `advancements.ts` registry that's trivially unit-testable. Save bumps **v12 → v13** (additive, death-proof, worldgen untouched).

**Three things my exploration corrected from the original draft** (all resolved in the plan):
1. `MOB_TEMPLATES` has **no `hostile` field** → added a `HOSTILE_MOB_KINDS` set as the source of truth for "kill a hostile."
2. No `crafted` event exists → adding one; `recordEvent` resolves the recipe's station for the villager-trade advancement.
3. Advancement icons use `PixelImg` + `itemIconUrl` directly (single renderer handles blocks and items), with a per-icon fallback noted.

It ships as **4 focused commits** on `feat/advancements`, with the full CI verify gate + e2e + a manual icon/toast pass.

A note on scope: this is a substantial multi-system feature (engine, save migration, UI, audio, docs). Once you approve, I'll implement all four slices, keep lint green per commit, and run the full verify gate — but per your saved preference I'll **commit locally and not push or open the PR until you've reviewed**.

## Approved Plan (edited by user):
# Advancements + Statistics

Branch: `feat/advancements` — branch → focused commits → PR (`gh pr create --repo hutusi/monecraft --base main`).

## Context

Monecraft (v0.11.0, save **v12**) is content-rich but **goal-poor** — the only objective
is "beat the Dragon Lord," and new players get no guided tour of the systems. After
three combat/gear releases (#35/#36/#37), this adds the missing **progression meta**:

- **Statistics** — running counters (blocks mined, mobs killed, distance walked, deaths,
  items crafted/enchanted, potions drunk, fish caught, time played…).
- **Advancements** — a tight, flat set of ~15 achievements that unlock as the player
  engages each major system, with an unlock toast + chime.

Both **persist in the save and survive death** (like `xp`, not `effects`), surface in a
dedicated **`L`-key overlay** (two tabs), and add **zero binary assets** (icons reuse
existing item/block sprites). User-confirmed scope: **L-key overlay**, **tight ~15 flat set**.

This plan is verified against the current code; integration points and the three
refinements below are confirmed.

## Verified integration points

- **Emit chokepoint** — every gameplay event funnels through `GameEngine.emit`
  (`lib/game/engine/GameEngine.ts` L668–670: `private emit = (event) => { this.events.push(event); }`).
  Hook one observer here. The `GameEvent` union (`lib/game/engine/state.ts` L321–369, ~48 types)
  already carries every signal we need: `blockBroken{blockId,x,y,z}`, `mobDied{kind,…}`,
  `enchanted{enchant}`, `drankPotion`, `fishingCaught{items,…}`, `mobBred{kind}`,
  `bossDefeated`, `died`, `jumped`, `bowFired`, `sleepStarted`, `pickedUp`.
- **`xp` persistence pattern to mirror** — `GameState.xp` (`state.ts` L171, "NOT cleared on
  death"); serialized in `GameEngine.serialize` (L649 `xp: state.xp`); restored in the
  constructor (L247 `restoreXp(save)`, after effects). `clearEffects`
  (`systems/statusEffects.ts` L54–58) touches **only** `state.effects` + timers — it never
  references `xp`, so new GameState fields are death-proof for free. `restoreXp`/
  `serializeEffects`/`restoreEffects` (`save.ts` L334–353, L442–446) are the validation templates.
- **Snapshot** — `refreshSnapshot` does a blanket per-key `!==` compare
  (`GameEngine.ts` L1019–1026), so adding **primitive** snapshot keys is safe. `inventoryOpen`
  is a ref pass-through (L1001); `xpLevel`/`xpProgress` show the projection pattern.
- **`GameApi`** is `{ dispatch, serialize }` (`state.ts` L265–268) and `api: this` in the
  snapshot — adding an accessor method is trivial.
- **UI patterns** — `KeyI` block (`inputController.ts` L85–90: dispatch, `keys.clear()`,
  `exitPointerLock`); `inventoryOpen` backdrop+panel gate (`MinecraftGame.tsx` L159–189);
  recipe-book grid + `ItemIcon`/`PixelImg` (`InventoryPanel.tsx` L167–194, 270–283); PauseMenu
  tabs (`PauseMenu.tsx` L28–34, 75–107); event-drain `flashMessage` loop with `event.name`
  available (`useMinecraftGame.ts` L293–330); audio `handleEvent` switch with `XP_SOUND`
  (`audioDirector.ts` L194–315); `.pause-overlay`/`.pause-tabs`/`.recipe-*` CSS
  (`hud.css`, `ui.css`).

### Three refinements (resolved)

1. **Hostility source of truth.** `MOB_TEMPLATES[kind]` has **no `hostile` field** (it's
   passed at spawn). Add `export const HOSTILE_MOB_KINDS: ReadonlySet<MobKind>` =
   `{zombie, skeleton, spider, creeper, boss}` to `lib/game/mobs.ts` (next to
   `MOB_TEMPLATES`). `recordEvent` reads it for the `hostiles_killed` stat / Monster Hunter.
2. **`crafted` event for trades.** No craft event exists today (the handler only emits
   `smelted` for station recipes — `GameEngine.ts` L374–385). Add `{ type:"crafted"; recipeId:string }`
   to the union and emit it on every successful craft. `recordEvent` resolves the recipe's
   `station` (via `RECIPES`, as the craft handler already does) to bump a `trades` stat when
   `station === "villager"`. Keep the existing `smelted` emit (audio depends on it).
3. **Icons.** `itemIconUrl(id)` → `renderSpritePixels(id)` (`lib/ui/sprites.ts` L38–40) — a
   single renderer; blocks render the same as items. Advancement tiles use
   `<PixelImg src={itemIconUrl(icon)} …>` directly (no fake inventory slot). Each `icon` id
   must be one `renderSpritePixels` handles — verify per-icon at build time; fall back to a
   guaranteed item id if a block id doesn't render.

## Implementation

### New module — `lib/game/engine/systems/advancements.ts`

Declarative, trivially unit-testable:

- `recordEvent(state, event)` — maps each event to stat increments:
  `blockBroken` → `blocks_mined` (+ per-ore counter for ore blockIds, + a wheat-harvest
  counter for the mature-wheat blockId); `mobDied` → `hostiles_killed` when
  `HOSTILE_MOB_KINDS.has(event.kind)`; `enchanted` → `items_enchanted`; `drankPotion` →
  `potions_drunk`; `fishingCaught` → `fish_caught`; `mobBred` → `animals_bred`;
  `bossDefeated` → `boss_defeated`; `bowFired` → `arrows_fired`; `sleepStarted` → `sleeps`;
  `died` → `deaths`; `jumped` → `jumps`; `crafted` → `items_crafted` + `crafted_<recipeId>`
  + `trades` when the recipe's station is `villager`.
- `STATS` — display metadata (`id → label`) for the Statistics tab.
- `ADVANCEMENTS` — registry of `{ id, title, description, icon, category, stat, threshold }`.
  Unlock is uniformly `state.stats.get(stat) ?? 0 >= threshold`. `ADVANCEMENTS_BY_ID` lookup
  for titles. `icon` is an existing item/block id rendered via `itemIconUrl`.
- `evaluateAdvancements(state)` — returns the **not-yet-unlocked** ids whose threshold is met
  (order-independent; never re-returns an unlocked id).

**~15 advancements** (tunable, all map to detectable signals): Getting Wood (mine a log),
Stone Age (mine stone), Tool Up (craft a pickaxe), Hot Topic (craft a furnace), Iron-ish
(mine sliver ore), Diamonds! (mine diamond ore), Take Aim (`bowFired`), Monster Hunter (kill
a hostile), Two by Two (`mobBred`), Time to Farm (harvest mature wheat), Enchanter
(`enchanted`), Local Brewery (`drankPotion`), Sleep Tight (`sleepStarted`), Hired Help
(`trades`), Gone Fishing (`fishingCaught`), Dragon Slayer (`bossDefeated`).

### Engine wiring — `GameEngine.ts` / `state.ts` / `commands.ts`

- `GameState` += `stats: Map<string,number>` and `advancements: Set<string>` (near `xp`,
  `state.ts` ~L171); `advancementsOpen: boolean` (near `inventoryOpen` L174). Initialize empty
  in the constructor; restore from save after the `xp` restore.
- **Emit observer** (`GameEngine.ts` L668):
  ```ts
  private emit = (event: GameEvent): void => {
    this.events.push(event);
    if (event.type !== "advancementUnlocked") this.observeProgress(event); // guard recursion
  };
  private observeProgress(event: GameEvent): void {
    recordEvent(this.state, event);
    for (const id of evaluateAdvancements(this.state)) {
      this.state.advancements.add(id);                                       // add before emit → idempotent
      this.emit({ type: "advancementUnlocked", id, name: ADVANCEMENTS_BY_ID[id].title });
    }
  }
  ```
- `GameEvent` union += `{ type:"crafted"; recipeId:string }` and
  `{ type:"advancementUnlocked"; id:string; name:string }` (`state.ts`).
- `craft` handler (`GameEngine.ts` L374–385) — emit `{ type:"crafted", recipeId: recipe.id }`
  on success (keep the existing `smelted` emit).
- **Tick stats** in `step()` after `const move = tickPlayerMotion(...)` (L324): increment
  `distance_walked` by `move.horizontalDistance` and `play_time` by `dt` directly on
  `state.stats` (no event, no advancement depends on them — they stay out of the unlock path).
- `Command` union (`commands.ts`) += `{ type:"toggleAdvancements" }`; `dispatch` toggles
  `state.advancementsOpen` (mirror the `toggleInventory` case L365–372).
- Snapshot (`buildSnapshot`) += `advancementsOpen: state.advancementsOpen` and
  `advancementsUnlocked: state.advancements.size` (both primitives). `GameSnapshot` type updated.
- `GameApi` += `advancementState(): { stats: Array<{id:string;value:number}>; unlocked: string[] }`
  implemented on the engine (reads `state.stats`/`state.advancements`) — the panel reads detail
  **on render** via `snapshot.api` to avoid per-frame snapshot churn.

### Persistence — save v12 → v13 (additive, death-proof)

- `lib/game/types.ts`: rename the current `SaveData` body to `SaveDataV12`; add
  `SavedStat = { id:string; value:number }`; define
  `SaveData = Omit<SaveDataV12,"version"> & { version:13; stats?: SavedStat[]; advancements?: string[] }`.
- `lib/game/save.ts`: add `serializeStats(map)` / `restoreStats(save)` (finite, ≥0 values;
  mirror `serializeEffects`/`restoreEffects`) and `restoreAdvancements(save)` (string ids,
  dedup → array); add `migrateSaveV12toV13` (pure `{...save, version:13}` bump — template at
  L77–79); extend the `readSave` chain with `if (migrated.version === 12) migrated =
  migrateSaveV12toV13(migrated)` and change the final guard `!== 12` → `!== 13`.
- `GameEngine.serialize`: bump `version: 12` → `13`; add `stats: serializeStats(state.stats)`,
  `advancements: [...state.advancements]`. Constructor: restore both after `restoreXp` (so
  they survive reload; `clearEffects` never touches them → survive death).
- `SAVE_KEY` unchanged (legacy `minecraft_save_v7`; live saves are per-world
  `minecraft_world_save_<id>`); **`WORLDGEN_VERSION` stays 8**.

### UI — dedicated `L`-key overlay

- Input: `KeyL` handler in `inputController.ts` (mirror `KeyI` L85–90 — dispatch
  `toggleAdvancements`, `keys.clear()`, `exitPointerLock`). `KeyL` is currently unbound.
- `components/game/AdvancementsPanel.tsx` — reads `snapshot.api.advancementState()` on render.
  Two tabs (PauseMenu pattern): **Advancements** grid grouped by `category` with locked/unlocked
  styling (mirror the recipe-book grid + `PixelImg`/`itemIconUrl`), **Statistics** list
  (`STATS` labels → values). Re-renders on every snapshot change and on unlock.
- `components/MinecraftGame.tsx` — render the panel + share the `.menu-backdrop` when
  `snapshot.advancementsOpen` (mirror the `inventoryOpen` block L159–189).
- Toast: `useMinecraftGame.ts` drain loop (L293–330) += `if (event.type ===
  "advancementUnlocked") flashMessage(\`Advancement Unlocked: ${event.name}\`)`.
- Audio: `audioDirector.ts` `handleEvent` switch += `case "advancementUnlocked"` — a
  celebratory ZZFX chime (new synthesized sound, in keeping with zero binary assets; reuse
  `XP_SOUND` if a dedicated chime proves fiddly).
- CSS in `app/hud.css` mirroring `.pause-overlay`/`.pause-tabs`/`.recipe-*` (own class prefix,
  e.g. `.adv-*`).

## Files

- **New**: `lib/game/engine/systems/advancements.ts` (+ `.test.ts`);
  `components/game/AdvancementsPanel.tsx` (+ `.test.tsx`).
- **Engine**: `lib/game/engine/state.ts`, `lib/game/engine/commands.ts`,
  `lib/game/engine/GameEngine.ts`, `lib/game/mobs.ts` (`HOSTILE_MOB_KINDS`).
- **Save**: `lib/game/types.ts`, `lib/game/save.ts`.
- **Input/UI/Audio**: `lib/game/input/inputController.ts`, `components/MinecraftGame.tsx`,
  `lib/game/useMinecraftGame.ts`, `app/hud.css`, `lib/game/audio/audioDirector.ts`.
- **Reuse**: `itemIconUrl` (`lib/ui/sprites.ts`), `PixelImg`/`ItemIcon`, recipe-book grid +
  PauseMenu tab patterns, `RECIPES` (recipe→station lookup).

## Commit slices (branch `feat/advancements`)

1. `feat(stats): track gameplay statistics` — `stats` Map + `recordEvent` + `STATS` +
   `HOSTILE_MOB_KINDS` + step tick-stats + the `crafted` event/emit + save v13 persistence of
   `stats`; engine + save tests.
2. `feat(advancements): registry + unlock tracking` — `ADVANCEMENTS`, the emit observer,
   `advancementUnlocked` event, `advancements` Set persistence; tests.
3. `feat(ui): advancements & statistics overlay (L key)` — flag/command/`KeyL`, `GameApi`
   accessor, `AdvancementsPanel` + tabs + CSS, toast + chime; component + e2e tests.
4. `docs` — reference (advancements + stats tables), manual (open with `L`; what unlocks),
   save-format (v13), adding-content (new "An advancement / a stat" extension section),
   testing.md coverage map, `CHANGELOG.md` `[Unreleased]`.

Each commit keeps `bun run lint` green so the branch stays bisectable; Conventional Commit
messages with a body explaining the why; **no `Co-Authored-By` trailers**.

## Verification

- Full CI `verify` gate green: `bun run lint`, `bun run typecheck`, `bun run format:check`,
  `bun test`, `bun run build` — plus `bun run test:e2e` (renderer/shell touched).
- **`advancements.test.ts`**: `recordEvent` bumps the right counter per event (incl. per-ore,
  hostile-only kill via `HOSTILE_MOB_KINDS`, `crafted`→`items_crafted`/`trades`);
  `evaluateAdvancements` unlocks exactly at the threshold, never twice, order-independent.
- **`GameEngine.test.ts`**: a headless run that mines an ore / kills a hostile / enchants /
  drinks a potion / defeats the boss emits `advancementUnlocked` once each and stats reflect
  it; a full **save round-trip preserves stats + advancements**, and they **survive a death**
  (assert not cleared, unlike effects).
- **`save.test.ts`**: `migrateSaveV12toV13` pure bump; `serializeStats`/`restoreStats`/
  `restoreAdvancements` round-trip + drop garbage; the v1→…→v13 chain. Update the e2e
  save-version assertion 12 → 13.
- **`AdvancementsPanel.test.tsx`**: renders locked vs unlocked entries, switches tabs, shows
  stat values. **e2e**: press `L` → overlay opens (pointer lock released) → close.
- **Manual pass** (not headless-verifiable): overlay layout/icons render, unlock toast + chime.

4	 * auditable entry point into the simulation.

12	 * chest (local index = value - base); below it they address the player
13	 * inventory. This lets one move command span both grids.
14	 */

99	/** A persisted inventory/container slot: just enough to rebuild it on load. */
100	export type SavedSlot = { id: string | null; count: number; durability?: number; enchantments?: Enchantment[]; customName?: string };
101	
102	/** Legacy persisted armor (v1–v11): a by-id reference into the inventory. */
103	export type SavedArmorById = Partial<Record<ArmorSlot, string | null>>;

125	/**
126	 * v3 save shape: v2 plus persisted time-of-day, player stats, and the bed
127	 * respawn point. All added fields are optional so the v2→v3 migration is a pure
128	 * version bump and pre-v3 saves load with sensible defaults.
129	 */
130	export type SaveDataV3 = Omit<SaveDataV2, "version"> & {
131	  version: 3;
132	  dayClock?: number;
133	  hearts?: number;
134	  hunger?: number;
135	  spawnPoint?: { x: number; y: number; z: number } | null;
136	};
137	
138	/**
139	 * v4 save shape: v3 plus chest contents as block-entities. The field is
140	 * optional so the v3→v4 migration is a pure version bump and pre-v4 saves load
141	 * with no containers.
142	 */
143	export type SaveDataV4 = Omit<SaveDataV3, "version"> & {
144	  version: 4;
145	  blockEntities?: SavedContainer[];
146	};
147	
148	/**
149	 * v5 save shape: v4 plus the set of dungeon loot-chest voxel indices the player
150	 * has already opened or broken. Dungeon chests are filled lazily on first access
151	 * and this set — not the chest's emptiness — is what prevents a re-roll on reload
152	 * (an emptied chest is dropped from `blockEntities`). The field is optional so
153	 * the v4→v5 migration is a pure version bump.
154	 */
155	export type SaveDataV5 = Omit<SaveDataV4, "version"> & {
156	  version: 5;
157	  lootedChests?: number[];
158	  /** Generation preset; absent ⇒ "default" (pre-feature and legacy saves). Like `seed`, fixed for the world's life. */
159	  worldType?: WorldType;
160	};
161	
162	/** One persisted status effect: its id and the seconds remaining when saved. */
163	export type SavedEffect = { id: EffectId; remaining: number };
164	
165	/**
166	 * v6 save shape: v5 plus the active status effects (with their remaining
167	 * seconds). The field is optional so the v5→v6 migration is a pure version bump
168	 * and pre-effect saves load with none. Effects are cleared on death, so a
169	 * saved-then-reloaded world restores whatever was active at save.
170	 */
171	export type SaveDataV6 = Omit<SaveDataV5, "version"> & {
172	  version: 6;
173	  effects?: SavedEffect[];
174	};
175	
176	/**
177	 * v7 save shape: v6 plus banked XP (`xp`). Enchantments ride inside each
178	 * `SavedSlot` (additive). Both are optional, so the v6→v7 migration is a pure
179	 * version bump and pre-XP saves load with `xp` 0 and no enchantments. Unlike
180	 * effects, XP is a long-term currency and is NOT cleared on death.
181	 */
182	export type SaveDataV7 = Omit<SaveDataV6, "version"> & {
183	  version: 7;
184	  xp?: number;
185	};
186	
187	/**
188	 * v8 save shape: v7 plus the player's game mode (`gameMode`). The field is
189	 * optional, so the v7→v8 migration is a pure version bump and pre-mode saves
190	 * load as "survival". Unlike `worldType` (fixed for the world's life),
191	 * `gameMode` is switchable in-game, so the saved value is the *current* mode.
192	 */
193	export type SaveDataV8 = Omit<SaveDataV7, "version"> & {
194	  version: 8;
195	  gameMode?: GameMode;
196	};
197	
198	/**
199	 * v9 save shape: v8 plus the player's difficulty (`difficulty`). The field is
200	 * optional, so the v8→v9 migration is a pure version bump and pre-v9 saves load
201	 * as "normal". Like `gameMode`, difficulty is switchable in-game, so the saved
202	 * value is the *current* difficulty.
203	 */
204	export type SaveDataV9 = Omit<SaveDataV8, "version"> & {
205	  version: 9;
206	  difficulty?: Difficulty;
207	};
208	
209	/**
210	 * v10 save shape: v9 plus the Hardcore flag (`hardcore`, immutable for the
211	 * world's life) and the permadeath `gameOver` flag (set once a hardcore run has
212	 * ended). Both are optional, so the v9→v10 migration is a pure version bump and
213	 * pre-v10 saves load as a normal, non-hardcore world. A persisted `gameOver`
214	 * reloads straight into the spectator "dead world" state, never a playable one.
215	 */
216	export type SaveDataV10 = Omit<SaveDataV9, "version"> & {
217	  version: 10;
218	  hardcore?: boolean;
219	  gameOver?: boolean;
220	};
221	
222	/**
223	 * v11 save shape: v10 plus a per-item `customName` (the anvil rename), which rides
224	 * inside each `SavedSlot` (additive). The Mending enchantment rides the existing
225	 * per-slot `enchantments`, so it adds no new field. The v10→v11 migration is a
226	 * pure version bump and pre-v11 saves load with no custom names.
227	 */
228	export type SaveDataV11 = Omit<SaveDataV10, "version"> & {
229	  version: 11;
230	};
231	
232	/**
233	 * Current save shape (v12): armor moves to dedicated storage. `equippedArmor` is no
234	 * longer a by-id reference into the inventory (`SavedArmorById`) but the worn pieces
235	 * themselves (`SavedEquippedArmor`, per-slot `SavedSlot`). The v11→v12 migration
236	 * moves each legacy by-id equip out of `inventorySlots` into the armor record so a
237	 * worn piece no longer double-occupies an inventory slot.
238	 */
239	export type SaveData = Omit<SaveDataV11, "version" | "equippedArmor"> & {
240	  version: 12;
241	  equippedArmor?: SavedEquippedArmor;
242	};
243

1	import { CHEST_SLOTS, CUSTOM_NAME_MAX_LEN, HOTBAR_SLOTS, INVENTORY_SLOTS, MAX_HEARTS, MAX_HUNGER, MAX_STACK_SIZE } from "@/lib/game/config";
2	import { ENCHANTMENT_DEFS } from "@/lib/game/enchantments";
3	import { ARMOR_SLOTS, createEmptyArmorEquipment, createEmptySlot, createSlot, ITEM_DEF_BY_ID, maxStackSizeForItem } from "@/lib/game/items";
4	import type {
5	  EffectId,
6	  Enchantment,
7	  EnchantmentId,
8	  EquippedArmor,
9	  SaveData,
10	  SaveDataV1,
11	  SaveDataV2,
12	  SaveDataV3,
13	  SaveDataV4,
14	  SaveDataV5,
15	  SaveDataV6,
16	  SaveDataV7,
17	  SaveDataV8,
18	  SaveDataV9,
19	  SaveDataV10,
20	  SaveDataV11,
21	  SavedContainer,
22	  SavedEffect,
23	  SavedEquippedArmor,
24	  SavedSlot,
25	  InventorySlot
26	} from "@/lib/game/types";
27	import { isGameMode, type GameMode } from "@/lib/game/gameModes";
28	import { isDifficulty, type Difficulty } from "@/lib/game/difficulties";
29	
30	/**
31	 * Migrates a v1 save (40 slots, 10-slot hotbar) to v2 (36 slots, 9-slot
32	 * hotbar): non-empty slots are packed in order, stackable items merge into
33	 * earlier stacks, and anything that still overflows 36 slots is dropped.
34	 */
35	/** Coerces a persisted hotbar index to a finite integer within 0..HOTBAR_SLOTS-1. */
36	function normalizeSelectedSlot(value: number): number {
37	  const index = Number.isFinite(value) ? Math.floor(value) : 0;
38	  return Math.max(0, Math.min(HOTBAR_SLOTS - 1, index));
39	}
40	
41	export function migrateSaveV1toV2(save: SaveDataV1): SaveDataV2 {
42	  const migrated: SaveDataV2 = { ...save, version: 2, selectedSlot: normalizeSelectedSlot(save.selectedSlot) };
43	
44	  if (Array.isArray(save.inventorySlots)) {
45	    const packed: Array<{ id: string | null; count: number; durability?: number }> = [];
46	    for (const saved of save.inventorySlots) {
47	      if (!saved?.id || saved.count <= 0) continue;
48	      // Items without durability stack; merge them into an earlier stack first.
49	      const stackable = ITEM_DEF_BY_ID[saved.id] ? !ITEM_DEF_BY_ID[saved.id].maxDurability : false;
50	      let remaining = saved.count;
51	      if (stackable) {
52	        for (const slot of packed) {
53	          if (slot.id !== saved.id || slot.count >= MAX_STACK_SIZE) continue;
54	          const moved = Math.min(MAX_STACK_SIZE - slot.count, remaining);
55	          slot.count += moved;
56	          remaining -= moved;
57	          if (remaining === 0) break;
58	        }
59	      }
60	      while (remaining > 0 && packed.length < INVENTORY_SLOTS) {
61	        const moved = Math.min(maxStackSizeForItem(saved.id), remaining);
62	        packed.push({ id: saved.id, count: moved, durability: saved.durability });
63	        remaining -= moved;
64	      }
65	    }
66	    migrated.inventorySlots = packed;
67	  }
68	
69	  return migrated;
70	}
71	
72	/**
73	 * Migrates a v2 save to v3 — a pure version bump. The new persisted fields
74	 * (dayClock, hearts, hunger, spawnPoint) are optional, so an older save simply
75	 * loads with the engine's defaults for them.
76	 */
77	export function migrateSaveV2toV3(save: SaveDataV2): SaveDataV3 {
78	  return { ...save, version: 3 };
79	}
80	
81	/**
82	 * Migrates a v3 save to v4 — a pure version bump. `blockEntities` (chest
83	 * contents) is optional, so a pre-chest save simply loads with no containers.
84	 */
85	export function migrateSaveV3toV4(save: SaveDataV3): SaveDataV4 {
86	  return { ...save, version: 4 };
87	}
88	
89	/**
90	 * Migrates a v4 save to v5 — a pure version bump. `lootedChests` is optional,
91	 * so a pre-dungeon save simply loads with no dungeon chests yet looted. (In
92	 * practice the SAVE_KEY bump to v6 discards pre-dungeon saves, but the
93	 * migration keeps the version chain complete and the readers total.)
94	 */
95	export function migrateSaveV4toV5(save: SaveDataV4): SaveDataV5 {
96	  return { ...save, version: 5 };
97	}
98	
99	/**
100	 * Migrates a v5 save to v6 — a pure version bump. `effects` (active status
101	 * effects) is optional, so a pre-effect save simply loads with none active.
102	 */
103	export function migrateSaveV5toV6(save: SaveDataV5): SaveDataV6 {
104	  return { ...save, version: 6 };
105	}
106	
107	/**
108	 * Migrates a v6 save to v7 — a pure version bump. `xp` (and per-slot
109	 * `enchantments`) are optional, so a pre-XP save simply loads with `xp` 0 and no
110	 * enchantments.
111	 */
112	export function migrateSaveV6toV7(save: SaveDataV6): SaveDataV7 {
113	  return { ...save, version: 7 };
114	}
115	
116	/**
117	 * Migrates a v7 save to v8 — a pure version bump. `gameMode` is optional, so a
118	 * pre-mode save simply loads as "survival" (see restoreGameMode).
119	 */
120	export function migrateSaveV7toV8(save: SaveDataV7): SaveDataV8 {
121	  return { ...save, version: 8 };
122	}
123	
124	/**
125	 * Migrates a v8 save to v9 — a pure version bump. `difficulty` is optional, so a
126	 * pre-difficulty save simply loads as "normal" (see restoreDifficulty).
127	 */
128	export function migrateSaveV8toV9(save: SaveDataV8): SaveDataV9 {
129	  return { ...save, version: 9 };
130	}
131	
132	/**
133	 * Migrates a v9 save to v10 — a pure version bump. `hardcore`/`gameOver` are
134	 * optional, so a pre-Hardcore save simply loads as a normal, non-hardcore world
135	 * (see restoreHardcore/restoreGameOver).
136	 */
137	export function migrateSaveV9toV10(save: SaveDataV9): SaveDataV10 {
138	  return { ...save, version: 10 };
139	}
140	
141	/**
142	 * Migrates a v10 save to v11. v11 adds an optional per-item `customName` (the
143	 * anvil rename) inside each `SavedSlot`; the Mending enchantment rides the
144	 * existing per-slot `enchantments`. Both are additive, so this is a pure version
145	 * bump and pre-v11 saves load with no custom names.
146	 */
147	export function migrateSaveV10toV11(save: SaveDataV10): SaveDataV11 {
148	  return { ...save, version: 11 };
149	}
150	
151	/**
152	 * Migrates a v11 save to v12 — armor moves to dedicated storage. The legacy
153	 * `equippedArmor` was a by-id reference into `inventorySlots` (the worn item also
154	 * lived in the inventory). For each valid equip, move the matching inventory item
155	 * out into the armor record (preserving durability/enchantments/customName); drop
156	 * equips whose item isn't found or isn't valid armor for the slot. Pre-`inventorySlots`
157	 * (legacy counts-only) saves simply drop the equip map and keep items in inventory.
158	 */
159	export function migrateSaveV11toV12(save: SaveDataV11): SaveData {
160	  const legacy = save.equippedArmor;
161	  if (!legacy || !Array.isArray(save.inventorySlots)) {
162	    return { ...save, version: 12, equippedArmor: undefined };
163	  }
164	  const slots = save.inventorySlots.slice();
165	  const equipped: SavedEquippedArmor = {};
166	  for (const armorSlot of ARMOR_SLOTS) {
167	    const id = legacy[armorSlot];
168	    if (!id) continue;
169	    const def = ITEM_DEF_BY_ID[id];
170	    if (def?.kind !== "armor" || def.armorSlot !== armorSlot) continue; // drop invalid equips
171	    const idx = slots.findIndex((s) => s?.id === id && s.count > 0);
172	    if (idx < 0) continue; // item not in inventory → drop the equip
173	    equipped[armorSlot] = slots[idx]; // preserves durability/enchantments/customName
174	    slots[idx] = { id: null, count: 0 };
175	  }
176	  return { ...save, version: 12, inventorySlots: slots, equippedArmor: equipped };
177	}
178	
179	// Storage is injectable so save logic can be tested without a browser.
180	export function readSave(saveKey: string, storage: Storage = localStorage): SaveData | null {
181	  try {
182	    const raw = storage.getItem(saveKey);
183	    if (!raw) return null;
184	    const parsed = JSON.parse(raw) as
185	      | SaveData
186	      | SaveDataV11
187	      | SaveDataV10
188	      | SaveDataV9
189	      | SaveDataV8
190	      | SaveDataV7
191	      | SaveDataV6
192	      | SaveDataV5
193	      | SaveDataV4
194	      | SaveDataV3
195	      | SaveDataV2
196	      | SaveDataV1;
197	    if (!parsed || !Number.isFinite(parsed.seed) || !Array.isArray(parsed.changes)) return null;
198	    let migrated: SaveDataV2 | SaveDataV3 | SaveDataV4 | SaveDataV5 | SaveDataV6 | SaveDataV7 | SaveDataV8 | SaveDataV9 | SaveDataV10 | SaveDataV11 | SaveData =
199	      parsed.version === 1 ? migrateSaveV1toV2(parsed) : parsed;
200	    if (migrated.version === 2) migrated = migrateSaveV2toV3(migrated);
201	    if (migrated.version === 3) migrated = migrateSaveV3toV4(migrated);
202	    if (migrated.version === 4) migrated = migrateSaveV4toV5(migrated);
203	    if (migrated.version === 5) migrated = migrateSaveV5toV6(migrated);
204	    if (migrated.version === 6) migrated = migrateSaveV6toV7(migrated);
205	    if (migrated.version === 7) migrated = migrateSaveV7toV8(migrated);
206	    if (migrated.version === 8) migrated = migrateSaveV8toV9(migrated);
207	    if (migrated.version === 9) migrated = migrateSaveV9toV10(migrated);
208	    if (migrated.version === 10) migrated = migrateSaveV10toV11(migrated);
209	    if (migrated.version === 11) migrated = migrateSaveV11toV12(migrated);
210	    if (migrated.version !== 12) return null;
211	    return migrated;
212	  } catch {
213	    return null;
214	  }
215	}
216	
217	export function writeSave(saveKey: string, data: SaveData, storage: Storage = localStorage): void {
218	  storage.setItem(saveKey, JSON.stringify(data));
219	}
220	
221	export function inventorySlotsSnapshot(inventory: InventorySlot[]): SavedSlot[] {
222	  return inventory.map((slot) => ({
223	    id: slot.id,
224	    count: slot.count,
225	    durability: slot.durability,
226	    enchantments: slot.enchantments,
227	    customName: slot.customName
228	  }));
229	}
230	
231	/** Snapshots the worn armor pieces for persistence (sparse — empty slots are omitted). */
232	export function serializeEquippedArmor(equipped: EquippedArmor): SavedEquippedArmor {
233	  const out: SavedEquippedArmor = {};
234	  for (const armorSlot of ARMOR_SLOTS) {
235	    const piece = equipped[armorSlot];
236	    if (!piece?.id || piece.count <= 0) continue;
237	    out[armorSlot] = { id: piece.id, count: piece.count, durability: piece.durability, enchantments: piece.enchantments, customName: piece.customName };
238	  }
239	  return out;
240	}
241	
242	// Every known enchantment id, keyed so a new enchantment can't be forgotten here.
243	const VALID_ENCHANT_IDS: Record<EnchantmentId, true> = {
244	  sharpness: true,
245	  protection: true,
246	  efficiency: true,
247	  unbreaking: true,
248	  mending: true,
249	  power: true,
250	  punch: true,
251	  knockback: true,
252	  looting: true,
253	  fortune: true,
254	  feather_falling: true
255	};
256	
257	/** Validates persisted enchantments: known ids and integer levels clamped to 1..(that enchant's max); undefined when none survive. */
258	function restoreEnchantments(saved: SavedSlot["enchantments"]): Enchantment[] | undefined {
259	  if (!Array.isArray(saved)) return undefined;
260	  const out: Enchantment[] = [];
261	  for (const entry of saved) {
262	    if (!entry || typeof entry.id !== "string" || !Object.hasOwn(VALID_ENCHANT_IDS, entry.id)) continue;
263	    if (!Number.isFinite(entry.level) || Math.floor(entry.level) < 1) continue;
264	    // Clamp to the enchantment's own cap so e.g. a tampered mending:3 loads as 1.
265	    out.push({ id: entry.id, level: Math.min(ENCHANTMENT_DEFS[entry.id].maxLevel, Math.floor(entry.level)) });
266	  }
267	  return out.length > 0 ? out : undefined;
268	}
269	
270	/**
271	 * Rebuilds one slot from its saved snapshot: drops unknown ids, clamps the
272	 * count, and clamps/defaults durability for gear (a non-positive saved
273	 * durability means the piece broke and is dropped). Returns an empty slot for
274	 * anything invalid. Shared by inventory and container restore.
275	 */
276	function restoreSlot(saved: SavedSlot | undefined): InventorySlot {
277	  if (!saved?.id || saved.count <= 0 || !ITEM_DEF_BY_ID[saved.id]) return createEmptySlot();
278	  const slot = createSlot(saved.id, Math.min(maxStackSizeForItem(saved.id), Math.max(0, Math.floor(saved.count))));
279	  if ((slot.kind === "tool" || slot.kind === "weapon" || slot.kind === "armor") && slot.maxDurability) {
280	    if (typeof saved.durability === "number") {
281	      const loaded = Math.floor(saved.durability);
282	      if (loaded <= 0) return createEmptySlot();
283	      slot.durability = Math.max(1, Math.min(slot.maxDurability, loaded));
284	    } else {
285	      slot.durability = slot.maxDurability;
286	    }
287	    // Enchantments live only on durable gear; validated against the known set.
288	    const enchantments = restoreEnchantments(saved.enchantments);
289	    if (enchantments) slot.enchantments = enchantments;
290	    // A custom name (anvil rename) also lives only on durable gear; trim and cap it.
291	    if (typeof saved.customName === "string") {
292	      const name = saved.customName.trim().slice(0, CUSTOM_NAME_MAX_LEN);
293	      if (name) slot.customName = name;
294	    }
295	  }
296	  return slot;
297	}
298	
299	/** Snapshots non-empty chest containers for persistence (empty chests carry no data). */
300	export function serializeContainers(containers: Map<number, InventorySlot[]>): SavedContainer[] {
301	  const out: SavedContainer[] = [];
302	  for (const [index, slots] of containers) {
303	    if (!slots.some((slot) => slot.id && slot.count > 0)) continue;
304	    out.push({ index, slots: inventorySlotsSnapshot(slots) });
305	  }
306	  return out;
307	}
308	
309	/** Snapshots the set of opened/broken dungeon chest voxel indices for persistence. */
310	export function serializeLootedChests(looted: Set<number>): number[] {
311	  return [...looted];
312	}
313	
314	/** Reads the opened/broken dungeon chest indices from a save (finite numbers only). */
315	export function readLootedChests(save: SaveData): number[] {
316	  if (!Array.isArray(save.lootedChests)) return [];
317	  return save.lootedChests.filter((value) => Number.isFinite(value));
318	}
319	
320	// Every known status-effect id, as a Record keyed by EffectId so adding an
321	// effect without listing it here is a compile error (keeps the reader total).
322	const VALID_EFFECT_IDS: Record<EffectId, true> = {
323	  speed: true,
324	  strength: true,
325	  regeneration: true,
326	  fire_resistance: true,
327	  water_breathing: true,
328	  haste: true,
329	  resistance: true,
330	  jump_boost: true,
331	  poison: true
332	};
333	
334	/** Snapshots the active status effects (id + remaining seconds) for persistence. */
335	export function serializeEffects(effects: Map<EffectId, number>): SavedEffect[] {
336	  const out: SavedEffect[] = [];
337	  for (const [id, remaining] of effects) {
338	    if (remaining > 0) out.push({ id, remaining });
339	  }
340	  return out;
341	}
342	
343	/** Reads active effects from a save, dropping unknown ids and non-positive/garbage durations. */
344	export function restoreEffects(save: SaveData): SavedEffect[] {
345	  if (!Array.isArray(save.effects)) return [];
346	  const out: SavedEffect[] = [];
347	  for (const entry of save.effects) {
348	    if (!entry || typeof entry.id !== "string" || !Object.hasOwn(VALID_EFFECT_IDS, entry.id)) continue;
349	    if (!Number.isFinite(entry.remaining) || entry.remaining <= 0) continue;
350	    out.push({ id: entry.id, remaining: entry.remaining });
351	  }
352	  return out;
353	}
354	
355	/**
356	 * Reads chest containers from a save into validated, CHEST_SLOTS-length slot
357	 * arrays keyed by voxel index. Per-slot validation only — the engine still
358	 * confirms each index actually holds a Chest block before using it.
359	 */
360	export function readContainers(save: SaveData): Array<{ index: number; slots: InventorySlot[] }> {
361	  if (!Array.isArray(save.blockEntities)) return [];
362	  const out: Array<{ index: number; slots: InventorySlot[] }> = [];
363	  for (const entry of save.blockEntities) {
364	    if (!entry || !Number.isFinite(entry.index) || !Array.isArray(entry.slots)) continue;
365	    const slots = Array.from({ length: CHEST_SLOTS }, (_, i) => restoreSlot(entry.slots[i]));
366	    out.push({ index: entry.index, slots });
367	  }
368	  return out;
369	}
370	
371	/**
372	 * Rebuilds inventory slots from a save, dropping unknown items, clamping
373	 * counts and durability, and skipping broken gear. Supports both the current
374	 * inventorySlots shape and the legacy inventoryCounts shape. Returns null when
375	 * the save carries no inventory.
376	 */
377	export function restoreInventorySlots(save: SaveData): InventorySlot[] | null {
378	  if (Array.isArray(save.inventorySlots)) {
379	    const slots = Array.from({ length: INVENTORY_SLOTS }, () => createEmptySlot());
380	    for (let i = 0; i < Math.min(INVENTORY_SLOTS, save.inventorySlots.length); i += 1) {
381	      slots[i] = restoreSlot(save.inventorySlots[i]);
382	    }
383	    return slots;
384	  }
385	
386	  if (save.inventoryCounts) {
387	    const slots = Array.from({ length: INVENTORY_SLOTS }, () => createEmptySlot());
388	    let cursor = 0;
389	    for (const [id, raw] of Object.entries(save.inventoryCounts)) {
390	      if (!ITEM_DEF_BY_ID[id]) continue;
391	      let remaining = Math.max(0, Math.floor(raw));
392	      while (remaining > 0 && cursor < slots.length) {
393	        const add = Math.min(maxStackSizeForItem(id), remaining);
394	        slots[cursor] = createSlot(id, add);
395	        cursor += 1;
396	        remaining -= add;
397	      }
398	    }
399	    return slots;
400	  }
401	
402	  return null;
403	}
404	
405	/** Restores worn armor, rebuilding each piece through restoreSlot and dropping anything that isn't valid armor for its slot. */
406	export function restoreEquippedArmor(save: SaveData): EquippedArmor | null {
407	  if (!save.equippedArmor) return null;
408	  const next = createEmptyArmorEquipment();
409	  for (const armorSlot of ARMOR_SLOTS) {
410	    const saved = save.equippedArmor[armorSlot];
411	    if (!saved) continue;
412	    const slot = restoreSlot(saved);
413	    if (slot.kind !== "armor" || slot.armorSlot !== armorSlot || slot.count <= 0) continue;
414	    next[armorSlot] = slot;
415	  }
416	  return next;
417	}
418	
419	export function restoreSelectedSlot(save: SaveData): number | null {
420	  if (typeof save.selectedSlot !== "number") return null;
421	  return normalizeSelectedSlot(save.selectedSlot);
422	}
423	
424	/** Restores the day clock from a save (finite, non-negative); null if absent/invalid. */
425	export function restoreDayClock(save: SaveData): number | null {
426	  if (typeof save.dayClock !== "number" || !Number.isFinite(save.dayClock) || save.dayClock < 0) return null;
427	  return save.dayClock;
428	}
429	
430	/** Restores hearts clamped to 1..MAX_HEARTS; null if absent/invalid. */
431	export function restoreHearts(save: SaveData): number | null {
432	  if (typeof save.hearts !== "number" || !Number.isFinite(save.hearts)) return null;
433	  return Math.max(1, Math.min(MAX_HEARTS, Math.floor(save.hearts)));
434	}
435	
436	/** Restores hunger clamped to 0..MAX_HUNGER; null if absent/invalid. */
437	export function restoreHungerLevel(save: SaveData): number | null {
438	  if (typeof save.hunger !== "number" || !Number.isFinite(save.hunger)) return null;
439	  return Math.max(0, Math.min(MAX_HUNGER, Math.floor(save.hunger)));
440	}
441	
442	/** Restores banked XP (finite, ≥ 0); 0 when absent or invalid. */
443	export function restoreXp(save: SaveData): number {
444	  if (typeof save.xp !== "number" || !Number.isFinite(save.xp) || save.xp < 0) return 0;
445	  return Math.floor(save.xp);
446	}
447	
448	/** Restores the saved game mode; "survival" when absent or invalid (pre-v8 saves). */
449	export function restoreGameMode(save: SaveData): GameMode {
450	  return isGameMode(save.gameMode) ? save.gameMode : "survival";
451	}
452	
453	/** Restores the saved difficulty; "normal" when absent or invalid (pre-v9 saves). */
454	export function restoreDifficulty(save: SaveData): Difficulty {
455	  return isDifficulty(save.difficulty) ? save.difficulty : "normal";
456	}
457	
458	/** Restores the Hardcore flag; false when absent or non-boolean (pre-v10 saves). */
459	export function restoreHardcore(save: SaveData): boolean {
460	  return save.hardcore === true;
461	}
462	
463	/** Restores the permadeath game-over flag — only ever true on a hardcore save, so a stray flag on a non-hardcore (corrupt) save can't lock it into spectator. */
464	export function restoreGameOver(save: SaveData): boolean {
465	  return save.hardcore === true && save.gameOver === true;
466	}
467	
468	/** Restores the bed respawn point; null if absent or explicitly cleared. */
469	export function restoreSpawnPoint(save: SaveData): { x: number; y: number; z: number } | null {
470	  const sp = save.spawnPoint;
471	  if (!sp || !Number.isFinite(sp.x) || !Number.isFinite(sp.y) || !Number.isFinite(sp.z)) return null;
472	  return { x: Math.floor(sp.x), y: Math.floor(sp.y), z: Math.floor(sp.z) };
473	}
474	
475	/**
476	 * Restores the player's position; null when absent or non-finite. A corrupt save
477	 * with NaN/Infinity coords would otherwise load a broken world — and slip past the
478	 * `position.y < 2` unstuck net, since any comparison with NaN is false. Coords stay
479	 * floats (the player isn't grid-aligned), unlike the floored spawn point.
480	 */
481	export function restorePlayerPosition(save: SaveData): { x: number; y: number; z: number } | null {
482	  const p = save.player;
483	  if (!p || !Number.isFinite(p.x) || !Number.isFinite(p.y) || !Number.isFinite(p.z)) return null;
484	  return { x: p.x, y: p.y, z: p.z };
485	}
