101	
102	export type GameEngineOptions = {
103	  /** A parsed save to restore, or null for a fresh world. */
104	  save?: SaveData | null;
105	  /** Seed for a fresh world; ignored when a save is provided. */
106	  seed?: number;
107	  /** Generation preset; the save's own worldType wins when restoring. Defaults to "default". */
108	  worldType?: WorldType;
109	  /** Initial game mode for a fresh world; the save's own gameMode wins when restoring. Defaults to "survival". */
110	  gameMode?: GameMode;
111	  /** Initial difficulty for a fresh world; the save's own difficulty wins when restoring. Defaults to "normal". */
112	  difficulty?: Difficulty;
113	  /** Hardcore flag for a fresh world; the save's own wins when restoring. Forces Survival + Hard + permadeath. Defaults to false. */
114	  hardcore?: boolean;
115	  /** Randomness source for mob spawning/AI — injectable for deterministic tests. */
116	  rng?: () => number;
117	  /** World dimensions override for fast headless tests. */
118	  worldSize?: { x: number; y: number; z: number };
119	};
120	
121	/**
122	 * The framework-agnostic game core. Owns all simulation state, advances it in
123	 * step(), and accepts player intents through dispatch(). No React, no DOM, no
124	 * rendering — the renderer reads state, the React shell subscribes to
125	 * snapshots via subscribe()/getSnapshot() (useSyncExternalStore compatible).
126	 */
127	export class GameEngine {
128	  readonly state: GameState;
129	  private readonly rng: () => number;
130	  private readonly worldType: WorldType;
131	  private readonly surfaceYAt: SurfaceYAtFn;
132	  private readonly listeners = new Set<() => void>();
133	  private events: GameEvent[] = [];
134	  private snapshot: GameSnapshot;
135	  // Navigation values are rounded, so the ref-equality snapshot diff updates
136	  // the HUD responsively without forcing a React render for sub-block movement.
137	  private lastBoss: ({ hpPercent: number } & BossTracking) | null = null;
138	  // The effects HUD projection is rebuilt only when its content (ids / rounded
139	  // seconds) changes, so the snapshot ref stays stable frame-to-frame and the
140	  // countdown re-renders at most ~once per second instead of every frame.
141	  private lastEffectsKey = "";
142	  private lastEffects: GameSnapshot["activeEffects"] = [];
143	
144	  constructor(options: GameEngineOptions = {}) {
145	    const save = options.save ?? null;
146	    this.rng = options.rng ?? Math.random;
147	
148	    const seed = save?.seed ?? options.seed ?? Math.floor(Math.random() * 2147483647);
149	    // A restored save's own type wins (the block-diffs were recorded against it);
150	    // a fresh world takes the requested type, defaulting to "default".
151	    this.worldType = save?.worldType ?? options.worldType ?? "default";
152	    const size = options.worldSize ?? { x: WORLD_SIZE_X, y: WORLD_SIZE_Y, z: WORLD_SIZE_Z };
153	    const world = new VoxelWorld(size.x, size.y, size.z, seed);
154	    generateWorld(world, this.worldType);
155	    // Re-derive the dungeon chest/spawner positions from the seed (the world is
156	    // regenerated deterministically each load, so these match generation).
157	    const dungeonSites = collectDungeonSites(world, this.worldType);
158	
159	    const blockChanges = createBlockChangeTracker(world);
160	    if (save) blockChanges.applySavedChanges(save.changes);
161	
162	    // Bake per-voxel light now the block grid is final (worldgen + saved edits).
163	    // Derived cache, never serialized — see lighting.ts / docs/save-format.md.
164	    world.light = computeFullLight(world);
165	
166	    this.surfaceYAt = createSurfaceYAt(world);
167	
168	    const firstSpawn = findSpawnOnLand(world, Math.floor(world.sizeX / 2), Math.floor(world.sizeZ / 2));
169	    // Hardcore is resolved first because it OVERRIDES mode/difficulty: a hardcore
170	    // world is locked to Survival + Hard. A persisted gameOver (the run already
171	    // ended in death) boots straight into Spectator so the dead world is roamable,
172	    // not playable. isFlying = (gameMode === "spectator") then yields a free camera.
173	    const hardcore = save ? restoreHardcore(save) : (options.hardcore ?? false);
174	    const gameOver = save ? restoreGameOver(save) : false;
175	    // A restored save's own (possibly switched) difficulty wins; hardcore forces Hard.
176	    const difficulty = hardcore ? "hard" : save ? restoreDifficulty(save) : (options.difficulty ?? "normal");
177	    // A restored save's own (possibly switched) mode wins; hardcore forces Survival,
178	    // except after game-over, where the player spectates their dead world.
179	    const gameMode = gameOver ? "spectator" : hardcore ? "survival" : save ? restoreGameMode(save) : (options.gameMode ?? "survival");
180	    this.state = {
181	      world,
182	      blockChanges,
183	      player: {
184	        position: new THREE.Vector3(firstSpawn.x, firstSpawn.y, firstSpawn.z),
185	        velocity: new THREE.Vector3(),
186	        yaw: 0,
187	        pitch: 0,
188	        onGround: false
189	      },
190	      inventory: createInitialInventory(),
191	      equippedArmor: createEmptyArmorEquipment(),
192	      selectedSlot: 0,
193	      gameMode,
194	      difficulty,
195	      hardcore,
196	      gameOver,
197	      isFlying: gameMode === "spectator", // Spectator is always airborne
198	      hearts: MAX_HEARTS,
199	      hunger: MAX_HUNGER,
200	      oxygen: MAX_OXYGEN,
201	      effects: new Map(),
202	      xp: 0,
203	      isDead: false,
204	      respawnTimer: 0,
205	      inventoryOpen: false,
206	      craftingStation: null,
207	      containers: new Map(),
208	      primedTnt: new Map(),
209	      openContainerIndex: null,
210	      dungeonChestIndices: new Set(dungeonSites.chestIndices),
211	      dungeonSpawnerIndices: new Set(dungeonSites.spawnerIndices),
212	      lootedDungeonChests: new Set(),
213	      paused: false,
214	      debugOpen: false,
215	      debugInfo: null,
216	      cameraMode: "first",
217	      capsActive: false,
218	      mobs: [],
219	      nextMobId: 1,
220	      thrownSpears: [],
221	      nextThrownSpearId: 1,
222	      projectiles: [],
223	      nextProjectileId: 1,
224	      fishing: null,
225	      dayClock: 0,
226	      daylight: daylightAt(0),
227	      daylightPercent: Math.round(daylightAt(0) * 100),
228	      weather: { kind: "clear", intensity: 0 },
229	      sleepTimer: 0,
230	      spawnPoint: null,
231	      mining: { targetKey: "", progress: 0 },
232	      timers: createTimers(),
233	      worldMeshDirty: true,
234	      victory: false
235	    };
236	
237	    if (save) {
238	      this.state.inventory = restoreInventorySlots(save) ?? this.state.inventory;
239	      this.state.equippedArmor = restoreEquippedArmor(save) ?? this.state.equippedArmor;
240	      this.state.selectedSlot = restoreSelectedSlot(save) ?? this.state.selectedSlot;
241	      this.state.hearts = restoreHearts(save) ?? this.state.hearts;
242	      this.state.hunger = restoreHungerLevel(save) ?? this.state.hunger;
243	      this.state.spawnPoint = restoreSpawnPoint(save);
244	      this.state.lootedDungeonChests = new Set(readLootedChests(save));
245	      // Restore any active effects (cleared on death, so a live save carries them).
246	      for (const { id, remaining } of restoreEffects(save)) this.state.effects.set(id, remaining);
247	      this.state.xp = restoreXp(save); // XP is a long-term currency — kept across reload and death
248	
249	      // Restore chest contents only for indices that still hold a Chest block.
250	      for (const { index, slots } of readContainers(save)) {
251	        if (index >= 0 && index < world.blocks.length && world.blocks[index] === BlockId.Chest) {
252	          this.state.containers.set(index, slots);
253	        }
254	      }
255	      const savedClock = restoreDayClock(save);
256	      if (savedClock !== null) {
257	        this.state.dayClock = savedClock;
258	        this.state.daylight = daylightAt(savedClock);
259	        this.state.daylightPercent = Math.round(this.state.daylight * 100);
260	      }
261	      const pos = restorePlayerPosition(save);
262	      if (pos) this.state.player.position.set(pos.x, pos.y, pos.z);
263	    }
264	
265	    // Safety check: if stuck after load, relocate to a plain — but never for a
266	    // Spectator, who legitimately loads inside terrain (or low) while noclipping.
267	    if (
268	      !isNoclip(this.state.gameMode) &&
269	      (collidesAt(world, this.state.player.position, PLAYER_HALF_WIDTH, PLAYER_HEIGHT) || this.state.player.position.y < 2)
270	    ) {
271	      this.forceUnstuck();
272	    }
273	
274	    spawnInitialMobs(this.state, this.rng, this.surfaceYAt);
275	    // Seed weather from the (possibly restored) dayClock + player position so a
276	    // loaded save's first frame/snapshot is consistent before the first step().
277	    tickWeather(this.state);
278	    this.snapshot = this.buildSnapshot();
279	  }
280	
281	  /** Advances the simulation by dt seconds. The renderer draws the state afterwards. */
282	  step(dt: number, input: FrameInput): void {
283	    const state = this.state;
284	    if (state.paused) {
285	      // Full freeze: mobs, the day clock, mining, and stats all stop.
286	      this.refreshSnapshot();
287	      return;
288	    }
289	    state.capsActive = input.capsActive;
290	
291	    // Stuck detection / auto-unstuck — skipped for Spectator, which legitimately
292	    // sits inside terrain while noclipping and must never be teleported out.
293	    if (!isNoclip(state.gameMode)) {
294	      const inBadState = collidesAt(state.world, state.player.position, PLAYER_HALF_WIDTH, PLAYER_HEIGHT) || state.player.position.y < 2;
295	      state.timers.stuckTimer = inBadState ? state.timers.stuckTimer + dt : 0;
296	      if (state.timers.stuckTimer > STUCK_RESET_SECONDS) {
297	        this.forceUnstuck();
298	        state.timers.stuckTimer = 0;
299	      }
300	    }
301	
302	    // Death: only mobs and the respawn countdown tick while dead.
303	    if (state.isDead) {
304	      if (tickRespawnTimer(state, dt)) this.respawn();
305	      else {
306	        tickMobs(state, dt, this.mobTickDeps);
307	        // Keep ticking so lit fuses and in-flight arrows resolve instead of
308	        // freezing for the respawn countdown; applyDamage no-ops while dead.
309	        tickPrimedTnt(state, dt, this.mobTickDeps);
310	        tickProjectiles(state, dt, this.mobTickDeps);
311	      }
312	      this.refreshSnapshot();
313	      return;
314	    }
315	
316	    // Sleeping: a full freeze during the fade, then a jump to the next morning.
317	    if (state.sleepTimer > 0) {
318	      state.sleepTimer = Math.max(0, state.sleepTimer - dt);
319	      if (state.sleepTimer === 0) this.wakeToMorning();
320	      this.refreshSnapshot();
321	      return;
322	    }
323	
324	    const move = tickPlayerMotion(state, input, dt, this.applyDamage);
325	    if (move.didJump) this.emit({ type: "jumped" });
326	    if (move.didLand) this.emit({ type: "landed", impact: move.landImpact });
327	    tickHungerDrain(state, move);
328	    tickHealthRegen(state, dt);
329	    // Starvation reads the freshly-drained hunger: Easy/Normal chip to a floor,
330	    // Hard (floor 0) can kill via the environmental-damage path.
331	    tickStarvation(state, dt, this.applyStarvationFloored, this.applyEnvironmentalDamage);
332	    // Status effects tick here so the fire-resist / water-breathing gates below are current.
333	    tickStatusEffects(state, dt, { applyPoisonDamage: this.applyPoisonDamage, emit: this.emit });
334	    tickWaterExposure(state, dt, this.applyEnvironmentalDamage);
335	    tickLavaExposure(state, dt, this.applyEnvironmentalDamage, hasEffect(state, "fire_resistance"));
336	    tickOxygen(state, dt, this.applyEnvironmentalDamage, hasEffect(state, "water_breathing"));
337	    state.timers.bowCooldownTimer = Math.max(0, state.timers.bowCooldownTimer - dt);
338	    tickMining(state, input, dt, this.emit, this.rng);
339	    tickThrownSpears(state, dt, this.removeMobAt, this.emit);
340	    tickFishing(state, dt, this.rng, this.emit);
341	    tickDayNight(state, dt);
342	    tickWeather(state);
343	    tickRandomBlocks(state, dt, this.rng);
344	    tickHostileSpawnDirector(state, dt, this.rng, this.surfaceYAt);
345	    tickSpawnerDirector(state, dt, this.rng, this.emit);
346	    tickMobs(state, dt, this.mobTickDeps);
347	    tickPrimedTnt(state, dt, this.mobTickDeps);
348	    tickProjectiles(state, dt, this.mobTickDeps);
349	    tickBreeding(state, dt, this.rng, this.surfaceYAt, this.emit);
350	    this.tickDebugInfo(dt);
351	
352	    this.refreshSnapshot();
353	  }
354	
355	  /** Applies a discrete player intent. */
356	  dispatch(command: Command): void {
357	    const state = this.state;
358	    switch (command.type) {
359	      case "selectSlot": {
360	        if (command.index >= 0 && command.index < Math.min(HOTBAR_SLOTS, state.inventory.length)) {
361	          state.selectedSlot = command.index;
362	        }
363	        break;
364	      }
365	      case "toggleInventory": {
366	        if (!canInteract(state.gameMode)) break; // Spectator has no inventory
367	        state.inventoryOpen = !state.inventoryOpen;
368	        if (!state.inventoryOpen) {
369	          state.craftingStation = null; // leaving the panel closes the station
370	          state.openContainerIndex = null; // ...and the open chest
371	        }
372	        break;
373	      }
374	      case "craft": {
375	        const recipe = RECIPES.find((entry) => entry.id === command.recipeId);
376	        if (!recipe || state.isDead || !canInteract(state.gameMode)) break;
377	        // Station recipes (e.g. furnace smelting) require that station to be open.
378	        // The UI gates these too, but dispatch is the spoofable surface to guard.
379	        if (recipe.station && recipe.station !== state.craftingStation) break;
380	        const next = inv.craft(state.inventory, recipe);
381	        if (!next) break;
382	        state.inventory = next;
383	        if (recipe.station) this.emit({ type: "smelted" });
384	        break;
385	      }
386	      case "swapSlots": {
387	        if (!canInteract(state.gameMode)) break;
388	        state.inventory = inv.swapSlots(state.inventory, command.from, command.to) ?? state.inventory;
389	        break;
390	      }
391	      case "moveStack": {
392	        if (!canInteract(state.gameMode)) break;
393	        this.applyMoveStack(command.from, command.to);
394	        break;
395	      }
396	      case "toggleEquipArmor": {
397	        if (!canInteract(state.gameMode)) break;
398	        const equip = inv.toggleEquipArmor(state.inventory, state.equippedArmor, command.index);
399	        if (equip) {
400	          state.inventory = equip.slots;
401	          state.equippedArmor = equip.equipped;
402	        }
403	        break;
404	      }
405	      case "unequipArmor": {
406	        if (!canInteract(state.gameMode)) break;
407	        const unequip = inv.unequipArmor(state.inventory, state.equippedArmor, command.slot);
408	        if (unequip) {
409	          state.inventory = unequip.slots;
410	          state.equippedArmor = unequip.equipped;
411	        }
412	        break;
413	      }
414	      case "eatFood": {
415	        if (state.isDead || state.inventoryOpen || state.sleepTimer > 0 || !canInteract(state.gameMode)) break;
416	        const slot = state.inventory[state.selectedSlot];
417	        if (!slot?.id || slot.kind !== "food" || !slot.hunger || slot.count <= 0) break;
418	        const next = inv.adjustSlotCount(state.inventory, slot.id, -1, state.selectedSlot);
419	        if (!next) break;
420	        state.inventory = next;
421	        state.hunger = restoreHunger(state.hunger, slot.hunger);
422	        // Rotten flesh sometimes poisons — a never-lethal nibble of risk on the
423	        // most desperate food. Uses the injected rng so the roll is deterministic.
424	        if (slot.id === "rotten_flesh" && this.rng() < ROTTEN_FLESH_POISON_CHANCE) {
425	          addEffect(state, "poison", POISON_DURATION);
426	        }
427	        this.emit({ type: "ateFood" });
428	        break;
429	      }
430	      case "drinkPotion": {
431	        if (state.isDead || state.inventoryOpen || state.sleepTimer > 0 || !canInteract(state.gameMode)) break;
432	        const slot = state.inventory[state.selectedSlot];
433	        if (!slot?.id || !slot.effect || slot.count <= 0) break;
434	        const next = inv.adjustSlotCount(state.inventory, slot.id, -1, state.selectedSlot);
435	        if (!next) break;
436	        state.inventory = next;
437	        addEffect(state, slot.effect.id, slot.effect.durationSeconds);
438	        this.emit({ type: "drankPotion" });
439	        break;
440	      }
441	      case "enchant": {
442	        // Only at an open enchanting table; applies to the selected item instance.
443	        if (state.isDead || !canInteract(state.gameMode) || state.craftingStation !== "enchanting") break;
444	        const slot = state.inventory[state.selectedSlot];
445	        if (!canEnchant(slot, command.enchant)) break;
446	        if (!spendXpLevels(state, ENCHANT_COST_LEVELS)) break; // too few levels
447	        const next = [...state.inventory];
448	        next[state.selectedSlot] = applyEnchant(slot, command.enchant);
449	        state.inventory = next;
450	        this.emit({ type: "enchanted", enchant: command.enchant });
451	        break;
452	      }
453	      case "anvilCombine": {
454	        // Combine a duplicate of the selected gear into it: repair + merge enchants.
455	        if (state.isDead || !canInteract(state.gameMode) || state.craftingStation !== "anvil") break;
456	        const i = state.selectedSlot;
457	        const target = state.inventory[i];
458	        if (!isAnvilGear(target)) break;
459	        const sacrifice = findSacrificeIndex(state.inventory, i);
460	        if (sacrifice < 0 || !wouldCombineHelp(target, state.inventory[sacrifice])) break;
461	        if (!spendXpLevels(state, ANVIL_COMBINE_COST_LEVELS)) break;
462	        const next = [...state.inventory];
463	        next[i] = combineSlots(target, state.inventory[sacrifice]);
464	        next[sacrifice] = createEmptySlot();
465	        state.inventory = next;
466	        this.emit({ type: "anvilCombined" });
467	        break;
468	      }
469	      case "anvilRepair": {
470	        // Repair the selected gear by consuming one unit of its tier material.
471	        if (state.isDead || !canInteract(state.gameMode) || state.craftingStation !== "anvil") break;
472	        const i = state.selectedSlot;
473	        const target = state.inventory[i];
474	        if (!canMaterialRepair(target, state.inventory)) break;
475	        const material = repairMaterialFor(target)!;
476	        if (!spendXpLevels(state, ANVIL_REPAIR_COST_LEVELS)) break;
477	        const consumed = inv.adjustSlotCount(state.inventory, material, -1);
478	        if (!consumed) break; // belt-and-braces: canMaterialRepair already verified stock
479	        consumed[i] = materialRepair(target);
480	        state.inventory = consumed;
481	        this.emit({ type: "anvilRepaired" });
482	        break;
483	      }
484	      case "anvilRename": {
485	        // Rename the selected durable item (or clear the name with "").
486	        if (state.isDead || !canInteract(state.gameMode) || state.craftingStation !== "anvil") break;
487	        const slot = state.inventory[state.selectedSlot];
488	        if (!isAnvilGear(slot)) break;
489	        const name = sanitizeCustomName(command.name);
490	        if ((slot.customName ?? "") === name) break; // no-op: nothing to charge for
491	        if (!spendXpLevels(state, ANVIL_RENAME_COST_LEVELS)) break;
492	        const next = [...state.inventory];
493	        next[state.selectedSlot] = { ...slot, customName: name || undefined };
494	        state.inventory = next;
495	        this.emit({ type: "anvilRenamed" });
496	        break;
497	      }
498	      case "grindstoneStrip": {
499	        // Strip the selected gear's enchantments, refunding XP for them.
500	        if (state.isDead || !canInteract(state.gameMode) || state.craftingStation !== "grindstone") break;
501	        const slot = state.inventory[state.selectedSlot];
502	        if (!canStripEnchantments(slot)) break;
503	        state.xp += enchantRefund(slot);
504	        const next = [...state.inventory];
505	        next[state.selectedSlot] = stripEnchantments(slot);
506	        state.inventory = next;
507	        this.emit({ type: "grindstoneStripped" });
508	        break;
509	      }
510	      case "placeBlock": {
511	        if (state.isDead || state.inventoryOpen || state.sleepTimer > 0 || !canInteract(state.gameMode)) break;
512	        // Spears consume the right-click/E action before all world interaction.
513	        if (tryThrowSelectedSpear(state, this.emit, this.rng)) break;
514	        // Right-click precedence: feed an aimed animal, then interact with the
515	        // aimed block (bed, furnace), then use the held item (hoe, seeds); only
516	        // place a block if none of those consumed the click.
517	        if (tryFeedAimedMob(state, this.emit)) break;
518	        if (tryTradeAimedVillager(state, this.emit)) break;
519	        if (tryInteractBlock(state, this.emit)) break;
520	        if (this.trySummonBoss()) break;
521	        if (tryFish(state, this.emit, this.rng)) break;
522	        if (tryUseHeldItem(state, this.emit, this.rng)) break;
523	        placeSelectedBlock(state, this.emit);
524	        break;
525	      }
526	      case "attack": {
527	        if (state.isDead || state.inventoryOpen || state.sleepTimer > 0 || !canInteract(state.gameMode)) break;
528	        this.emit({ type: "attackSwung" });
529	        // A held bow fires arrows instead of meleeing; tryFireBow no-ops on
530	        // cooldown or with no arrows, but the swing animation still plays.
531	        if (isBow(state.inventory[state.selectedSlot])) {
532	          tryFireBow(state, this.emit, this.rng);
533	          break;
534	        }
535	        const heldWeapon = state.inventory[state.selectedSlot];
536	        const hitKind = tryAttackMob(
537	          state,
538	          weaponDamage(state) + strengthBonus(state) + sharpnessBonus(heldWeapon),
539	          this.removeMobAt,
540	          weaponReach(state),
541	          knockbackBonus(heldWeapon),
542	          lootingLevel(heldWeapon)
543	        );
544	        if (hitKind) {
545	          this.emit({ type: "mobHit", kind: hitKind });
546	          state.inventory = inv.consumeToolDurability(state.inventory, state.selectedSlot, 1, this.rng) ?? state.inventory;
547	          resetMining(state);
548	        }
549	        break;
550	      }
551	      case "toggleFlight": {
552	        // Creative only — Spectator is permanently airborne, survival can't fly.
553	        if (state.gameMode !== "creative" || state.isDead || state.inventoryOpen) break;
554	        state.isFlying = !state.isFlying;
555	        break;
556	      }
557	      case "creativeGiveItem": {
558	        // Pulls a full stack from the creative palette into the inventory
559	        // (lowest empty slot first, so it lands on the hotbar). Creative only.
560	        if (state.gameMode !== "creative" || !ITEM_DEF_BY_ID[command.itemId]) break;
561	        state.inventory = inv.adjustSlotCount(state.inventory, command.itemId, maxStackSizeForItem(command.itemId), state.selectedSlot) ?? state.inventory;
562	        break;
563	      }
564	      case "setGameMode": {
565	        this.switchGameMode(command.mode);
566	        break;
567	      }
568	      case "setDifficulty": {
569	        this.switchDifficulty(command.difficulty);
570	        break;
571	      }
572	      case "unstuck": {
573	        if (state.isDead) break;
574	        this.forceUnstuck();
575	        break;
576	      }
577	      case "pause": {
578	        // The inventory panel, death screen, and victory screen own their
579	        // lock-loss; only plain gameplay lock-loss (or an explicit Escape) opens
580	        // the pause menu. Sleeping is a brief, atomic freeze — pausing mid-fade
581	        // would stall the sleep timer (step early-returns on paused before sleep).
582	        if (state.inventoryOpen || state.isDead || state.sleepTimer > 0 || state.victory || state.gameOver) break;
583	        state.paused = true;
584	        state.craftingStation = null;
585	        state.openContainerIndex = null;
586	        break;
587	      }
588	      case "resume": {
589	        state.paused = false;
590	        break;
591	      }
592	      case "toggleDebug": {
593	        state.debugOpen = !state.debugOpen;
594	        state.debugInfo = state.debugOpen ? this.currentDebugInfo() : null;
595	        break;
596	      }
597	      case "toggleCameraView": {
598	        // Render-only, so it works even while dead or paused (like Minecraft F5).
599	        state.cameraMode = nextCameraMode(state.cameraMode);
600	        break;
601	      }
602	      case "respawn": {
603	        // Skip the rest of the countdown; the next step performs the respawn.
604	        if (state.isDead) state.respawnTimer = 0;
605	        break;
606	      }
607	      case "dismissVictory": {
608	        state.victory = false;
609	        break;
610	      }
611	    }
612	    this.refreshSnapshot();
613	  }
614	
615	  /** Mouse-look: applied directly by the input controller (radians). */
616	  applyLook(deltaYaw: number, deltaPitch: number): void {
617	    const player = this.state.player;
618	    player.yaw += deltaYaw;
619	    player.pitch = Math.max(-Math.PI / 2 + 0.01, Math.min(Math.PI / 2 - 0.01, player.pitch + deltaPitch));
620	  }
621	
622	  /** Current world + player state as a persistable save. */
623	  serialize(): SaveData {
624	    const state = this.state;
625	    return {
626	      version: 12,
627	      seed: state.world.seed,
628	      worldType: this.worldType,
629	      gameMode: state.gameMode,
630	      difficulty: state.difficulty,
631	      hardcore: state.hardcore,
632	      gameOver: state.gameOver,
633	      changes: state.blockChanges.changes(),
634	      inventorySlots: inventorySlotsSnapshot(state.inventory),
635	      equippedArmor: serializeEquippedArmor(state.equippedArmor),
636	      selectedSlot: state.selectedSlot,
637	      player: {
638	        x: state.player.position.x,
639	        y: state.player.position.y,
640	        z: state.player.position.z
641	      },
642	      dayClock: state.dayClock,
643	      hearts: state.hearts,
644	      hunger: state.hunger,
645	      spawnPoint: state.spawnPoint ? { ...state.spawnPoint } : null,
646	      blockEntities: serializeContainers(state.containers),
647	      lootedChests: serializeLootedChests(state.lootedDungeonChests),
648	      effects: serializeEffects(state.effects),
649	      xp: state.xp
650	    };
651	  }
652	
653	  subscribe = (listener: () => void): (() => void) => {
654	    this.listeners.add(listener);
655	    return () => this.listeners.delete(listener);
656	  };
657	
658	  getSnapshot = (): GameSnapshot => this.snapshot;
659	
660	  /** Drains queued one-shot gameplay events for the shell (death screen, audio). */
661	  consumeEvents(): GameEvent[] {
662	    if (this.events.length === 0) return this.events;
663	    const drained = this.events;
664	    this.events = [];
665	    return drained;
666	  }
667	
668	  private emit = (event: GameEvent): void => {
669	    this.events.push(event);
670	  };
671	
672	  /**
673	   * Moves a slot between the player inventory and the open chest. Indices at or
674	   * above CONTAINER_SLOT_BASE address the container; below it, the inventory.
675	   * Writes back whichever of the two arrays the move touched.
676	   */
677	  private applyMoveStack(from: number, to: number): void {
678	    const state = this.state;
679	    const container = state.openContainerIndex !== null ? state.containers.get(state.openContainerIndex) : undefined;
680	    const resolve = (index: number): { arr: InventorySlot[]; local: number } | null => {
681	      if (index >= CONTAINER_SLOT_BASE) return container ? { arr: container, local: index - CONTAINER_SLOT_BASE } : null;
682	      return { arr: state.inventory, local: index };
683	    };
684	    const a = resolve(from);
685	    const b = resolve(to);
686	    if (!a || !b) return;
687	    const moved = inv.moveStack(a.arr, a.local, b.arr, b.local);
688	    if (!moved) return;
689	
690	    // Resolve each side's destination BEFORE writing: the first write reassigns
691	    // state.inventory, which would make a later `arr === state.inventory` check
692	    // stale and misroute an inventory↔inventory move into the open chest.
693	    const aIsInventory = a.arr === state.inventory;
694	    const bIsInventory = b.arr === state.inventory;
695	    const writeBack = (isInventory: boolean, next: InventorySlot[]): void => {
696	      if (isInventory) state.inventory = next;
697	      else if (state.openContainerIndex !== null) state.containers.set(state.openContainerIndex, next);
698	    };
699	    writeBack(aIsInventory, moved.a);
700	    writeBack(bIsInventory, moved.b);
701	  }
702	
703	  private applyDamage = (amount: number): void => {
704	    const heartsBefore = this.state.hearts;
705	    const died = applyDamageWithArmor(this.state, amount, this.rng);
706	    if (died) {
707	      if (this.state.hardcore) return void this.triggerGameOver();
708	      clearEffects(this.state);
709	      resetMining(this.state);
710	      this.emit({ type: "died" });
711	    } else if (this.state.hearts < heartsBefore) {
712	      this.emit({ type: "playerHurt" });
713	    }
714	  };
715	
716	  private applyEnvironmentalDamage = (amount: number): void => {
717	    const heartsBefore = this.state.hearts;
718	    const died = applyUnmitigatedDamage(this.state, amount);
719	    if (died) {
720	      if (this.state.hardcore) return void this.triggerGameOver();
721	      clearEffects(this.state);
722	      resetMining(this.state);
723	      this.emit({ type: "died" });
724	    } else if (this.state.hearts < heartsBefore) {
725	      this.emit({ type: "playerHurt" });
726	    }
727	  };
728	
729	  /**
730	   * Hardcore permadeath. The run is over: instead of the respawn countdown the
731	   * player is dropped into a Spectator free-cam over their dead world and a
732	   * persisted gameOver flag fires the Game Over screen. We deliberately leave
733	   * isDead/respawnTimer cleared so the respawn DeathScreen never shows, and set
734	   * the mode DIRECTLY (not via the now-locked switchGameMode). The shell saves
735	   * on the {type:"gameOver"} event so closing the tab still resumes the dead world.
736	   */
737	  private triggerGameOver(): void {
738	    const state = this.state;
739	    state.gameOver = true;
740	    state.gameMode = "spectator"; // free-cam roam; takesDamage=false → fully inert
741	    state.isFlying = true;
742	    state.isDead = false;
743	    state.respawnTimer = 0;
744	    state.hearts = 0; // the run's final state (bars are hidden in spectator anyway)
745	    clearEffects(state);
746	    resetMining(state);
747	    state.inventoryOpen = false;
748	    state.craftingStation = null;
749	    state.openContainerIndex = null;
750	    state.paused = false;
751	    this.emit({ type: "gameOver" });
752	  }
753	
754	  /** Poison damage: armor-bypassing but never lethal (floors at half a heart). */
755	  private applyPoisonDamage = (amount: number): void => {
756	    if (applyNonLethalDamage(this.state, amount, POISON_FLOOR_HP)) this.emit({ type: "playerHurt" });
757	  };
758	
759	  /** Starvation chip on Easy/Normal: armor-bypassing, floored at the difficulty's HP (Hard kills via applyEnvironmentalDamage). */
760	  private applyStarvationFloored = (amount: number, floorHp: number): void => {
761	    if (applyNonLethalDamage(this.state, amount, floorHp)) this.emit({ type: "playerHurt" });
762	  };
763	
764	  private removeMobAt = (index: number, lootingLevel = 0): void => {
765	    const state = this.state;
766	    const mob = state.mobs[index];
767	    state.mobs.splice(index, 1);
768	    // Babies drop nothing — only grown animals yield loot. `lootingLevel` is the
769	    // *killing* melee weapon's Looting (forwarded by tryAttackMob); indirect kills
770	    // (arrows, thrown spears, explosions) pass 0, since Looting is a melee enchant.
771	    const drops = mob.ageTimer <= 0 ? rollMobDrops(mob.kind, this.rng, lootingLevel) : [];
772	    for (const drop of drops) {
773	      state.inventory = inv.adjustSlotCount(state.inventory, drop.itemId, drop.count) ?? state.inventory;
774	    }
775	    // XP on kill, baby-gated like drops; the boss pays a jackpot.
776	    if (mob.ageTimer <= 0) awardXp(state, xpForMob(mob.kind), this.emit);
777	    this.emit({ type: "mobDied", kind: mob.kind, x: mob.position.x, y: mob.position.y, z: mob.position.z });
778	    // Drops land straight in inventory (no ground item), so announce them.
779	    if (drops.length > 0) this.emit({ type: "pickedUp", items: drops });
780	    // Defeating the boss is the win condition — fire the one-shot victory.
781	    if (mob.kind === "boss") {
782	      state.victory = true;
783	      this.emit({ type: "bossDefeated", x: mob.position.x, y: mob.position.y, z: mob.position.z });
784	    }
785	  };
786	
787	  /**
788	   * Summons the boss from a held Cursed Totem at a seeded land point near the
789	   * player, consuming the totem. Refuses (keeping the totem) when a boss already
790	   * walks. Returns true when the click was consumed.
791	   */
792	  private trySummonBoss(): boolean {
793	    const state = this.state;
794	    const slot = state.inventory[state.selectedSlot];
795	    if (slot?.id !== "boss_summoner" || slot.count <= 0) return false;
796	    if (state.mobs.some((mob) => mob.kind === "boss")) {
797	      this.emit({ type: "summonFailed" });
798	      return true;
799	    }
800	    const point = randomLandPointNear(state.world, this.surfaceYAt, state.player.position.x, state.player.position.z, BOSS_SUMMON_RADIUS, this.rng);
801	    spawnBoss(state, point.x, point.y, point.z, this.rng);
802	    state.inventory = inv.adjustSlotCount(state.inventory, "boss_summoner", -1, state.selectedSlot) ?? state.inventory;
803	    this.emit({ type: "bossSummoned", x: point.x, y: point.y, z: point.z });
804	    return true;
805	  }
806	
807	  private get mobTickDeps() {
808	    return {
809	      surfaceYAt: this.surfaceYAt,
810	      applyDamage: this.applyDamage,
811	      removeMobAt: this.removeMobAt,
812	      rng: this.rng,
813	      emit: this.emit
814	    };
815	  }
816	
817	  /**
818	   * Switches the live game mode (from the pause menu), resetting transient state
819	   * so nothing leaks across: queued hazard damage is cleared, the bars refill
820	   * (hidden anyway in no-damage modes), flight matches the mode (Spectator
821	   * always airborne), open panels close, and leaving noclip lifts the player out
822	   * of any wall they were sitting in.
823	   */
824	  private switchGameMode(next: GameMode): void {
825	    const state = this.state;
826	    if (state.hardcore) return; // locked to Survival (Spectator after game-over); the death transition sets the mode directly
827	    if (!isGameMode(next) || next === state.gameMode || state.isDead) return;
828	    state.gameMode = next;
829	
830	    const t = state.timers;
831	    t.lavaBurnTimer = 0;
832	    t.lavaDamageTimer = 0;
833	    t.voidTimer = 0;
834	    t.waterExposureTimer = 0;
835	    t.waterDamageTimer = 0;
836	    t.drownTimer = 0;
837	    state.hearts = MAX_HEARTS;
838	    state.hunger = MAX_HUNGER;
839	    state.oxygen = MAX_OXYGEN;
840	
841	    state.isFlying = next === "spectator";
842	    resetMining(state);
843	    state.inventoryOpen = false;
844	    state.craftingStation = null;
845	    state.openContainerIndex = null;
846	
847	    if (!isNoclip(next) && (collidesAt(state.world, state.player.position, PLAYER_HALF_WIDTH, PLAYER_HEIGHT) || state.player.position.y < 2)) {
848	      this.forceUnstuck();
849	    }
850	  }
851	
852	  /**
853	   * Switches the live difficulty (from the pause menu). The spawn directors and
854	   * mob AI read state.difficulty every tick, so raising/lowering just changes the
855	   * numbers they use next tick — the one thing needing action here is dropping to
856	   * Peaceful, which must clear the hostiles already on the field (the directors
857	   * only govern *new* spawns). Resetting the starvation accumulator stops a stale
858	   * timer from biting right after a Peaceful→Hard flip. Unlike switchGameMode this
859	   * has no respawn-coupled state to guard, so it is allowed even while dead — a
860	   * player should be able to flip to Peaceful to stop a hostile pile-on.
861	   */
862	  private switchDifficulty(next: Difficulty): void {
863	    const state = this.state;
864	    if (state.hardcore) return; // locked to Hard for the world's life
865	    if (!isDifficulty(next) || next === state.difficulty) return;
866	    state.difficulty = next;
867	    state.timers.starvationTimer = 0;
868	    if (!hostilesSpawn(next)) this.despawnHostiles();
869	  }
870	
871	  /**
872	   * Silently removes every hostile mob (the Peaceful switch). A plain splice — not
873	   * removeMobAt — so there is no loot, no XP, and no false boss victory; the
874	   * renderer drops the orphaned models when it reconciles state.mobs by id.
875	   * Descends so the splices don't shift the indices still to be visited.
876	   */
877	  private despawnHostiles(): void {
878	    const mobs = this.state.mobs;
879	    for (let i = mobs.length - 1; i >= 0; i -= 1) {
880	      if (mobs[i].hostile) mobs.splice(i, 1);
881	    }
882	  }
883	
884	  private forceUnstuck(): void {
885	    const state = this.state;
886	    const safe = findSpawnOnLand(state.world, state.player.position.x, state.player.position.z, true);
887	    state.player.position.set(safe.x, safe.y, safe.z);
888	    state.player.velocity.set(0, 0, 0);
889	    state.player.onGround = false;
890	    state.worldMeshDirty = true;
891	  }
892	
893	  private respawn(): void {
894	    const state = this.state;
895	    const bed = state.spawnPoint;
896	    if (bed && state.world.get(bed.x, bed.y, bed.z) === BlockId.Bed) {
897	      // Respawn standing on the bed; the bed block is non-solid head room above it.
898	      state.player.position.set(bed.x + 0.5, bed.y + 1.05, bed.z + 0.5);
899	    } else {
900	      const spawn = randomLandPointNear(state.world, this.surfaceYAt, state.world.sizeX / 2, state.world.sizeZ / 2, RENDER_RADIUS * 0.9, this.rng);
901	      state.player.position.set(spawn.x, spawn.y + 2, spawn.z);
902	    }
903	    state.player.velocity.set(0, 0, 0);
904	    state.player.pitch = 0;
905	    clearEffects(state);
906	    resetMining(state);
907	    state.thrownSpears = [];
908	    state.fishing = null;
909	    state.worldMeshDirty = true;
910	    this.events.push({ type: "respawned" });
911	  }
912	
913	  /** Advances the day clock to the next morning after a sleep completes. */
914	  private wakeToMorning(): void {
915	    const state = this.state;
916	    const nextDay = Math.floor(state.dayClock / DAY_CYCLE_SECONDS) + 1;
917	    state.dayClock = (nextDay + WAKE_DAY_PHASE) * DAY_CYCLE_SECONDS;
918	    state.daylight = daylightAt(state.dayClock);
919	    state.daylightPercent = Math.round(state.daylight * 100);
920	    this.emit({ type: "wokeUp" });
921	  }
922	
923	  /** Refreshes the F3 readout at ~4 Hz so React is not re-rendered every frame. */
924	  private tickDebugInfo(dt: number): void {
925	    const state = this.state;
926	    if (!state.debugOpen) return;
927	    state.timers.debugHudTimer += dt;
928	    if (state.timers.debugHudTimer < 0.25) return;
929	    state.timers.debugHudTimer = 0;
930	    state.debugInfo = this.currentDebugInfo();
931	  }
932	
933	  private currentDebugInfo() {
934	    const { player, daylight } = this.state;
935	    return {
936	      x: Math.round(player.position.x * 10) / 10,
937	      y: Math.round(player.position.y * 10) / 10,
938	      z: Math.round(player.position.z * 10) / 10,
939	      daylight: Math.round(daylight * 100) / 100
940	    };
941	  }
942	
943	  /** Boss HUD data as a ref-stable object, rebuilt only when a visible rounded value moves. */
944	  private bossSnapshot(): ({ hpPercent: number } & BossTracking) | null {
945	    const bossMob = this.state.mobs.find((mob) => mob.kind === "boss");
946	    if (!bossMob) {
947	      if (this.lastBoss !== null) this.lastBoss = null;
948	      return null;
949	    }
950	    const percent = Math.max(0, Math.min(1, Math.round((bossMob.hp / BOSS_HP) * 100) / 100));
951	    const tracking = bossTracking(this.state.player, bossMob);
952	    if (
953	      !this.lastBoss ||
954	      percent !== this.lastBoss.hpPercent ||
955	      tracking.bearingDegrees !== this.lastBoss.bearingDegrees ||
956	      tracking.distanceBlocks !== this.lastBoss.distanceBlocks
957	    ) {
958	      this.lastBoss = { hpPercent: percent, ...tracking };
959	    }
960	    return this.lastBoss;
961	  }
962	
963	  /**
964	   * Active effects as a ref-stable array, rebuilt only when the visible content
965	   * (ids or rounded seconds) changes — `Math.ceil(remaining)` only moves on
966	   * integer-second boundaries, so the HUD countdown re-renders ~1 Hz, not 60 Hz.
967	   */
968	  private effectsProjection(): GameSnapshot["activeEffects"] {
969	    const next: GameSnapshot["activeEffects"] = [];
970	    for (const id of EFFECT_ORDER) {
971	      const remaining = this.state.effects.get(id);
972	      if (remaining && remaining > 0) next.push({ id, seconds: Math.ceil(remaining) });
973	    }
974	    const key = next.map((entry) => `${entry.id}:${entry.seconds}`).join(",");
975	    if (key !== this.lastEffectsKey) {
976	      this.lastEffectsKey = key;
977	      this.lastEffects = next;
978	    }
979	    return this.lastEffects;
980	  }
981	
982	  private buildSnapshot(): GameSnapshot {
983	    const state = this.state;
984	    return {
985	      api: this,
986	      inventory: state.inventory,
987	      equippedArmor: state.equippedArmor,
988	      selectedSlot: state.selectedSlot,
989	      gameMode: state.gameMode,
990	      difficulty: state.difficulty,
991	      hardcore: state.hardcore,
992	      gameOver: state.gameOver,
993	      isFlying: state.isFlying,
994	      hearts: state.hearts,
995	      hunger: state.hunger,
996	      oxygen: state.oxygen,
997	      daylightPercent: state.daylightPercent,
998	      passiveCount: state.mobs.reduce((acc, mob) => acc + (mob.hostile ? 0 : 1), 0),
999	      hostileCount: state.mobs.reduce((acc, mob) => acc + (mob.hostile ? 1 : 0), 0),
1000	      respawnSeconds: state.isDead ? Math.max(0, Math.ceil(state.respawnTimer)) : 0,
1001	      inventoryOpen: state.inventoryOpen,
1002	      paused: state.paused,
1003	      debugOpen: state.debugOpen,
1004	      debug: state.debugInfo,
1005	      cameraMode: state.cameraMode,
1006	      armorPoints: inv.equippedDefense(state.equippedArmor),
1007	      capsActive: state.capsActive,
1008	      sleeping: state.sleepTimer > 0,
1009	      craftingStation: state.craftingStation,
1010	      container: state.openContainerIndex !== null ? (state.containers.get(state.openContainerIndex) ?? null) : null,
1011	      boss: this.bossSnapshot(),
1012	      victory: state.victory,
1013	      activeEffects: this.effectsProjection(),
1014	      xpLevel: xpLevel(state.xp),
1015	      xpProgress: xpProgress(state.xp)
1016	    };
1017	  }
1018	
1019	  private refreshSnapshot(): void {
1020	    const next = this.buildSnapshot();
1021	    const prev = this.snapshot;
1022	    const changed = (Object.keys(next) as Array<keyof GameSnapshot>).some((key) => next[key] !== prev[key]);
1023	    if (!changed) return;
1024	    this.snapshot = next;
1025	    for (const listener of this.listeners) listener();
1026	  }
1027	}

599	// Villager trades are station-gated recipes, so they share the recipe book + the
600	// `craft` command. They live in their own module (the trade analog of recipes).
601	export const RECIPES: Recipe[] = [...CRAFTING_RECIPES, ...TRADES];
602	
603	// --- Recipe categories (recipe-book grouping) ---
604	
605	/**
606	 * The section a recipe falls under in the recipe book. Station recipes get their
607	 * own group (a furnace smelts, a villager trades); everything else is grouped by
608	 * the kind of item it produces.
609	 */
610	export type RecipeCategory = "Tools" | "Weapons" | "Armor" | "Building" | "Food" | "Materials" | "Smelting" | "Brewing" | "Trades";
611	
612	/** Fixed display order; `groupRecipes` emits sections in this sequence. */
613	export const RECIPE_CATEGORY_ORDER: RecipeCategory[] = ["Tools", "Weapons", "Armor", "Building", "Food", "Materials", "Smelting", "Brewing", "Trades"];
614	
615	const KIND_TO_CATEGORY: Record<ItemKind, RecipeCategory> = {
616	  tool: "Tools",
617	  weapon: "Weapons",
618	  armor: "Armor",
619	  block: "Building",
620	  food: "Food",
621	  material: "Materials"
622	};
623	
624	/**
625	 * The recipe book section for a recipe. Station recipes take precedence so
626	 * smelting and trades stay together; otherwise the category is derived from the
627	 * result item's `kind` (falling back to "Materials" for an unknown result).
628	 */
629	export function recipeCategory(recipe: Recipe): RecipeCategory {
630	  if (recipe.station === "villager") return "Trades";
631	  if (recipe.station === "furnace") return "Smelting";
632	  if (recipe.station === "brewing") return "Brewing";
633	  const kind = ITEM_DEF_BY_ID[recipe.result.slotId]?.kind;
634	  return kind ? KIND_TO_CATEGORY[kind] : "Materials";
635	}
636	
637	export type RecipeGroup = { category: RecipeCategory; recipes: Recipe[] };
638	
639	/**
640	 * Buckets recipes into the fixed category order, dropping empty categories.
641	 * Within each group, recipes the player can make right now (`canMakeNow`) are
642	 * listed first; both partitions preserve the original source order.
643	 */
644	export function groupRecipes(recipes: Recipe[], canMakeNow: (recipe: Recipe) => boolean): RecipeGroup[] {
645	  const buckets = new Map<RecipeCategory, Recipe[]>();
646	  for (const recipe of recipes) {
647	    const category = recipeCategory(recipe);
648	    const bucket = buckets.get(category);
649	    if (bucket) bucket.push(recipe);
650	    else buckets.set(category, [recipe]);
651	  }
652	
653	  const groups: RecipeGroup[] = [];
654	  for (const category of RECIPE_CATEGORY_ORDER) {
655	    const bucket = buckets.get(category);
656	    if (!bucket) continue;
657	    const ready: Recipe[] = [];
658	    const rest: Recipe[] = [];
659	    for (const recipe of bucket) (canMakeNow(recipe) ? ready : rest).push(recipe);
660	    groups.push({ category, recipes: [...ready, ...rest] });
661	  }
662	  return groups;
663	}
664	
```

55	function memoryStorage(initial: Record<string, string> = {}): Storage {
56	  const data = new Map(Object.entries(initial));
57	  return {
58	    get length() {
59	      return data.size;
60	    },
61	    clear: () => data.clear(),
62	    getItem: (key: string) => data.get(key) ?? null,
63	    key: (index: number) => [...data.keys()][index] ?? null,
64	    removeItem: (key: string) => void data.delete(key),
65	    setItem: (key: string, value: string) => void data.set(key, value)
66	  };
67	}

52	function mulberry32(seed: number): () => number {
53	  let t = seed >>> 0;
54	  return () => {
55	    t += 0x6d2b79f5;
56	    let r = Math.imul(t ^ (t >>> 15), 1 | t);
57	    r ^= r + Math.imul(r ^ (r >>> 7), 61 | r);
58	    return ((r ^ (r >>> 14)) >>> 0) / 4294967296;
59	  };
60	}
61	
62	function makeEngine(save: ReturnType<GameEngine["serialize"]> | null = null): GameEngine {
63	  return new GameEngine({ save, seed: 1337, rng: mulberry32(42), worldSize: { x: 64, y: 150, z: 64 } });
64	}
65	
66	function input(overrides: Partial<{ keys: string[]; capsActive: boolean; leftMouseHeld: boolean; pointerLocked: boolean }> = {}): FrameInput {
67	  return {
68	    keys: new Set(overrides.keys ?? []),
69	    capsActive: overrides.capsActive ?? false,
70	    leftMouseHeld: overrides.leftMouseHeld ?? false,
71	    pointerLocked: overrides.pointerLocked ?? false
72	  };
73	}
74	
75	function run(engine: GameEngine, seconds: number, frame: FrameInput = input()): void {
76	  const dt = 1 / 60;
77	  for (let t = 0; t < seconds; t += dt) engine.step(dt, frame);
78	}
79	
80	/**
81	 * The world boots at dawn (daylight 0.05), so the initial hostiles aggro
82	 * immediately. Tests about other mechanics clear them and move to midday.
83	 */
84	function calmDaytime(engine: GameEngine): void {
85	  engine.state.mobs = engine.state.mobs.filter((mob) => !mob.hostile);
86	  engine.state.dayClock = 60;
87	}

{
  "file_path": "/Users/hutusi/workspace/ai/david/monecraft/lib/game/engine/systems/advancements.ts",
  "content": "/**\n * Progression meta: gameplay statistics (running counters) and the advancement\n * registry. Everything funnels through `recordEvent`, called once per emitted\n * GameEvent from the engine's single emit chokepoint, plus `recordTick` for the\n * per-frame display counters (distance travelled, play time).\n *\n * Pure and declarative: it only reads/writes `state.stats` and\n * `state.advancements`, so it's trivially unit-testable without booting an\n * engine. `evaluateAdvancements` is a pure function of the current stats.\n */\nimport { BlockId } from \"@/lib/world\";\nimport { HOSTILE_MOB_KINDS } from \"@/lib/game/mobs\";\nimport { RECIPES } from \"@/lib/game/recipes\";\nimport type { GameEvent, GameState } from \"../state\";\n\n/** How a Statistics-tab value is rendered (a plain count vs. blocks travelled vs. a clock). */\nexport type StatFormat = \"count\" | \"distance\" | \"duration\";\n\n/** Display metadata for one statistic shown in the Statistics tab. */\nexport type StatMeta = { id: string; label: string; format: StatFormat };\n\n/**\n * The statistics surfaced (in order) in the Statistics tab. recordEvent/recordTick\n * may bump counters not listed here (the per-recipe `crafted_<id>` and the per-block\n * `*_mined` counters that drive advancements) — those stay out of the display.\n */\nexport const STATS: readonly StatMeta[] = [\n  { id: \"play_time\", label: \"Time Played\", format: \"duration\" },\n  { id: \"distance_walked\", label: \"Distance Travelled\", format: \"distance\" },\n  { id: \"blocks_mined\", label: \"Blocks Mined\", format: \"count\" },\n  { id: \"logs_chopped\", label: \"Logs Chopped\", format: \"count\" },\n  { id: \"diamond_ore_mined\", label: \"Diamond Ore Mined\", format: \"count\" },\n  { id: \"hostiles_killed\", label: \"Monsters Slain\", format: \"count\" },\n  { id: \"animals_bred\", label: \"Animals Bred\", format: \"count\" },\n  { id: \"items_crafted\", label: \"Items Crafted\", format: \"count\" },\n  { id: \"items_enchanted\", label: \"Items Enchanted\", format: \"count\" },\n  { id: \"potions_drunk\", label: \"Potions Drunk\", format: \"count\" },\n  { id: \"fish_caught\", label: \"Fish Caught\", format: \"count\" },\n  { id: \"arrows_fired\", label: \"Arrows Fired\", format: \"count\" },\n  { id: \"villager_trades\", label: \"Villager Trades\", format: \"count\" },\n  { id: \"jumps\", label: \"Jumps\", format: \"count\" },\n  { id: \"deaths\", label: \"Deaths\", format: \"count\" }\n];\n\n// Per-block \"mined\" counters. blockBroken always bumps the `blocks_mined` total;\n// these targeted counters drive the mining / farming advancements.\nconst MINED_STAT_BY_BLOCK: Partial<Record<BlockId, string>> = {\n  [BlockId.Wood]: \"logs_chopped\",\n  [BlockId.Stone]: \"stone_mined\",\n  [BlockId.CoalOre]: \"coal_ore_mined\",\n  [BlockId.SliverOre]: \"sliver_ore_mined\",\n  [BlockId.GoldOre]: \"gold_ore_mined\",\n  [BlockId.RubyOre]: \"ruby_ore_mined\",\n  [BlockId.SapphireOre]: \"sapphire_ore_mined\",\n  [BlockId.DiamondOre]: \"diamond_ore_mined\",\n  [BlockId.WheatStage3]: \"wheat_harvested\"\n};\n\nconst RECIPE_BY_ID = new Map(RECIPES.map((recipe) => [recipe.id, recipe]));\n\nfunction bump(state: GameState, id: string, by = 1): void {\n  state.stats.set(id, (state.stats.get(id) ?? 0) + by);\n}\n\n/**\n * Folds one gameplay event into the stat counters. Called for every event the\n * engine emits (the `advancementUnlocked` event is guarded out upstream so this\n * never recurses). Unknown / irrelevant event types are simply ignored.\n */\nexport function recordEvent(state: GameState, event: GameEvent): void {\n  switch (event.type) {\n    case \"blockBroken\": {\n      bump(state, \"blocks_mined\");\n      const mined = MINED_STAT_BY_BLOCK[event.blockId];\n      if (mined) bump(state, mined);\n      break;\n    }\n    case \"mobDied\":\n      if (HOSTILE_MOB_KINDS.has(event.kind)) bump(state, \"hostiles_killed\");\n      break;\n    case \"mobBred\":\n      bump(state, \"animals_bred\");\n      break;\n    case \"enchanted\":\n      bump(state, \"items_enchanted\");\n      break;\n    case \"drankPotion\":\n      bump(state, \"potions_drunk\");\n      break;\n    case \"fishingCaught\":\n      bump(state, \"fish_caught\");\n      break;\n    case \"bowFired\":\n      bump(state, \"arrows_fired\");\n      break;\n    case \"sleepStarted\":\n      bump(state, \"sleeps\");\n      break;\n    case \"bossDefeated\":\n      bump(state, \"boss_defeated\");\n      break;\n    case \"died\":\n      bump(state, \"deaths\");\n      break;\n    case \"jumped\":\n      bump(state, \"jumps\");\n      break;\n    case \"crafted\": {\n      const recipe = RECIPE_BY_ID.get(event.recipeId);\n      if (!recipe) break;\n      bump(state, \"items_crafted\");\n      bump(state, `crafted_${recipe.id}`);\n      // \"Tool Up\" wants any pickaxe (7 tiers, 7 recipes), so aggregate them.\n      if (recipe.result.slotId.endsWith(\"_pickaxe\")) bump(state, \"pickaxes_crafted\");\n      // A villager trade is a station-gated recipe — drive the trade advancement.\n      if (recipe.station === \"villager\") bump(state, \"villager_trades\");\n      break;\n    }\n  }\n}\n\n/**\n * Accumulates the per-frame display counters: seconds of active play and blocks\n * travelled. No advancement depends on these, so they stay out of recordEvent\n * (and the unlock path) — the engine calls this each active step.\n */\nexport function recordTick(state: GameState, dt: number, horizontalDistance: number): void {\n  bump(state, \"play_time\", dt);\n  bump(state, \"distance_walked\", horizontalDistance);\n}\n"
}

  "old_string": "/**\n * Current save shape (v12): armor moves to dedicated storage. `equippedArmor` is no\n * longer a by-id reference into the inventory (`SavedArmorById`) but the worn pieces\n * themselves (`SavedEquippedArmor`, per-slot `SavedSlot`). The v11→v12 migration\n * moves each legacy by-id equip out of `inventorySlots` into the armor record so a\n * worn piece no longer double-occupies an inventory slot.\n */\nexport type SaveData = Omit<SaveDataV11, \"version\" | \"equippedArmor\"> & {\n  version: 12;\n  equippedArmor?: SavedEquippedArmor;\n};",
  "new_string": "/**\n * v12 save shape: armor moves to dedicated storage. `equippedArmor` is no longer a\n * by-id reference into the inventory (`SavedArmorById`) but the worn pieces\n * themselves (`SavedEquippedArmor`, per-slot `SavedSlot`). The v11→v12 migration\n * moves each legacy by-id equip out of `inventorySlots` into the armor record so a\n * worn piece no longer double-occupies an inventory slot.\n */\nexport type SaveDataV12 = Omit<SaveDataV11, \"version\" | \"equippedArmor\"> & {\n  version: 12;\n  equippedArmor?: SavedEquippedArmor;\n};\n\n/** One persisted statistic: its id and accumulated value (kept as a number — play_time/distance are fractional). */\nexport type SavedStat = { id: string; value: number };\n\n/**\n * Current save shape (v13): progression meta. `stats` are the running gameplay\n * counters and `advancements` the unlocked ids. Both are optional, so the v12→v13\n * migration is a pure version bump and pre-v13 saves load with none. Like `xp`\n * (and unlike `effects`), both are long-term and are NOT cleared on death.\n */\nexport type SaveData = Omit<SaveDataV12, \"version\"> & {\n  version: 13;\n  stats?: SavedStat[];\n  advancements?: string[];\n};"
}
```

{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/Users/hutusi/workspace/ai/david/monecraft/lib/game/save.ts",
  "old_string": "export function migrateSaveV11toV12(save: SaveDataV11): SaveData {\n  const legacy = save.equippedArmor;\n  if (!legacy || !Array.isArray(save.inventorySlots)) {\n    return { ...save, version: 12, equippedArmor: undefined };\n  }\n  const slots = save.inventorySlots.slice();\n  const equipped: SavedEquippedArmor = {};\n  for (const armorSlot of ARMOR_SLOTS) {\n    const id = legacy[armorSlot];\n    if (!id) continue;\n    const def = ITEM_DEF_BY_ID[id];\n    if (def?.kind !== \"armor\" || def.armorSlot !== armorSlot) continue; // drop invalid equips\n    const idx = slots.findIndex((s) => s?.id === id && s.count > 0);\n    if (idx < 0) continue; // item not in inventory → drop the equip\n    equipped[armorSlot] = slots[idx]; // preserves durability/enchantments/customName\n    slots[idx] = { id: null, count: 0 };\n  }\n  return { ...save, version: 12, inventorySlots: slots, equippedArmor: equipped };\n}",
  "new_string": "export function migrateSaveV11toV12(save: SaveDataV11): SaveDataV12 {\n  const legacy = save.equippedArmor;\n  if (!legacy || !Array.isArray(save.inventorySlots)) {\n    return { ...save, version: 12, equippedArmor: undefined };\n  }\n  const slots = save.inventorySlots.slice();\n  const equipped: SavedEquippedArmor = {};\n  for (const armorSlot of ARMOR_SLOTS) {\n    const id = legacy[armorSlot];\n    if (!id) continue;\n    const def = ITEM_DEF_BY_ID[id];\n    if (def?.kind !== \"armor\" || def.armorSlot !== armorSlot) continue; // drop invalid equips\n    const idx = slots.findIndex((s) => s?.id === id && s.count > 0);\n    if (idx < 0) continue; // item not in inventory → drop the equip\n    equipped[armorSlot] = slots[idx]; // preserves durability/enchantments/customName\n    slots[idx] = { id: null, count: 0 };\n  }\n  return { ...save, version: 12, inventorySlots: slots, equippedArmor: equipped };\n}\n\n/**\n * Migrates a v12 save to v13 — a pure version bump. `stats` (gameplay counters)\n * and `advancements` (unlocked ids) are optional, so a pre-progression save\n * simply loads with no stats and no advancements yet earned.\n */\nexport function migrateSaveV12toV13(save: SaveDataV12): SaveData {\n  return { ...save, version: 13 };\n}"
}

  "new_string": "    const parsed = JSON.parse(raw) as\n      | SaveData\n      | SaveDataV12\n      | SaveDataV11\n      | SaveDataV10\n      | SaveDataV9\n      | SaveDataV8\n      | SaveDataV7\n      | SaveDataV6\n      | SaveDataV5\n      | SaveDataV4\n      | SaveDataV3\n      | SaveDataV2\n      | SaveDataV1;\n    if (!parsed || !Number.isFinite(parsed.seed) || !Array.isArray(parsed.changes)) return null;\n    let migrated: SaveDataV2 | SaveDataV3 | SaveDataV4 | SaveDataV5 | SaveDataV6 | SaveDataV7 | SaveDataV8 | SaveDataV9 | SaveDataV10 | SaveDataV11 | SaveDataV12 | SaveData =\n      parsed.version === 1 ? migrateSaveV1toV2(parsed) : parsed;\n    if (migrated.version === 2) migrated = migrateSaveV2toV3(migrated);\n    if (migrated.version === 3) migrated = migrateSaveV3toV4(migrated);\n    if (migrated.version === 4) migrated = migrateSaveV4toV5(migrated);\n    if (migrated.version === 5) migrated = migrateSaveV5toV6(migrated);\n    if (migrated.version === 6) migrated = migrateSaveV6toV7(migrated);\n    if (migrated.version === 7) migrated = migrateSaveV7toV8(migrated);\n    if (migrated.version === 8) migrated = migrateSaveV8toV9(migrated);\n    if (migrated.version === 9) migrated = migrateSaveV9toV10(migrated);\n    if (migrated.version === 10) migrated = migrateSaveV10toV11(migrated);\n    if (migrated.version === 11) migrated = migrateSaveV11toV12(migrated);\n    if (migrated.version === 12) migrated = migrateSaveV12toV13(migrated);\n    if (migrated.version !== 13) return null;\n    return migrated;"

{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/Users/hutusi/workspace/ai/david/monecraft/lib/game/save.ts",
  "old_string": "/** Reads active effects from a save, dropping unknown ids and non-positive/garbage durations. */\nexport function restoreEffects(save: SaveData): SavedEffect[] {\n  if (!Array.isArray(save.effects)) return [];\n  const out: SavedEffect[] = [];\n  for (const entry of save.effects) {\n    if (!entry || typeof entry.id !== \"string\" || !Object.hasOwn(VALID_EFFECT_IDS, entry.id)) continue;\n    if (!Number.isFinite(entry.remaining) || entry.remaining <= 0) continue;\n    out.push({ id: entry.id, remaining: entry.remaining });\n  }\n  return out;\n}",
  "new_string": "/** Reads active effects from a save, dropping unknown ids and non-positive/garbage durations. */\nexport function restoreEffects(save: SaveData): SavedEffect[] {\n  if (!Array.isArray(save.effects)) return [];\n  const out: SavedEffect[] = [];\n  for (const entry of save.effects) {\n    if (!entry || typeof entry.id !== \"string\" || !Object.hasOwn(VALID_EFFECT_IDS, entry.id)) continue;\n    if (!Number.isFinite(entry.remaining) || entry.remaining <= 0) continue;\n    out.push({ id: entry.id, remaining: entry.remaining });\n  }\n  return out;\n}\n\n/**\n * Snapshots the gameplay statistics for persistence. Zero/absent counters carry\n * no data (an unset stat reads as 0 anyway), so only positive values are written.\n * Unlike effect ids, stat ids are an open set (per-recipe/per-block counters), so\n * there's no allow-list — only a finite, positive-value check.\n */\nexport function serializeStats(stats: Map<string, number>): SavedStat[] {\n  const out: SavedStat[] = [];\n  for (const [id, value] of stats) {\n    if (Number.isFinite(value) && value > 0) out.push({ id, value });\n  }\n  return out;\n}\n\n/** Reads gameplay statistics from a save, dropping non-string ids and negative / garbage values. */\nexport function restoreStats(save: SaveData): SavedStat[] {\n  if (!Array.isArray(save.stats)) return [];\n  const out: SavedStat[] = [];\n  for (const entry of save.stats) {\n    if (!entry || typeof entry.id !== \"string\") continue;\n    if (!Number.isFinite(entry.value) || entry.value < 0) continue;\n    out.push({ id: entry.id, value: entry.value });\n  }\n  return out;\n}"
}

\"@/lib/game/engine/systems/advancements\";\nimport type { GameEvent, GameState } from \"@/lib/game/engine/state\";\n\n// recordEvent / recordTick only ever touch `state.stats`, so a bare stub is all\n// the unit needs — no engine, world, or Three.js.\nfunction freshState(): GameState {\n  return { stats: new Map<string, number>() } as unknown as GameState;\n}\n\nfunction record(state: GameState, ...events: GameEvent[]): void {\n  for (const event of events) recordEvent(state, event);\n}\n\ndescribe(\"recordEvent — block mining\", () => {\n  test(\"every break bumps the blocks_mined total\", () => {\n    const state = freshState();\n    record(state, { type: \"blockBroken\", blockId: BlockId.Dirt, x: 0, y: 0, z: 0 });\n    expect(state.stats.get(\"blocks_mined\")).toBe(1);\n    // A plain block has no targeted counter.\n    expect(state.stats.get(\"logs_chopped\")).toBeUndefined();\n  });\n\n  test(\"a log bumps logs_chopped alongside the total\", () => {\n    const state = freshState();\n    record(state, { type: \"blockBroken\", blockId: BlockId.Wood, x: 0, y: 0, z: 0 });\n    expect(state.stats.get(\"blocks_mined\")).toBe(1);\n    expect(state.stats.get(\"logs_chopped\")).toBe(1);\n  });\n\n  test(\"ores bump their per-ore counter\", () => {\n    const state = freshState();\n    record(\n      state,\n      { type: \"blockBroken\", blockId: BlockId.SliverOre, x: 0, y: 0, z: 0 },\n      { type: \"blockBroken\", blockId: BlockId.DiamondOre, x: 0, y: 0, z: 0 }\n    );\n    expect(state.stats.get(\"sliver_ore_mined\")).toBe(1);\n    expect(state.stats.get(\"diamond_ore_mined\")).toBe(1);\n    expect(state.stats.get(\"blocks_mined\")).toBe(2);\n  });\n\n  test(\"harvesting mature wheat bumps wheat_harvested (immature stages do not)\", () => {\n    const state = freshState();\n    record(\n      state,\n      { type: \"blockBroken\", blockId: BlockId.WheatStage1, x: 0, y: 0, z: 0 },\n      { type: \"blockBroken\", blockId: BlockId.WheatStage3, x: 0, y: 0, z: 0 }\n    );\n    expect(state.stats.get(\"wheat_harvested\")).toBe(1);\n  });\n});\n\ndescribe(\"recordEvent — combat & mobs\", () => {\n  test(\"killing a hostile bumps hostiles_killed; a passive animal does not\", () => {\n    const state = freshState();\n    record(\n      state,\n      { type: \"mobDied\", kind: \"zombie\", x: 0, y: 0, z: 0 },\n      { type: \"mobDied\", kind: \"sheep\", x: 0, y: 0, z: 0 }\n    );\n    expect(state.stats.get(\"hostiles_killed\")).toBe(1);\n  });\n\n  test(\"the boss counts as a hostile kill\", () => {\n    const state = freshState();\n    record(state, { type: \"mobDied\", kind: \"boss\", x: 0, y: 0, z: 0 });\n    expect(state.stats.get(\"hostiles_killed\")).toBe(1);\n  });\n\n  test(\"breeding, firing, and the boss kill each have their own counter\", () => {\n    const state = freshState();\n    record(\n      state,\n      { type: \"mobBred\", kind: \"cow\" },\n      { type: \"bowFired\" },\n      { type: \"bossDefeated\", x: 0, y: 0, z: 0 }\n    );\n    expect(state.stats.get(\"animals_bred\")).toBe(1);\n    expect(state.stats.get(\"arrows_fired\")).toBe(1);\n    expect(state.stats.get(\"boss_defeated\")).toBe(1);\n  });\n});\n\ndescribe(\"recordEvent — crafting\", () => {\n  test(\"any pickaxe recipe bumps the aggregate pickaxes_crafted\", () => {\n    const state = freshState();\n    record(state, { type: \"crafted\", recipeId: \"stone_pickaxe\" });\n    expect(state.stats.get(\"items_crafted\")).toBe(1);\n    expect(state.stats.get(\"pickaxes_crafted\")).toBe(1);\n    expect(state.stats.get(\"crafted_stone_pickaxe\")).toBe(1);\n    expect(state.stats.get(\"villager_trades\")).toBeUndefined();\n  });\n\n  test(\"the furnace recipe drives crafted_furnace but not pickaxes_crafted\", () => {\n    const state = freshState();\n    record(state, { type: \"crafted\", recipeId: \"furnace\" });\n    expect(state.stats.get(\"crafted_furnace\")).toBe(1);\n    expect(state.stats.get(\"pickaxes_crafted\")).toBeUndefined();\n  });\n\n  test(\"a villager trade bumps villager_trades\", () => {\n    const state = freshState();\n    record(state, { type: \"crafted\", recipeId: \"trade_wheat\" });\n    expect(state.stats.get(\"items_crafted\")).toBe(1);\n    expect(state.stats.get(\"villager_trades\")).toBe(1);\n  });\n\n  test(\"an unknown recipe id records nothing\", () => {\n    const state = freshState();\n    record(state, { type: \"crafted\", recipeId: \"not_a_recipe\" });\n    expect(state.stats.size).toBe(0);\n  });\n});\n\ndescribe(\"recordEvent — system events & accumulation\", () => {\n  test(\"each tracked event maps to its counter\", () => {\n    const state = freshState();\n    record(\n      state,\n      { type: \"enchanted\", enchant: \"sharpness\" },\n      { type: \"drankPotion\" },\n      { type: \"fishingCaught\", items: [], x: 0, y: 0, z: 0 },\n      { type: \"sleepStarted\" },\n      { type: expect(state.stats.get(\"fish_caught\")).toBe(1);\n    expect(state.stats.get(\"sleeps\")).toBe(1);\n    expect(state.stats.get(\"deaths\")).toBe(1);\n    expect(state.stats.get(\"jumps\")).toBe(1);\n  });\n\n  test(\"counters accumulate across repeated events\", () => {\n    const state = freshState();\n    record(state, { type: \"jumped\" }, { type: \"jumped\" }, { type: \"jumped\" });\n    expect(state.stats.get(\"jumps\")).toBe(3);\n  });\n\n  test(\"irrelevant events record nothing\", () => {\n    const state = freshState();\n    record(state, { type: \"blockPlaced\", blockId: BlockId.Dirt, x: 0, y: 0, z: 0 }, { type: \"attackSwung\" });\n    expect(state.stats.size).toBe(0);\n  });\n});\n\ndescribe(\"recordTick\", () => {\n  test(\"accumulates play time and distance travelled\", () => {\n    const state = freshState();\n    recordTick(state, 0.5, 1.25);\n    recordTick(state, 0.5, 0.75);\n    expect(state.stats.get(\"play_time\")).toBe(1);\n    expect(state.stats.get(\"distance_walked\")).toBe(2);\n  });\n});\n\ndescribe(\"STATS metadata\", () => {\n  test(\"the displayed stat ids are unique\", () => {\n    const ids = STATS.map((entry) => entry.id);\n    expect(new Set(ids).size).toBe(ids.length);\n  });\n});\n"

{
  "command": "cd /Users/hutusi/workspace/ai/david/monecraft\ngit commit -q -F - <<'EOF'\nfeat(stats): track gameplay statistics\n\nMonecraft is content-rich but goal-poor — there was no record of what a\nplayer has done across a world. This adds running gameplay statistics\n(blocks mined, mobs killed, items crafted/enchanted, potions drunk, fish\ncaught, distance travelled, time played, …) as the foundation for the\nadvancements that follow.\n\nEvery system already emits through the single GameEngine.emit chokepoint,\nso one observer there folds each event into the counters — no per-system\nbookkeeping. A new declarative `advancements` module owns the mapping\n(recordEvent) plus the per-frame tick counters (recordTick); it is pure\nand reads/writes only state.stats, so it unit-tests without an engine.\n\nA new `crafted` event is emitted on every successful craft (the handler\npreviously only fired `smelted` for station recipes). It drives\nitems_crafted, the per-recipe and aggregate pickaxe counters, and the\nvillager-trade counter — and is broadly useful beyond stats.\n\nHostility isn't stored on MOB_TEMPLATES (it's set per-mob at spawn), so a\nnew HOSTILE_MOB_KINDS set in mobs.ts is the single source of truth for\ncounting hostile kills.\n\nStats persist via save v12 → v13 (additive, pure migration) and, like xp\nand unlike effects, survive death — clearEffects never touches them.\nEOF\ngit log --oneline -1",
  "description": "Commit slice 1"
}

export function createInitialInventory(): InventorySlot[] {
  const slots: InventorySlot[] = Array.from({ length: INVENTORY_SLOTS }, () => createEmptySlot());
  const starter: Array<{ id: string; count: number }> = [
    { id: "grass", count: 64 },
    { id: "dirt", count: 64 },
    { id: "stone", count: 64 },
    { id: "wood", count: 64 },
    { id: "planks", count: 20 },
    { id: "cobble", count: 20 },
    { id: "sand", count: 20 },
    { id: "wood_pickaxe", count: 1 },
    { id: "knife", count: 1 }
  ];
  for (let i = 0; i < starter.length && i < slots.length; i += 1) slots[i] = createSlot(starter[i].id, starter[i].count);
  return slots;
}

  /** Drops a stationary mob at an offset from the player (test-controlled stats). */
  function spawnTestMob(engine: GameEngine, kind: MobKind, hostile: boolean, offset: { x: number; y: number; z: number }): void {
    const { state } = engine;
    const p = state.player.position;
    state.mobs.push({
      id: state.nextMobId++,
      kind,
      hostile,
      hp: 50,
      position: new THREE.Vector3(p.x + offset.x, p.y + offset.y, p.z + offset.z),
      direction: new THREE.Vector3(0, 0, 1),
      yaw: 0,
      turnTimer: 9,
      speed: 0,
      moveSpeed: 0,
      detectRange: 12,
      attackDamage: 2,
      attackCooldown: 5,
      attackTimer: 0,
      halfHeight: 0.9,
      bobSeed: 0,
      fedTimer: 0,
      ageTimer: 0
    });
  }

  test("breaking a block emits blockBroken with the block id", () => {
    const engine = makeEngine();
    calmDaytime(engine);
    run(engine, 1);
    const { state } = engine;
    const px = Math.floor(state.player.position.x);
    const py = Math.floor(state.player.position.y) - 1;
    const pz = Math.floor(state.player.position.z);
    const targetBlock = state.world.get(px, py, pz);
    state.player.position.x = px + 0.5;
    state.player.position.z = pz + 0.5;
    state.player.pitch = -Math.PI / 2 + 0.02;
    engine.consumeEvents();
    run(engine, 4, input({ leftMouseHeld: true, pointerLocked: true }));
    const events = engine.consumeEvents();
    expect(events.some((event) => event.type === "blockBroken" && event.blockId === targetBlock)).toBe(true);
  });

  test("placing a block emits blockPlaced with the block id", () => {
    const engine = makeEngine();
    calmDaytime(engine);
    run(engine, 1);
    const { state } = engine;
    const ex = Math.floor(state.player.position.x);
    const ez = Math.floor(state.player.position.z);
    state.player.position.x = ex + 0.5;
    state.player.position.z = ez + 0.5;
    state.player.yaw = 0; // looking -Z
    state.player.pitch = 0;
    // A clear shooting lane at eye height ending in a stone backstop.
    const ey = Math.floor(state.player.position.y + EYE_HEIGHT);
    state.blockChanges.set(ex, ey, ez - 1, BlockId.Air);
    state.blockChanges.set(ex, ey, ez - 2, BlockId.Air);
    state.blockChanges.set(ex, ey, ez - 3, BlockId.Stone);
    engine.consumeEvents();
    engine.dispatch({ type: "placeBlock" }); // slot 0 holds grass blocks
    const events = engine.consumeEvents();
    expect(events.some((event) => event.type === "blockPlaced" && event.blockId === BlockId.Grass)).toBe(true);
    expect(state.world.get(ex, ey, ez - 2)).toBe(BlockId.Grass);
  });

Slayer\", description: \"Defeat the Dragon Lord.\", icon: \"dragon_heart\", category: \"Combat\", stat: \"boss_defeated\", threshold: 1 },\n  { id: \"two_by_two\", title: \"Two by Two\", description: \"Breed two animals.\", icon: \"wheat\", category: \"Farming\", stat: \"animals_bred\", threshold: 1 },\n  { id: \"time_to_farm\", title: \"Time to Farm\", description: \"Harvest fully-grown wheat.\", icon: \"bread\", category: \"Farming\", stat: \"wheat_harvested\", threshold: 1 },\n  { id: \"gone_fishing\", title: \"Gone Fishing\", description: \"Reel in a catch.\", icon: \"fishing_rod\", category: \"Farming\", stat: \"fish_caught\", threshold: 1 },\n  { id: \"enchanter\", title: \"Enchanter\", description: \"Enchant an item.\", icon: \"enchanting_table\", category: \"Magic\", stat: \"items_enchanted\", threshold: 1 },\n  { id: \"local_brewery\", title: \"Local Brewery\", description: \"Drink a potion.\", icon: \"potion_strength\", category: \"Magic\", stat: \"potions_drunk\", threshold: 1 },\n  { id: \"sleep_tight\", title: \"Sleep Tight\", description: \"Sleep through the night.\", icon: \"bed\", category: \"Adventure\", stat: \"sleeps\", threshold: 1 },\n  { id: \"hired_help\", title: \"Hired Help\", description: \"Trade with a villager.\", icon: \"emerald\", category: \"Adventure\", stat: \"villager_trades\", threshold: 1 }\n];\n\n/** Lookup by id — used to resolve an unlock's display title for the toast. */\nexport const ADVANCEMENTS_BY_ID: Record<string, Advancement> = Object.fromEntries(ADVANCEMENTS.map((advancement) => [advancement.id, advancement]));\n\n/**\n * Returns the ids of advancements that just became earnable: not yet unlocked\n * and whose stat has reached its threshold. Pure over the current state, so the\n * engine can add each to `state.advancements` and announce it. Order follows the\n * registry, so the result is deterministic regardless of which event triggered it.\n */\nexport function evaluateAdvancements(state: GameState): string[] {\n  const unlocked: string[] = [];\n  for (const advancement of ADVANCEMENTS) {\n    if (state.advancements.has(advancement.id)) continue;\n    if ((state.stats.get(advancement.stat) ?? 0) >= advancement.threshold) unlocked.push(advancement.id);\n  }\n  return unlocked;\n}"
}

379	/**
380	 * Snapshots the gameplay statistics for persistence. Zero/absent counters carry
381	 * no data (an unset stat reads as 0 anyway), so only positive values are written.
382	 * Unlike effect ids, stat ids are an open set (per-recipe/per-block counters), so
383	 * there's no allow-list — only a finite, positive-value check.
384	 */
385	export function serializeStats(stats: Map<string, number>): SavedStat[] {
386	  const out: SavedStat[] = [];
387	  for (const [id, value] of stats) {
388	    if (Number.isFinite(value) && value > 0) out.push({ id, value });
389	  }
390	  return out;
391	}

{

  "old_string": "/** Reads gameplay statistics from a save, dropping non-string ids and negative / garbage values. */\nexport function restoreStats(save: SaveData): SavedStat[] {\n  if (!Array.isArray(save.stats)) return [];\n  const out: SavedStat[] = [];\n  for (const entry of save.stats) {\n    if (!entry || typeof entry.id !== \"string\") continue;\n    if (!Number.isFinite(entry.value) || entry.value < 0) continue;\n    out.push({ id: entry.id, value: entry.value });\n  }\n  return out;\n}",
  "new_string": "/** Reads gameplay statistics from a save, dropping non-string ids and negative / garbage values. */\nexport function restoreStats(save: SaveData): SavedStat[] {\n  if (!Array.isArray(save.stats)) return [];\n  const out: SavedStat[] = [];\n  for (const entry of save.stats) {\n    if (!entry || typeof entry.id !== \"string\") continue;\n    if (!Number.isFinite(entry.value) || entry.value < 0) continue;\n    out.push({ id: entry.id, value: entry.value });\n  }\n  return out;\n}\n\n/** Reads the unlocked advancement ids from a save (non-empty strings only, de-duplicated). */\nexport function restoreAdvancements(save: SaveData): string[] {\n  if (!Array.isArray(save.advancements)) return [];\n  const seen = new Set<string>();\n  for (const id of save.advancements) {\n    if (typeof id === \"string\" && id) seen.add(id);\n  }\n  return [...seen];\n}"
}

{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/Users/hutusi/workspace/ai/david/monecraft/lib/game/engine/GameEngine.ts",
  "old_string": "  private emit = (event: GameEvent): void => {\n    this.events.push(event);\n    // Fold every gameplay event into the statistics counters at the one chokepoint\n    // every system already emits through — no per-system bookkeeping.\n    recordEvent(this.state, event);\n  };",
  "new_string": "  private emit = (event: GameEvent): void => {\n    this.events.push(event);\n    // Observe progress at the one chokepoint every system already emits through —\n    // but guard against recursion: observing an unlock re-enters emit.\n    if (event.type !== \"advancementUnlocked\") this.observeProgress(event);\n  };\n\n  /**\n   * Folds a gameplay event into the statistics counters, then unlocks any\n   * advancement whose threshold the new stats just crossed — adding it to the set\n   * (before the emit, so a re-entrant observe is idempotent) and announcing it.\n   */\n  private observeProgress(event: GameEvent): void {\n    recordEvent(this.state, event);\n    for (const id of evaluateAdvancements(this.state)) {\n      this.state.advancements.add(id);\n      this.emit({ type: \"advancementUnlocked\", id, name: ADVANCEMENTS_BY_ID[id].title });\n    }\n  }"
}
