expect(engine.state.stats.get(\"deaths\")).toBe(1); // the death itself is counted\n  });\n});\n\ndescribe(\"advancements (save v13)\", () => {\n  const die = (engine: GameEngine) => (engine as unknown as { applyDamage: (amount: number) => void }).applyDamage(100);\n  const removeMobAt = (engine: GameEngine, index: number) => (engine as unknown as { removeMobAt: (i: number, looting?: number) => void }).removeMobAt(index);\n\n  function pushHostile(engine: GameEngine, kind: MobKind): void {\n    const p = engine.state.player.position;\n    engine.state.mobs.push({\n      id: engine.state.nextMobId++,\n      kind,\n      hostile: true,\n      hp: 1,\n      position: new THREE.Vector3(p.x, p.y, p.z),\n      direction: new THREE.Vector3(0, 0, 1),\n      yaw: 0,\n      turnTimer: 0,\n      speed: 0,\n      moveSpeed: 0,\n      detectRange: 0,\n      attackDamage: 0,\n      attackCooldown: 0,\n      attackTimer: 0,\n      halfHeight: 0.9,\n      bobSeed: 0,\n      fedTimer: 0,\n      ageTimer: 0\n    });\n  }\n\n  test(\"crafting a pickaxe unlocks Tool Up once, announcing it\", () => {\n    const engine = makeEngine();\n    engine.dispatch({ type: \"craft\", recipeId: \"wood_pickaxe\" });\n    const unlocks = engine.consumeEvents().filter((event) => event.type === \"advancementUnlocked\");\n    expect(unlocks).toHaveLength(1);\n    expect(unlocks[0]).toMatchObject({ id: \"tool_up\", name: \"Tool Up\" });\n    expect(engine.state.advancements.has(\"tool_up\")).toBe(true);\n    // A second pickaxe must not re-announce the already-earned advancement.\n    engine.dispatch({ type: \"craft\", recipeId: \"wood_pickaxe\" });\n    expect(engine.consumeEvents().some((event) => event.type === \"advancementUnlocked\")).toBe(false);\n  });\n\n  test(\"killing a hostile unlocks Monster Hunter through the emit chokepoint\", () => {\n    const engine = makeEngine();\n    engine.state.mobs = [];\n    pushHostile(engine, \"zombie\");\n    removeMobAt(engine, engine.state.mobs.length - 1);\n    expect(engine.consumeEvents().some((event) => event.type === \"advancementUnlocked\" && event.id === \"monster_hunter\")).toBe(true);\n    expect(engine.state.advancements.has(\"monster_hunter\")).toBe(true);\n  });\n\n  test(\"advancements persist through serialize -> reload and survive death\", () => {\n    const engine = makeEngine();\n    engine.state.mobs = [];\n    engine.dispatch({ type: \"craft\", recipeId: \"wood_pickaxe\" }); // unlocks tool_up\n    expect(engine.state.advancements.has(\"tool_up\")).toBe(true);\n\n    const restored = makeEngine(engine.serialize());\n    expect(restored.state.advancements.has(\"tool_up\")).toBe(true);\n\n    die(engine);\n    expect(engine.state.isDead).toBe(true);\n    expect(engine.state.advancements.has(\"tool_up\")).toBe(true); // not cleared on death\n  });\n});"

{
  "command": "cd /Users/hutusi/workspace/ai/david/monecraft\ngit add lib/game/engine/systems/advancements.ts lib/game/engine/systems/advancements.test.ts \\\n  lib/game/engine/state.ts lib/game/save.ts lib/game/save.test.ts \\\n  lib/game/engine/GameEngine.ts lib/game/engine/GameEngine.test.ts\ngit commit -q -F - <<'EOF'\nfeat(advancements): registry + unlock tracking\n\nBuilds the guided-progression layer on top of the statistics from the\nprevious commit. A flat set of 16 advancements — one per major system\n(mining tiers, crafting a pickaxe/furnace, combat, the boss, breeding,\nfarming, fishing, enchanting, brewing, sleeping, trading) — is a single\ndeclarative table: each row is { stat, threshold }, and unlock is uniformly\n`stats.get(stat) >= threshold`, so evaluateAdvancements is a pure function\nand adding one is a one-line edit with zero new assets (icons reuse\nexisting item/block sprites).\n\nUnlocking hooks the same emit chokepoint as the stats: observeProgress\nrecords the event, then unlocks any advancement the new counters just\ncrossed, adds it to state.advancements before announcing it (so a\nre-entrant observe is idempotent), and emits `advancementUnlocked`. emit\nguards that event type to avoid recursing into itself.\n\nadvancements persist via save v13 alongside stats and, like xp and unlike\neffects, survive death — verified by a round-trip-then-die engine test.\nEOF\ngit log --oneline -3",
  "description": "Commit slice 2"
}

1	import type { GameEngine } from "@/lib/game/engine/GameEngine";
2	import type { FrameInput } from "@/lib/game/engine/state";
3	import { FLY_DOUBLE_TAP_WINDOW_SECONDS } from "@/lib/game/config";
4	
5	const MOUSE_SENSITIVITY = 0.0021;
6	
7	type MutableInput = {
8	  keys: Set<string>;
9	  capsActive: boolean;
10	  leftMouseHeld: boolean;
11	  pointerLocked: boolean;
12	};
13	
14	export type InputController = {
15	  /** Live continuous-input view, passed to engine.step every frame. */
16	  readonly input: FrameInput;
17	  /** Drops held keys and the mouse button (on death/respawn). */
18	  clearKeys(): void;
19	  dispose(): void;
20	};
21	
22	type CreateInputControllerArgs = {
23	  /** The WebGL canvas — pointer lock target. */
24	  canvas: HTMLCanvasElement;
25	  engine: GameEngine;
26	  onResize: () => void;
27	  onLockChange: (locked: boolean) => void;
28	};
29	
30	/**
31	 * Owns every DOM listener. Continuous input (keys, mouse button, pointer
32	 * lock) is exposed as a FrameInput the engine reads each step; discrete
33	 * actions become engine commands; mouse-look goes through engine.applyLook.
34	 */
35	export function createInputController(args: CreateInputControllerArgs): InputController {
36	  const { canvas, engine, onResize, onLockChange } = args;
37	
38	  const input: MutableInput = {
39	    keys: new Set<string>(),
40	    capsActive: false,
41	    leftMouseHeld: false,
42	    pointerLocked: false
43	  };
44	
45	  const uiBlocked = () => engine.state.inventoryOpen || engine.state.isDead || engine.state.paused;
46	
47	  // Timestamp (ms) of the last discrete Space press, for double-tap-to-fly detection.
48	  let lastSpaceTapAt = -Infinity;
49	
50	  const onMouseMove = (evt: MouseEvent) => {
51	    if (!input.pointerLocked) return;
52	    engine.applyLook(-evt.movementX * MOUSE_SENSITIVITY, -evt.movementY * MOUSE_SENSITIVITY);
53	  };
54	
55	  const onKeyDown = (evt: KeyboardEvent) => {
56	    // Escape under pointer lock never reaches us — the browser consumes it to
57	    // exit the lock, and the pointerlockchange handler below opens the menu.
58	    if (evt.code === "Escape") {
59	      if (engine.state.paused) engine.dispatch({ type: "resume" });
60	      else if (engine.state.inventoryOpen) engine.dispatch({ type: "toggleInventory" });
61	      else if (!input.pointerLocked) engine.dispatch({ type: "pause" });
62	      return;
63	    }
64	
65	    // Render-only and engine-supported in every state, so it works even from
66	    // the pause menu — like Minecraft's F5.
67	    if (evt.code === "KeyV") {
68	      engine.dispatch({ type: "toggleCameraView" });
69	      return;
70	    }
71	
72	    if (engine.state.paused) return;
73	
74	    if (evt.code === "F3") {
75	      evt.preventDefault();
76	      engine.dispatch({ type: "toggleDebug" });
77	      return;
78	    }
79	
80	    if (evt.code.startsWith("Digit")) {
81	      const idx = Number.parseInt(evt.code.slice(5), 10) - 1;
82	      if (idx >= 0) engine.dispatch({ type: "selectSlot", index: idx });
83	    }
84	
85	    if (evt.code === "KeyI") {
86	      engine.dispatch({ type: "toggleInventory" });
87	      input.keys.clear();
88	      if (input.pointerLocked) document.exitPointerLock();
89	      return;
90	    }
91	
92	    // Shift-gated so a stray `U` can't teleport the player mid-play; the 0.8s
93	    // auto-unstuck safeguard still covers genuine wedged-in-terrain cases.
94	    if (evt.shiftKey && evt.code === "KeyU") {
95	      engine.dispatch({ type: "unstuck" });
96	      return;
97	    }
98	
99	    if (uiBlocked()) return;
100	
101	    if (evt.code === "KeyE") {
102	      evt.preventDefault();
103	      engine.dispatch({ type: "placeBlock" });
104	    }
105	    if (evt.code === "KeyF") {
106	      evt.preventDefault();
107	      // One key for consumables: a held potion is drunk for its effect, anything else is eaten.
108	      const held = engine.state.inventory[engine.state.selectedSlot];
109	      engine.dispatch({ type: held?.effect ? "drinkPotion" : "eatFood" });
110	    }
111	
112	    // Double-tap Space toggles flight (Creative/Spectator). Ignore auto-repeat
113	    // (held key) so only distinct presses count; the engine no-ops in modes
114	    // that can't fly.
115	    if (evt.code === "Space" && !evt.repeat) {
116	      const now = performance.now();
117	      if (now - lastSpaceTapAt <= FLY_DOUBLE_TAP_WINDOW_SECONDS * 1000) {
118	        engine.dispatch({ type: "toggleFlight" });
119	        lastSpaceTapAt = -Infinity; // consume the pair so a third tap starts fresh
120	      } else {
121	        lastSpaceTapAt = now;
122	      }
123	    }
124	
125	    input.keys.add(evt.code);
126	    input.capsActive = evt.getModifierState("CapsLock");
127	    if (evt.code === "Space") evt.preventDefault();
128	  };
129	
130	  const onKeyUp = (evt: KeyboardEvent) => {
131	    input.keys.delete(evt.code);
132	    input.capsActive = evt.getModifierState("CapsLock");
133	  };
134	
135	  const onMouseDown = (evt: MouseEvent) => {
136	    if (uiBlocked()) return;
137	    if (!input.pointerLocked) return;
138	
139	    if (evt.button === 0) {
140	      input.leftMouseHeld = true;
141	      engine.dispatch({ type: "attack" });
142	    }
143	    if (evt.button === 2) engine.dispatch({ type: "placeBlock" });
144	  };
145	
146	  const onDoubleClick = () => {
147	    if (uiBlocked() || input.pointerLocked) return;
148	    // Starting play is deliberate: a single click remains inert, while the
149	    // double-click gesture acquires pointer lock without also mining.
150	    // The request can legitimately reject (recent Esc, unfocused document,
151	    // headless browsers); the game just stays unlocked.
152	    Promise.resolve(canvas.requestPointerLock()).catch(() => {});
153	  };
154	
155	  const onMouseUp = (evt: MouseEvent) => {
156	    if (evt.button !== 0) return;
157	    input.leftMouseHeld = false;
158	  };
159	
160	  const onContextMenu = (evt: MouseEvent) => evt.preventDefault();
161	
162	  const onPointerLockChange = () => {
163	    input.pointerLocked = document.pointerLockElement === canvas;
164	    onLockChange(input.pointerLocked);
165	    // Losing the lock during plain gameplay means the player pressed Escape
166	    // (or the browser took it away) — open the pause menu. The inventory and
167	    // death paths set their state flags before the lock change fires, and the
168	    // pause command itself ignores those states as a second guard.
169	    if (!input.pointerLocked) engine.dispatch({ type: "pause" });
170	  };
171	
172	  window.addEventListener("resize", onResize);
173	  document.addEventListener("mousemove", onMouseMove);
174	  document.addEventListener("keydown", onKeyDown);
175	  document.addEventListener("keyup", onKeyUp);
176	  document.addEventListener("mousedown", onMouseDown);
177	  document.addEventListener("dblclick", onDoubleClick);
178	  document.addEventListener("mouseup", onMouseUp);
179	  document.addEventListener("contextmenu", onContextMenu);
180	  document.addEventListener("pointerlockchange", onPointerLockChange);
181	
182	  return {
183	    input,
184	
185	    clearKeys() {
186	      input.keys.clear();
187	      input.leftMouseHeld = false;
188	    },
189	
190	    dispose() {
191	      window.removeEventListener("resize", onResize);
192	      document.removeEventListener("mousemove", onMouseMove);
193	      document.removeEventListener("keydown", onKeyDown);
194	      document.removeEventListener("keyup", onKeyUp);
195	      document.removeEventListener("mousedown", onMouseDown);
196	      document.removeEventListener("dblclick", onDoubleClick);
197	      document.removeEventListener("mouseup", onMouseUp);
198	      document.removeEventListener("contextmenu", onContextMenu);
199	      document.removeEventListener("pointerlockchange", onPointerLockChange);
200	    }
201	  };
202	}
203	
```

2	
3	import { useCallback, useEffect, useMemo, useRef, useState, useSyncExternalStore } from "react";
4	import { createAudioDirector, DEFAULT_AUDIO_SETTINGS, type AudioDirector, type AudioSettings } from "@/lib/game/audio/audioDirector";
5	import { readAudioSettings, writeAudioSettings } from "@/lib/game/audio/settings";
6	import { AUTOSAVE_INTERVAL_MS, HOTBAR_SLOTS, MAX_HUNGER, MAX_HEARTS, MAX_OXYGEN } from "@/lib/game/config";
7	import { GameEngine } from "@/lib/game/engine/GameEngine";
8	import type { GameApi, GameSnapshot } from "@/lib/game/engine/state";
9	import { createInputController, type InputController } from "@/lib/game/input/inputController";
10	import * as inv from "@/lib/game/inventory";
11	import { getSkinPreset, type SkinId } from "@/lib/game/playerSkins";
12	import { type Profile, setProfileSkin } from "@/lib/game/profiles";
13	import { createEmptyArmorEquipment, createInitialInventory, ITEM_DEF_BY_ID } from "@/lib/game/items";
14	import { RECIPES } from "@/lib/game/recipes";
15	import { GameRenderer } from "@/lib/game/render/GameRenderer";
16	import { createMinimapRenderer, type MinimapRenderer } from "@/lib/game/render/minimap";
17	import { readSave, writeSave } from "@/lib/game/save";
18	import type { ArmorSlot, EnchantmentId, Recipe } from "@/lib/game/types";
19	import type { GameMode } from "@/lib/game/gameModes";
20	import type { Difficulty } from "@/lib/game/difficulties";
21	import { type WorldMeta, worldSaveKey } from "@/lib/game/worlds";
22	
23	/**
24	 * Thin React shell around the headless GameEngine and the GameRenderer.
25	 *
26	 * The engine is created in the canvas mount's callback ref (commit phase) and
27	 * held in React state; the UI reads it through useSyncExternalStore snapshots
28	 * and sends intents back as engine commands. The effect below owns everything
29	 * with a lifecycle: renderer, input listeners, the rAF loop, and autosave.
30	 */
31	
32	// Pre-mount snapshot (also the SSR snapshot): the starter loadout at full stats.
33	const PRE_MOUNT_SNAPSHOT: GameSnapshot = {
34	  api: null,
35	  inventory: createInitialInventory(),
36	  equippedArmor: createEmptyArmorEquipment(),
37	  selectedSlot: 0,
38	  gameMode: "survival",
39	  difficulty: "normal",
40	  hardcore: false,
41	  gameOver: false,
42	  isFlying: false,
43	  hearts: MAX_HEARTS,
44	  hunger: MAX_HUNGER,
45	  oxygen: MAX_OXYGEN,
46	  daylightPercent: 100,
47	  passiveCount: 0,
48	  hostileCount: 0,
49	  respawnSeconds: 0,
50	  inventoryOpen: false,
51	  paused: false,
52	  debugOpen: false,
53	  debug: null,
54	  cameraMode: "first",
55	  armorPoints: 0,
56	  capsActive: false,
57	  sleeping: false,
58	  craftingStation: null,
59	  container: null,
60	  boss: null,
61	  victory: false,
62	  activeEffects: [],
63	  xpLevel: 0,
64	  xpProgress: 0
65	};
66	
67	const noopSubscribe = () => () => {};
68	
69	type GameContext = { engine: GameEngine; node: HTMLDivElement };
70	
71	// Debug/test handle: lets the browser console and the Playwright E2E suite
72	// inspect the live simulation (single-player client game — nothing to protect).
73	declare global {
74	  interface Window {
75	    __monecraft?: { engine: GameEngine; renderer: GameRenderer; input: InputController; audio: AudioDirector };
76	  }
77	}
78	
79	function persistGame(api: GameApi, saveKey: string, onMessage: (text: string) => void): void {
80	  try {
81	    writeSave(saveKey, api.serialize());
82	    onMessage("Saved");
83	  } catch {
84	    onMessage("Save failed");
85	  }
86	}
87	
88	/**
89	 * One mounted game = one world played by one profile. The owning shell remounts
90	 * this hook (via a React `key` on the world id) to switch worlds, so the
91	 * per-world save key and the profile are fixed for the hook's lifetime.
92	 */
93	export type UseMinecraftGameOptions = {
94	  world: WorldMeta;
95	  profile: Profile;
96	  /** Leave this world and return to the world list (the shell unmounts us). */
97	  onQuitToWorlds: () => void;
98	  /** Re-read this world from disk by forcing a fresh mount (used by Load/Reset). */
99	  onReloadWorld: () => void;
100	};
101	
102	export function useMinecraftGame(opts: UseMinecraftGameOptions) {
103	  // The owning shell keys this hook by world id, so the world is fixed for the
104	  // mount's life; capturing it once in refs lets the long-lived rAF/autosave
105	  // effect read the save key and seed without re-subscribing.
106	  const saveKeyRef = useRef(worldSaveKey(opts.world.id));
107	  const worldSeedRef = useRef(opts.world.seed);
108	  const worldTypeRef = useRef(opts.world.worldType);
109	  const worldModeRef = useRef(opts.world.gameMode);

111	  const worldHardcoreRef = useRef(opts.world.hardcore);
112	  const [ctx, setCtx] = useState<GameContext | null>(null);

145	  const updateAudioSettings = useCallback((partial: Partial<AudioSettings>) => {

147	    audioSettingsRef.current = next;
148	    setAudioSettings(next);

153	  const updateSkin = useCallback(
154	    (id: SkinId) => {

166	    if (!node) {

169	    }

171	    // them); a fresh world (no blob yet) boots from the world's stored values.
172	    setCtx({
173	      engine: new GameEngine({
174	        save: readSave(saveKeyRef.current),
175	        seed: worldSeedRef.current,
176	        worldType: worldTypeRef.current,
177	        gameMode: worldModeRef.current,

191	  const engine = ctx?.engine ?? null;
192	  const subscribe = useMemo(() => engine?.subscribe ?? noopSubscribe, [engine]);
193	  const getSnapshot = useMemo(() => engine?.getSnapshot ?? (() => PRE_MOUNT_SNAPSHOT), [engine]);

195	  const snapshot = useSyncExternalStore(subscribe, getSnapshot, getServerSnapshot);

215	  const flashMessage = useCallback(

223	  useEffect(() => {

225	    const { engine: gameEngine, node } = ctx;

227	    const created = GameRenderer.create(node);

240	    const audio = createAudioDirector();

242	    audioRef.current = audio;
243	    const input = createInputController({
244	      canvas: renderer.domElement,

249	        // point, and it re-resumes a context suspended by the browser.

255	    // Autoplay policy: the AudioContext may only start inside a user gesture.

267	    window.__monecraft = { engine: gameEngine, renderer, input, audio };

269	    let minimap: MinimapRenderer | null = null;
270	    let last = performance.now();
271	    let animationFrame = 0;
272	    // Catch-up stepping: a slow frame (software GL, busy machine) can take far
273	    // longer than one 50ms step, and a single clamped step would run the
274	    // simulation in slow motion. Bounded substeps keep sim time tracking wall
275	    // time; the cap bounds work per frame and quietly drops time beyond it
276	    // (e.g. after a background-tab stall).
277	    const MAX_STEP_SECONDS = 0.05;
278	    const MAX_SUBSTEPS = 5;
279	    let pendingSeconds = 0;
280	    const clock = () => {
281	      const now = performance.now();
282	      pendingSeconds = Math.min(pendingSeconds + (now - last) / 1000, MAX_STEP_SECONDS * MAX_SUBSTEPS);
283	      last = now;
284	
285	      let frameSeconds = 0;
286	      while (pendingSeconds > 0) {
287	        const dt = Math.min(pendingSeconds, MAX_STEP_SECONDS);
288	        gameEngine.step(dt, input.input);
289	        pendingSeconds -= dt;
290	        frameSeconds += dt;
291	      }
292	
293	      for (const event of gameEngine.consumeEvents()) {
294	        if (event.type === "died" || event.type === "bossDefeated" || event.type === "gameOver") {
295	          // Free the cursor so the death/victory/game-over button is clickable; the
296	          // pause command ignores those states, so the lock-loss won't open the menu too.
297	          input.clearKeys();

299	        }

303	        if (event.type === "respawned") input.clearKeys();
304	        if (event.type === "attackSwung") renderer.triggerSwing();
305	        if (event.type === "openedStation" || event.type === "openedContainer") {
306	          // A furnace/chest opened the inventory from a mouse click — release the
307	          // keys and pointer lock the same way KeyI does on the DOM side.

312	          flashMessage("Not enough room to empty the chest");
313	        }
314	        if (event.type === "sleepDenied") {
315	          flashMessage(event.reason === "daylight" ? "You can only sleep at night" : "Monsters are nearby");
316	        }
317	        if (event.type === "pickedUp") {
318	          flashMessage(event.items.map((drop) => `+${drop.count} ${ITEM_DEF_BY_ID[drop.itemId]?.label ?? drop.itemId}`).join(", "));
319	        }
320	        if (event.type === "fishingCaught") {
321	          // Common fish/junk stay diegetic (splash + sound); only the rare treasure earns a toast.
322	          const treasure = event.items.find((drop) => drop.itemId === "emerald");
323	          if (treasure) flashMessage(`Reeled in treasure: ${ITEM_DEF_BY_ID[treasure.itemId]?.label ?? treasure.itemId}!`);
324	        }
325	        if (event.type === "summonFailed") {
326	          flashMessage("The totem lies dormant — a beast already walks");
327	        }

329	        audio.handleEvent(event);
330	      }
331	
332	      if (!minimap && minimapNodeRef.current) minimap = createMinimapRenderer(minimapNodeRef.current);
333	      // The minimap must read worldMeshDirty before renderer.sync clears it.
334	      minimap?.sync(gameEngine.state, now);
335	      renderer.sync(gameEngine.state, now);
336	      audio.sync(gameEngine.state, frameSeconds);

338	      animationFrame = requestAnimationFrame(clock);

340	    animationFrame = requestAnimationFrame(clock);

342	    return () => {

376	    attachMinimap,

385	    setDifficulty: (difficulty: Difficulty) => engine?.dispatch({ type: "setDifficulty", difficulty }),
386	    selectedSlot: snapshot.selectedSlot,

388	    capsActive: snapshot.capsActive,

51	export type AudioSettings = {
52	  /** 0..1 — everything. */
53	  master: number;
54	  /** 0..1 — the ambient pad only, under master. */
55	  music: number;
56	  muted: boolean;

58	
59	export const DEFAULT_AUDIO_SETTINGS: AudioSettings = { master: 0.8, music: 0.6, muted: false };
60	
61	/** The live WebAudio half, created lazily on unlock(). Injectable for tests. */
62	export type AudioGraph = {
63	  backend: SynthBackend;
64	  music: MusicPlayer;
65	  rain: RainLoop;
66	  setVolumes(master: number, music: number): void;
67	  resume(): void;
68	  dispose(): void;
69	};
70	
71	export type AudioDirector = {
72	  /** Builds/resumes the AudioContext. Must be called from a user gesture. */
73	  unlock(): void;
74	  handleEvent(event: GameEvent): void;
75	  /** Per-frame, after the engine step — drives all continuous sound. */
76	  sync(state: GameState, dt: number): void;
77	  setSettings(settings: AudioSettings): void;
78	  dispose(): void;
79	};
80	
81	/** Mining ticks per block — one per crack-overlay-ish stage. */
82	const MINING_TICK_STAGES = 4;
83	/** Touchdown speed that plays the landing thud at full volume. */
84	const FULL_LANDING_IMPACT = 12;
85	/** Seconds a newly entered biome must persist before the music follows it. */
86	const BIOME_HYSTERESIS_SECONDS = 5;
87	/** Music runs at quarter volume behind the pause menu. */
88	const PAUSE_DUCK = 0.25;
89	
90	/** The production graph: zzfx + AudioContext, imported only inside a gesture. */
91	async function createDefaultGraph(): Promise<AudioGraph> {
92	  // zzfx instantiates its own module-scope AudioContext on import, so the
93	  // import itself must wait for the unlock gesture (autoplay policy, SSR).
94	  const { ZZFX } = await import("zzfx");
95	  const ctx = new AudioContext();
96	  const compressor = ctx.createDynamicsCompressor();
97	  compressor.connect(ctx.destination);
98	  const masterGain = ctx.createGain();
99	  masterGain.connect(compressor);
100	  const sfxGain = ctx.createGain();
101	  sfxGain.connect(masterGain);
102	  const musicGain = ctx.createGain();
103	  musicGain.connect(masterGain);
104	
105	  const backend = createSynthBackend(ctx, sfxGain, (...params) => ZZFX.buildSamples(...params), ZZFX.sampleRate);
106	  const music = createMusicPlayer(ctx, musicGain, createMusicBrain());
107	  const rain = createRainLoop(ctx, masterGain);
108	
109	  return {
110	    backend,
111	    music,
112	    rain,
113	    setVolumes(master, musicVolume) {
114	      masterGain.gain.value = master;
115	      musicGain.gain.value = musicVolume;
116	    },
117	    resume() {
118	      if (ctx.state === "suspended") void ctx.resume();
119	    },
120	    dispose() {
121	      rain.dispose();
122	      music.dispose();
123	      backend.dispose();
124	      void ctx.close();
125	    }
126	  };
127	}
128	
129	export type AudioDirectorDeps = {
130	  createGraph?: () => Promise<AudioGraph> | AudioGraph;
131	  rng?: () => number;
132	};
133	
134	export function createAudioDirector(deps: AudioDirectorDeps = {}): AudioDirector {
135	  const createGraph = deps.createGraph ?? createDefaultGraph;
136	  const footsteps = createFootstepScheduler();
137	  const mobAmbience = createMobAmbienceScheduler(deps.rng);
138	
139	  let graph: AudioGraph | null = null;
140	  let unlocking = false;
141	  let disposed = false;
142	  let settings = DEFAULT_AUDIO_SETTINGS;
143	
144	  // Continuous-sound trackers, all derived from state in sync().
145	  let lastX = 0;
146	  let lastZ = 0;
147	  let hasLastPosition = false;
148	  let miningKey = "";
149	  let miningStage = 0;
150	  let miningTickSound = HIT_TICK_SOUNDS.stone;
151	  let miningHardness = 2;
152	  let stableBiome = BiomeId.Plains;
153	  let pendingBiome = BiomeId.Plains;
154	  let pendingBiomeSeconds = 0;
155	  let ducked = false;
156	
157	  const applyVolumes = (): void => {
158	    graph?.setVolumes(settings.muted ? 0 : settings.master, (ducked ? PAUSE_DUCK : 1) * settings.music);
159	  };
160	
161	  const surfaceGroupUnder = (state: GameState, fx: number, fy: number, fz: number) => {
162	    let block = state.world.get(fx, fy - 1, fz) as BlockId;
163	    if (block === BlockId.Air) block = state.world.get(fx, fy - 2, fz) as BlockId;
164	    return materialGroupFor(block);
165	  };
166	
167	  return {
168	    unlock() {
169	      if (disposed || graph || unlocking) {
170	        graph?.resume();
171	        return;
172	      }
173	      unlocking = true;
174	      void Promise.resolve(createGraph())
175	        .then((created) => {
176	          if (disposed) {
177	            created.dispose();
178	            return;
179	          }
180	          graph = created;
181	          // The constructor gesture may have expired by this microtask —
182	          // resume explicitly so the context reliably leaves "suspended".
183	          created.resume();
184	          applyVolumes();
185	        })
186	        .catch(() => {
187	          // Audio stays optional; the next gesture retries from scratch.
188	        })
189	        .finally(() => {
190	          unlocking = false;
191	        });
192	    },
193	
194	    handleEvent(event) {
195	      const backend = graph?.backend;
196	      if (!backend) return;
197	      switch (event.type) {
198	        case "blockBroken":
199	          backend.play(BREAK_SOUNDS[materialGroupFor(event.blockId)]);
200	          break;
201	        case "blockPlaced":
202	          backend.play(PLACE_SOUNDS[materialGroupFor(event.blockId)]);
203	          break;
204	        case "playerHurt":
205	          backend.play(HURT_SOUND);
206	          break;
207	        case "ateFood":
208	          backend.play(EAT_SOUND);
209	          break;
210	        case "drankPotion":
211	          backend.play(DRINK_SOUND);
212	          break;
213	        case "xpGained":
214	          backend.play(XP_SOUND);
215	          break;
216	        case "enchanted":
217	          backend.play(ENCHANT_SOUND);
218	          break;
219	        case "anvilCombined":
220	        case "anvilRepaired":
221	          backend.play(ENCHANT_SOUND);
222	          break;
223	        case "anvilRenamed":
224	          backend.play(XP_SOUND);
225	          break;
226	        case "grindstoneStripped":
227	          backend.play(XP_SOUND);
228	          break;
229	        case "jumped":
230	          backend.play(JUMP_SOUND);
231	          break;
232	        case "landed":
233	          backend.play(LAND_SOUND, { gain: Math.min(1.2, Math.max(0.3, event.impact / FULL_LANDING_IMPACT)) });
234	          break;
235	        case "mobAttacked":
236	          backend.play(MOB_ATTACK_SOUNDS[event.kind]);
237	          break;
238	        case "mobHit":
239	          backend.play(MOB_HIT_SOUND);
240	          break;
241	        case "mobDied":
242	          backend.play(MOB_DEATH_SOUND);
243	          break;
244	        case "mobSpawned":
245	          backend.play(MOB_SPAWN_SOUND);
246	          break;
247	        case "bowFired":
248	          backend.play(BOW_FIRE_SOUND);
249	          break;
250	        case "arrowHit":
251	          backend.play(ARROW_HIT_SOUND);
252	          break;
253	        case "bossSummoned":
254	          backend.play(BOSS_ROAR_SOUND);
255	          break;
256	        case "bossDefeated":
257	          backend.play(VICTORY_SOUND);
258	          break;
259	        case "explosion":
260	          backend.play(EXPLOSION_SOUND);
261	          break;
262	        case "tntPrimed":
263	          backend.play(TNT_FUSE_SOUND);
264	          break;
265	        case "died":
266	          backend.play(DEATH_SOUND);
267	          break;
268	        case "respawned":
269	          backend.play(RESPAWN_SOUND);
270	          break;
271	        case "sleepStarted":
272	          backend.play(SLEEP_SOUND);
273	          break;
274	        case "wokeUp":
275	          backend.play(WAKE_SOUND);
276	          break;
277	        case "tilledSoil":
278	          backend.play(TILL_SOUND);
279	          break;
280	        case "plantedSeed":
281	        case "plantedSapling":
282	          backend.play(PLANT_SOUND);
283	          break;
284	        case "usedBoneMeal":
285	          backend.play(BONE_MEAL_SOUND);
286	          break;
287	        case "fishingCast":
288	          backend.play(FISHING_CAST_SOUND);
289	          break;
290	        case "fishingBite":
291	          backend.play(FISHING_BITE_SOUND);
292	          break;
293	        case "fishingCaught":
294	          backend.play(FISHING_CATCH_SOUND);
295	          break;
296	        case "fishingReeledEmpty":
297	          backend.play(FISHING_REEL_EMPTY_SOUND);
298	          break;
299	        case "smelted":
300	          backend.play(SMELT_SOUND);
301	          break;
302	        case "openedContainer":
303	          backend.play(CHEST_OPEN_SOUND);
304	          break;
305	        case "doorToggled":
306	          backend.play(PLACE_SOUNDS.wood, { gain: event.open ? 0.8 : 1 });
307	          break;
308	        case "mobFed":
309	          backend.play(MOB_FED_SOUND);
310	          break;
311	        case "mobBred":
312	          backend.play(MOB_BRED_SOUND);
313	          break;
314	      }
315	    },
316	
317	    sync(state, dt) {
318	      if (!graph) return;
319	      const { player, world } = state;
320	      const fx = Math.floor(player.position.x);
321	      const fy = Math.floor(player.position.y);
322	      const fz = Math.floor(player.position.z);
323	
324	      // Footsteps from actual movement deltas (knockback and walking alike).
325	      if (!hasLastPosition) {
326	        lastX = player.position.x;
327	        lastZ = player.position.z;
328	        hasLastPosition = true;
329	      }
330	      const dx = player.position.x - lastX;
331	      const dz = player.position.z - lastZ;
332	      lastX = player.position.x;
333	      lastZ = player.position.z;
334	      if (!state.paused && !state.isDead && footsteps.tick(player.onGround, dx, dz)) {
335	        graph.backend.play(FOOTSTEP_SOUNDS[surfaceGroupUnder(state, fx, fy, fz)]);
336	      }
337	
338	      // Mining hit ticks: one per quarter of the block's hardness.
339	      if (state.mining.targetKey !== miningKey) {
340	        miningKey = state.mining.targetKey;
341	        miningStage = 0;
342	        if (miningKey) {
343	          const [bx, by, bz] = miningKey.split(",").map(Number);
344	          const block = world.get(bx, by, bz) as BlockId;
345	          miningTickSound = HIT_TICK_SOUNDS[materialGroupFor(block)];
346	          miningHardness = BREAK_HARDNESS[block] ?? 2;
347	        }
348	      }
349	      if (miningKey && state.mining.progress < miningHardness) {
350	        const stage = Math.floor((state.mining.progress / miningHardness) * MINING_TICK_STAGES);
351	        if (stage > miningStage) {
352	          miningStage = stage;
353	          graph.backend.play(miningTickSound);
354	        }
355	      }
356	
357	      // Mob ambience, frozen with the simulation while paused.
358	      if (!state.paused) {
359	        const calls = mobAmbience.tick(dt, state.mobs, player.position.x, player.position.z, player.yaw);
360	        for (const call of calls) {
361	          graph.backend.play(MOB_AMBIENT_SOUNDS[call.kind], { gain: call.gain, pan: call.pan });
362	        }
363	      }
364	
365	      // Music follows daylight immediately and the biome with hysteresis, so
366	      // zigzagging a chunk border doesn't whipsaw the mood.
367	      const biomeHere = world.getBiome(fx, fz);
368	      if (biomeHere === stableBiome) {
369	        pendingBiomeSeconds = 0;
370	      } else if (biomeHere === pendingBiome) {
371	        pendingBiomeSeconds += dt;
372	        if (pendingBiomeSeconds >= BIOME_HYSTERESIS_SECONDS) stableBiome = biomeHere;
373	      } else {
374	        pendingBiome = biomeHere;
375	        pendingBiomeSeconds = 0;
376	      }
377	      graph.music.sync(dt, moodFor(state.daylight, stableBiome));
378	
379	      // Rain bed follows rain intensity; snow is silent, and it goes quiet on pause.
380	      graph.rain.setIntensity(!state.paused && state.weather.kind === "rain" ? state.weather.intensity : 0);
381	
382	      if (ducked !== state.paused) {
383	        ducked = state.paused;
384	        applyVolumes();
385	      }
386	    },
387	
388	    setSettings(next) {
389	      settings = next;
390	      applyVolumes();
391	    },
392	
393	    dispose() {
394	      disposed = true;
395	      graph?.dispose();
396	      graph = null;
397	    }
398	  };
399	}
400

{
  "file_path": "/Users/hutusi/workspace/ai/david/monecraft/components/game/AdvancementsPanel.tsx",
  "content": "\"use client\";\n\nimport { useState } from \"react\";\nimport PixelImg from \"./PixelImg\";\nimport { itemIconUrl } from \"@/lib/ui/sprites\";\nimport { ADVANCEMENTS, ADVANCEMENT_CATEGORY_ORDER, STATS, type StatFormat } from \"@/lib/game/engine/systems/advancements\";\n\ntype AdvancementsPanelProps = {\n  /** Live stat counters, pulled on render via the engine's advancementState(). */\n  stats: Array<{ id: string; value: number }>;\n  /** Ids of the advancements the player has unlocked. */\n  unlocked: string[];\n  onClose: () => void;\n};\n\ntype Tab = \"advancements\" | \"statistics\";\n\nconst TABS: ReadonlyArray<{ id: Tab; label: string }> = [\n  { id: \"advancements\", label: \"Advancements\" },\n  { id: \"statistics\", label: \"Statistics\" }\n];\n\nfunction formatDuration(totalSeconds: number): string {\n  const seconds = Math.max(0, Math.floor(totalSeconds));\n  const hours = Math.floor(seconds / 3600);\n  const minutes = Math.floor((seconds % 3600) / 60);\n  const rest = seconds % 60;\n  if (hours > 0) return `${hours}h ${minutes}m`;\n  if (minutes > 0) return `${minutes}m ${rest}s`;\n  return `${rest}s`;\n}\n\nfunction formatStat(value: number, format: StatFormat): string {\n  if (format === \"duration\") return formatDuration(value);\n  if (format === \"distance\") return `${Math.round(value).toLocaleString()} blocks`;\n  return Math.round(value).toLocaleString();\n}\n\nexport default function AdvancementsPanel({ stats, unlocked, onClose }: AdvancementsPanelProps) {\n  const [tab, setTab] = useState<Tab>(\"advancements\");\n  const unlockedSet = new Set(unlocked);\n  const statValues = new Map(stats.map((entry) => [entry.id, entry.value]));\n  const earned = ADVANCEMENTS.filter((advancement) => unlockedSet.has(advancement.id)).length;\n\n  const groups = ADVANCEMENT_CATEGORY_ORDER.map((category) => ({\n    category,\n    entries: ADVANCEMENTS.filter((advancement) => advancement.category === category)\n  })).filter((group) => group.entries.length > 0);\n\n  return (\n    <div className=\"adv-overlay\" role=\"dialog\" aria-modal=\"true\" aria-label=\"Advancements and statistics\">\n      <div className=\"adv-panel\">\n        <div className=\"adv-header\">\n          <div className=\"adv-tabs\">\n            {TABS.map((entry) => (\n              <button key={entry.id} className=\"mc-button adv-tab\" aria-pressed={tab === entry.id} onClick={() => setTab(entry.id)}>\n                {entry.label}\n              </button>\n            ))}\n          </div>\n          <button className=\"mc-button adv-close\" onClick={onClose}>\n            Close\n          </button>\n        </div>\n\n        {tab === \"advancements\" ? (\n          <div className=\"adv-body\">\n            <div className=\"adv-progress\">\n              {earned} / {ADVANCEMENTS.length} unlocked\n            </div>\n            {groups.map((group) => (\n              <div key={group.category} className=\"adv-group\">\n                <div className=\"adv-category\">{group.category}</div>\n                <div className=\"adv-grid\">\n                  {group.entries.map((advancement) => {\n                    const isUnlocked = unlockedSet.has(advancement.id);\n                    return (\n                      <div key={advancement.id} className={`adv-entry${isUnlocked ? \" is-unlocked\" : \" is-locked\"}`} data-testid={`adv-${advancement.id}`}>\n                        <PixelImg src={itemIconUrl(advancement.icon)} alt=\"\" size={32} aria-hidden />\n                        <div className=\"adv-entry-text\">\n                          <div className=\"adv-entry-title\">{advancement.title}</div>\n                          <div className=\"adv-entry-desc\">{advancement.description}</div>\n                        </div>\n                      </div>\n                    );\n                  })}\n                </div>\n              </div>\n            ))}\n          </div>\n        ) : (\n          <div className=\"adv-body\">\n            <div className=\"adv-stats\">\n              {STATS.map((stat) => (\n                <div key={stat.id} className=\"adv-stat-row\">\n                  <span className=\"adv-stat-label\">{stat.label}</span>\n                  <span className=\"adv-stat-value\">{formatStat(statValues.get(stat.id) ?? 0, stat.format)}</span>\n                </div>\n              ))}\n            </div>\n          </div>\n        )}\n      </div>\n    </div>\n  );\n}\n"
}

import { describe, expect, test } from "bun:test";
import { render, screen } from "@testing-library/react";
import ActiveEffects from "./ActiveEffects";

describe("ActiveEffects", () => {
  test("renders nothing when no effects are active", () => {
    const { container } = render(<ActiveEffects effects={[]} />);
    expect(container.firstChild).toBeNull();
  });

  test("shows one labelled chip per effect with an m:ss countdown", () => {
    render(
      <ActiveEffects
        effects={[
          { id: "speed", seconds: 65 },
          { id: "poison", seconds: 8 }
        ]}
      />
    );

    expect(screen.getAllByRole("listitem")).toHaveLength(2);
    expect(screen.getByText("1:05")).toBeTruthy();
    expect(screen.getByText("0:08")).toBeTruthy();
    expect(screen.getByRole("listitem", { name: /Swiftness: 1:05/ })).toBeTruthy();
    expect(screen.getByRole("listitem", { name: /Poison: 0:08/ })).toBeTruthy();
  });
});

`fireEvent` is the pattern. To keep tests locale-independent, let me switch the panel's number formatting from `toLocaleString()` to a deterministic thousands grouper:

> TOOL

tool_use Edit
id: toolu_019MogdYG9H2wBbP5VXzAN1J
```json
{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/Users/hutusi/workspace/ai/david/monecraft/components/game/AdvancementsPanel.tsx",
  "old_string": "function formatStat(value: number, format: StatFormat): string {\n  if (format === \"duration\") return formatDuration(value);\n  if (format === \"distance\") return `${Math.round(value).toLocaleString()} blocks`;\n  return Math.round(value).toLocaleString();\n}",
  "new_string": "/** Thousands separators without locale dependence (so the display is stable everywhere). */\nfunction groupThousands(value: number): string {\n  return Math.round(value)\n    .toString()\n    .replace(/\\B(?=(\\d{3})+(?!\\d))/g, \",\");\n}\n\nfunction formatStat(value: number, format: StatFormat): string {\n  if (format === \"duration\") return formatDuration(value);\n  if (format === \"distance\") return `${groupThousands(value)} blocks`;\n  return groupThousands(value);\n}"
}

// count, thousands-separated\n    expect(screen.getByText(\"1h 2m\")).toBeTruthy(); // duration (3725s)\n    expect(screen.getByText(\"4,096 blocks\")).toBeTruthy(); // distance\n  });\n\n  test(\"missing stats render as zero\", () => {\n    render(<AdvancementsPanel stats={[]} unlocked={[]} onClose={noop} />);\n    fireEvent.click(screen.getByRole(\"button\", { name: \"Statistics\" }));\n    expect(screen.getByText(\"0s\")).toBeTruthy(); ```
1	# Save format & compatibility
2	
3	## Schema
4	
5	`SaveData` (version 12) in `lib/game/types.ts`:
6	
7	- world `seed`
8	- `worldType` (optional) — the generation preset (`"default"` | `"flat"` | `"amplified"` | `"islands"`); absent ⇒ `"default"`. Like `seed`, it is fixed for the world's life and the world must always regenerate with it, since the block-diffs were recorded against that terrain
9	- `gameMode` (optional) — the player's game mode (`"survival"` | `"creative"` | `"adventure"` | `"spectator"`); absent ⇒ `"survival"`. Unlike `worldType`, this is **switchable in-game** (pause menu), so the saved value is the _current_ mode, not the one chosen at creation. Restored by `restoreGameMode` (`lib/game/save.ts`); `isFlying` is session-only and never serialized
10	- `difficulty` (optional) — the player's difficulty (`"peaceful"` | `"easy"` | `"normal"` | `"hard"`); absent ⇒ `"normal"`. An axis orthogonal to `gameMode`, and likewise **switchable in-game** (pause menu), so the saved value is the _current_ difficulty. Restored by `restoreDifficulty` (`lib/game/save.ts`); the per-level multipliers live in `lib/game/difficulties.ts`, the starvation accumulator (`starvationTimer`) is session-only
11	- `hardcore` (optional) — the Hardcore flag (`boolean`); absent ⇒ `false`. **Immutable** (unlike `gameMode`/`difficulty`): a hardcore world is forced to Survival + Hard and the engine locks both switchers. Restored by `restoreHardcore`
12	- `gameOver` (optional) — the permadeath flag (`boolean`); absent ⇒ `false`. Set once a hardcore run has ended in death; a reloaded `gameOver` world boots into the spectator "dead world" state (never playable). Restored by `restoreGameOver`. (The transient `isDead`/`respawnTimer` stay session-only — hardcore death is captured by this flag instead.)
13	- a block **diff** list — `changes: [blockIndex, blockId][]`; edits that revert to the generated baseline are pruned (player-placed crops, beds, furnaces, chests, and both halves/states of doors ride this list — they are ordinary block edits)
14	- inventory slots with durability, **enchantments, and a custom name** (36 slots; the first 9 are the hotbar). Each `SavedSlot` is `{ id, count, durability?, enchantments?, customName? }`; `enchantments` (`{ id, level }[]`) and `customName` (the anvil rename) ride durable gear only and are validated on load (`restoreSlot` — known enchant ids via `Object.hasOwn`, levels clamped to `1..ENCHANT_MAX_LEVEL`; `customName` trimmed and capped to `CUSTOM_NAME_MAX_LEN`, blanks dropped). The same path serializes chest contents
15	- `xp` (optional) — banked experience points (`XP_PER_LEVEL` points = one level). Earned from kills/ore/fishing, spent at the enchanting table. Restored by `restoreXp` (finite, ≥ 0). Unlike effects, XP is a currency and is **kept across death**
16	- equipped armor — the worn pieces themselves (one per slot, dedicated storage), persisted as per-slot `SavedSlot` via `serializeEquippedArmor`/`restoreEquippedArmor` (so worn durability/enchantments/customName survive). A worn piece lives **only** here, not in the 36 inventory slots
17	- selected hotbar slot (0–8)
18	- player position — validated on load by `restorePlayerPosition` (`lib/game/save.ts`): coords keep their fractional part (the player isn't grid-aligned) but a missing or non-finite (NaN/Infinity) position is rejected, leaving the fresh spawn in place so a corrupt blob can't load a broken world or slip past the `position.y < 2` unstuck net
19	- `dayClock`, `hearts`, `hunger` (all optional) — time of day and player stats, so sleeping through the night and a hurt/hungry player survive a reload
20	- `spawnPoint` (optional, `{x,y,z}` or null) — the bed respawn point; respawn falls back to a random land point when it is null or the block there is no longer a bed
21	- `blockEntities` (optional) — chest contents as `{ index, slots }[]`, keyed by the block's voxel index (the same index space as `changes`). Only **non-empty** chests are stored; an empty one carries no data and is recreated empty when opened. On load each entry is kept only if that index still holds a Chest block. This is the first per-block metadata in the format — `lib/game/save.ts` (`serializeContainers` / `readContainers`) handles it
22	- `effects` (optional) — active status effects as `{ id, remaining }[]` (remaining in seconds). Drink-granted buffs (Speed/Strength/Regeneration/Fire Resistance/Water Breathing) and the rotten-flesh Poison hazard live in `state.effects` (a Map) and persist here, but are **cleared on death**, so a saved-then-reloaded world resumes whatever was active at save time. On load, `restoreEffects` (`lib/game/save.ts`) drops unknown ids and non-positive/garbage durations
23	- `lootedChests` (optional) — voxel indices of dungeon loot chests the player has already opened or broken. Dungeon chests are placed by worldgen, so their contents are **not** in `changes`/`blockEntities` until accessed; the engine fills them lazily on first access (loot seeded from `world.seed ^ index`) and records the index here. Because an emptied chest drops out of `blockEntities`, this set — not the chest's emptiness — is what prevents a re-roll on reload. The session-only set of _which_ indices are dungeon chests/spawners is not stored; it is re-derived from the seed each load via `collectDungeonSites` (`lib/world/generation.ts`). Handled by `serializeLootedChests` / `readLootedChests` in `lib/game/save.ts`
24	
25	Each world's `SaveData` is stored in its own localStorage key, `minecraft_world_save_<worldId>` (`worldSaveKey` in `lib/game/worlds.ts`). Read/write and restore validation live in `lib/game/save.ts` — `readSave`/`writeSave` take the key as a parameter and the `Storage` is injectable for tests; `GameEngine.serialize()` produces the save from live state, and `lib/game/engine/blockChanges.ts` maintains the block diff. (The legacy single-world key `SAVE_KEY` = `minecraft_save_v7` is now read only once, by the migration described under [Profiles & worlds](#profiles--worlds).)
26	
27	Audio volume preferences live under a **separate**, global key (`minecraft_audio_v1`, `lib/game/audio/settings.ts`), shared across all profiles and not part of any world save or its versioning.
28	
29	**Per-voxel light is never serialized.** It is a derived cache (`world.light`), recomputed from the blocks at load by `computeFullLight` and patched locally on every edit (`lib/world/lighting.ts`); the bake reads only `world.blocks`, so the save needs no lighting field.
30	
31	Continuous-water exposure, lava burn, and the oxygen/drowning meter all live only
32	in transient `GameTimers`/state. Saving, reloading, or respawning resets them
33	(breath restores to full). The status-effect **accumulators** (`effectRegenTimer`,
34	`effectPoisonTimer`) are likewise transient — but the effects **themselves**
35	(`state.effects`) persist via the `effects` field above and survive a reload.
36	
37	### Version history
38	
39	- **Unreleased (no save bump)** — new enchantments (**Power**, **Punch**, **Knockback**, **Looting**, **Fortune**, **Feather Falling**) ride the existing per-slot `enchantments` field, and new status effects (**Haste**, **Resistance**, **Jump Boost**) ride the existing top-level `effects` field. Their ids are added to the save validators' known-id sets (`VALID_ENCHANT_IDS` / `VALID_EFFECT_IDS`, both compile-enforced `Record<…, true>`). No new field, no new `BlockId`, no worldgen change — the save schema stays **v12** and **`WORLDGEN_VERSION` stays at 8** (the same additive-id pattern as Mending). Older builds silently drop unknown ids on load.
40	- **v12** (dedicated armor storage) — fixes a bug where equipped armor stayed in the inventory and occupied a hotbar/storage slot. `equippedArmor` is no longer a by-id reference into `inventorySlots` but the worn pieces themselves, persisted per slot as `SavedSlot` (via `serializeEquippedArmor`/`restoreEquippedArmor`, reusing the shared `restoreSlot` validation). `migrateSaveV11toV12` moves each legacy by-id equip out of `inventorySlots` into the armor record (preserving durability/enchantments/customName; equips whose item isn't found or isn't valid armor are dropped; a legacy `inventoryCounts`-only save just drops the equip map and keeps the items). No worldgen change — **`WORLDGEN_VERSION` stays at 8** and `SAVE_KEY` is unchanged.
41	- **v11** (anvil rename & Mending) — adds an optional `customName` on each `SavedSlot` (the anvil rename). `migrateSaveV10toV11` is a pure version bump (absent ⇒ no custom name). `customName` rides the existing `inventorySlotsSnapshot`/`restoreSlot` path (shared by inventory and chests), is restored on durable gear only, and is trimmed + capped to `CUSTOM_NAME_MAX_LEN` with blanks dropped. The **Mending** enchantment adds no new field — it rides the existing per-slot `enchantments` (and its `mending` id is added to the save validator's known-id set). The **Anvil** and **Grindstone** items save by string id and their `BlockId`s are **appended** so saved block ids never shift. No worldgen change — both are craft-only — so **`WORLDGEN_VERSION` stays at 8**.
42	- **v10** (Hardcore) — adds optional top-level `hardcore` and `gameOver` booleans. `migrateSaveV9toV10` is a pure version bump (both absent ⇒ a normal, non-hardcore world, via `restoreHardcore`/`restoreGameOver`). `hardcore` is immutable (forces Survival + Hard, locks both switchers); `gameOver` is the persisted permadeath flag, so a dead hardcore world reloads into its spectator state rather than respawning (the transient `isDead`/`respawnTimer` remain session-only). No worldgen change — so **`WORLDGEN_VERSION` stays at 8**.
43	- **v9** (difficulty) — adds an optional top-level `difficulty` (`"peaceful"` | `"easy"` | `"normal"` | `"hard"`). `migrateSaveV8toV9` is a pure version bump (absent ⇒ `"normal"`, via `restoreDifficulty`). Difficulty is an axis orthogonal to `gameMode`, switchable in-game, so the _current_ level is what's stored; the per-level multipliers live in `lib/game/difficulties.ts` and the `starvationTimer` accumulator is session-only. No worldgen change — difficulty touches no generation — so **`WORLDGEN_VERSION` stays at 8**.
44	- **v8** (game modes) — adds an optional top-level `gameMode` (`"survival"` | `"creative"` | `"adventure"` | `"spectator"`). `migrateSaveV7toV8` is a pure version bump (absent ⇒ `"survival"`, via `restoreGameMode`). The mode is switchable in-game, so the _current_ mode is what's stored; the transient `isFlying` is never serialized. No worldgen change — modes touch no generation — so **`WORLDGEN_VERSION` stays at 8**.
45	- **v7** (XP & enchanting) — adds optional top-level `xp` and an optional `enchantments` array on each `SavedSlot`. `migrateSaveV6toV7` is a pure version bump (absent → xp 0, no enchantments). XP persists and is **kept across death** (it's a currency, not a status effect); enchantments ride the existing `inventorySlotsSnapshot`/`restoreSlot` path (shared by inventory and chests) and are validated on load. The **Enchanting Table** item saves by string id and the `EnchantingTable` `BlockId` is **appended** (id 52) so saved block ids never shift. No worldgen change — **`WORLDGEN_VERSION` stays at 8**.
46	- **v6** (status effects, brewing & potions) — adds optional `effects` (`{ id, remaining }[]`). `migrateSaveV5toV6` is a pure version bump (absent → no active effects), so pre-effect saves load clean. Effects persist but are cleared on death; `serializeEffects`/`restoreEffects` (`lib/game/save.ts`) round-trip and validate them. Potions, glass bottles, and the **Brewing Stand** item save by string id; the `BrewingStand` `BlockId` is **appended at the end** of the enum (id 51) so existing saved block ids never shift, and a placed stand persists in the block diff like any block. No worldgen change — the new effects/recipes touch no generation — so **`WORLDGEN_VERSION` stays at 8** and the determinism hashes are untouched.
47	- **v5** (unchanged for fishing) — the active cast is the session-only `state.fishing` field (a transient bobber, like in-flight arrows and thrown spears); it is never serialized and is cleared on respawn. The fishing rod and raw/cooked fish are additive items saved by string id, and a catch is an ordinary inventory change. No worldgen change, so neither the `version` nor `WORLDGEN_VERSION` moved.
48	- **v5** (unchanged for the living world — saplings, bone meal & grass spread) — every effect is an ordinary block edit that rides the `changes` diff: a planted sapling, a grown tree's wood and leaves, and re-grassed dirt all persist with no new fields, and bone meal is an additive item saved by string id. The `Sapling` `BlockId` is **appended at the end** of the enum (id 50) so existing saved block ids never shift. Crucially, the runtime tree grower (`systems/treeGrowth.ts`) shares **no** code with worldgen's hash-pinned `placeTrees`, so the generated terrain is byte-identical and **neither the `version` nor `WORLDGEN_VERSION` moved**.
49	- **v5** (unchanged for villagers & trading) — the villager mob is transient like every mob, and trading needs no persisted state: a trade is a station-gated recipe (`station: "villager"`) run through the existing `craft` command, the open trade station (`craftingStation`) is session-only, and there are no per-trade use caps to store. **Emerald** and any traded goods save by string id in the existing inventory slots. No worldgen change (villagers spawn into the passive population; there are no village structures), so neither the `version` nor `WORLDGEN_VERSION` moved.
50	- **v5** (unchanged for creepers & TNT) — the creeper mob, gunpowder, and the explosion system add no persisted state: mobs are transient, **a creeper's lit fuse and a primed TNT block's countdown are session-only** (`MobState.fuseTimer` and `state.primedTnt` are never serialized — like the boss and in-flight arrows). Gunpowder and the TNT item save by string id; a **placed TNT block persists in the block diff** like any block, and a save written mid-fuse reloads with that TNT as an inert, un-lit block (and no creeper). There is no worldgen change — creepers are spawned and TNT is crafted — so neither the `version` nor `WORLDGEN_VERSION` moved. Blast-destroyed blocks are ordinary block-diff edits.
51	- **v5** (unchanged for cow/pig & coal) — the cow and pig mobs (and their meat/leather drops) add no persisted state; mobs are transient and the new foods save by string id. Coal ore, coal, and charcoal are likewise additive (the ore persists in the block diff, the items by string id), so the save _schema_ stays at version 5. But the new coal worldgen changes the deterministic terrain baseline, so **`WORLDGEN_VERSION` is bumped `7` → `8`**: a world whose recorded version differs discards its stale block-diffs and reboots from its seed. Coal runs on its own PRNG (`placeCoal`), so only underground coal cells differ from a v7 world — the surface, existing ores, trees, and structures are byte-identical (the meshing and structural-probe tests stay green; only the byte hashes were re-rolled).
52	- **v5** (unchanged for caves & lighting) — the lighting system adds no persisted state (light is a derived cache, recomputed at load), and lava is worldgen, not a saved field. So the save _schema_ stays at version 5. But the deep-cave lava changes the deterministic terrain baseline, so **`SAVE_KEY` is bumped `minecraft_save_v6` → `minecraft_save_v7`**: old block-diffs were computed against the lava-free world and must never be applied to the new one. Pre-lava saves are discarded in practice. Torches are additive items/blocks, persisted by string id / in the block diff like any other placed block.
53	- **v5** (unchanged for ranged combat & the endgame boss) — bows, arrows, the Cursed Totem, the Dragon Heart, and the Dragon Sword are **additive items**, persisted by string id in the existing inventory/container slots, so no schema change was needed. **Arrows in flight, the boss, its minions, and the `victory` flag are all transient** (never serialized — like mobs), and there is no worldgen change (the boss is summoned, not generated), so neither the `version` nor `SAVE_KEY` moved.
54	- **v5** — adds optional `lootedChests` (opened/broken dungeon loot chests). `migrateSaveV4toV5` is a pure version bump (absent → no dungeon chests yet looted). Unlike v3/v4, **`SAVE_KEY` is bumped to `minecraft_save_v6`** here — not because of the save _shape_ (the field is additive) but because the dungeon worldgen changed the deterministic terrain baseline, so old block-diffs would index against the wrong world. Pre-dungeon saves are therefore discarded in practice; the migration keeps the version chain complete regardless. `readSave` chains v1 → v2 → v3 → v4 → v5 → v6 → v7 → v8 → v9 → v10 → v11 → v12.
55	- **v4** — adds optional `blockEntities` (chest contents). `migrateSaveV3toV4` is a pure version bump (absent → restores as no containers), so **`SAVE_KEY` was unchanged** at the time (player-placed chests only; worldgen and the index formula were untouched) and v1/v2/v3 saves load fine.
56	- **v3** — adds optional `dayClock`, `hearts`, `hunger`, and `spawnPoint`. `migrateSaveV2toV3` is a pure version bump (the new fields stay absent and restore as engine defaults), so **`SAVE_KEY` is unchanged** and v1/v2 saves load fine.
57	- **v2** — same fields as v1, reinterpreted for the 36-slot inventory and 9-slot hotbar.
58	- **v1** — 40 slots, 10-slot hotbar. Still accepted: `readSave` runs `migrateSaveV1toV2`, which packs non-empty slots in order, merges stackable items into earlier stacks, clamps `selectedSlot` to 0–8, and drops items that overflow the smaller inventory.
59	- Unknown future versions are rejected (treated as no save).
60	
61	## Profiles & worlds
62	
63	Saves are organized two levels deep — a **profile** owns a list of **worlds**, and each world holds one `SaveData` blob — across three localStorage keys. All three are versioned `{ version: 1, ... }` envelopes and their readers are **total** (malformed entries are dropped, never thrown):
64	
65	- **`minecraft_profiles_v1`** (`lib/game/profiles.ts`) — the profile list plus `activeProfileId`. A profile is `{ id, name, skinId, createdAt }`: player identity only (name + skin). The skin moved here from the old global `minecraft_skin_v1` key; a dangling `activeProfileId` is repaired on read.
66	- **`minecraft_worlds_v1`** (`lib/game/worlds.ts`) — the world index. Each `WorldMeta` is `{ id, profileId, name, seed, worldType, worldgenVersion, createdAt, lastPlayedAt }`; `profileId` is the 1:N owner link and `lastPlayedAt` drives the most-recent-first ordering. The `seed` is resolved once at creation (blank → random, numeric text → clamped int, other text → stable FNV hash via `resolveSeed`); `worldType` is the chosen generation preset (sanitized to `"default"` on read for unknown/legacy entries). Both are the source of truth for regeneration and are also written into the per-world `SaveData` blob.
67	- **`minecraft_world_save_<worldId>`** — one `SaveData` blob per world (the schema above), read/written through `readSave`/`writeSave`.
68	
69	`worldgenVersion` records the `WORLDGEN_VERSION` (`lib/game/config.ts`, currently 8) the world was generated under. This **replaces the old whole-store `SAVE_KEY` bump**: when the worldgen baseline changes, bump `WORLDGEN_VERSION`, and a world whose recorded version differs has its stale block-diffs discarded and reboots from its stored seed — per-world, with no key rename and without touching unaffected worlds.
70	
71	**World types.** A world's `worldType` selects a generation preset (`lib/world/worldTypes.ts`). `generateWorld(world, worldType)` forks on it before any block write (`terrainConfigFor`, `lib/world/generation.ts`), varying only sea level and per-biome surface height; everything else reads `GEN`. The `"default"` branch returns the GEN values verbatim, so default worlds stay **byte-identical** to the pre-feature generator — the existing determinism hashes are unchanged. Each non-default type is its own deterministic save contract with its own SHA-256 baseline in `generation.test.ts`; `worldgenVersion` still guards all types (a deliberate change to any type's config bumps `WORLDGEN_VERSION`, discarding stale worlds of that vintage).
72	
73	The game shell (`components/GameShell.tsx`) drives a profile-select → world-select → play state machine and remembers the tab's active world in `sessionStorage` so a reload resumes it (a fresh tab cold-starts at profile-select). Deleting a profile cascades its worlds and their save blobs (`deleteWorldsForProfile`).
74	
75	### Legacy migration
76	
77	`migrateLegacySave` (`lib/game/legacyMigration.ts`) runs once on first boot, gated on the **absence** of `minecraft_profiles_v1` (its presence is the already-migrated flag, which survives a rollback that re-creates the legacy key). When a legacy `minecraft_save_v7` exists it creates a default "Player" profile (carrying the old global skin) and **copies** the save into one "My World" blob, then removes the legacy key — the copy precedes the delete so a mid-migration failure can never lose the original. A brand-new player with **no** legacy save gets nothing here; the menu opens straight into the create-profile form (`ProfileSelect` renders it whenever there are zero profiles), so their first act is naming and skinning a profile.
78	
79	## Autosave
80	
81	Every 15s via `setInterval`, plus on `beforeunload`, all writing to the active world's `minecraft_world_save_<id>` key. "Save & Quit to Worlds" also persists synchronously before leaving, because the interval is cleared on unmount and `beforeunload` does not fire on an in-app world switch.
82	
83	## Compatibility rules
84	
85	- Saves store **diffs against generated terrain**, so changing world generation or the voxel index formula (`x + z*sizeX + y*sizeX*sizeZ`) silently corrupts existing saves — bump `WORLDGEN_VERSION` when you do, and each world discards its stale diffs (rebooting from its seed) on the mismatch.
86	- Worldgen output is pinned by SHA-256 characterization tests in `lib/world/generation.test.ts`; they fail on any byte-level change. See [testing.md](testing.md) for the re-baseline policy. Caveat: the noise functions use `Math.sin`, whose exact results are engine-defined — the tests prove refactor purity on the pinned Bun version, not cross-browser save portability.
87	- Changing the save shape requires bumping the `version` field and handling (or discarding) old data. Round-trip tests live in `lib/game/save.test.ts` and `lib/game/engine/GameEngine.test.ts`.
88	- Durable gear restores at a maximum count of one per slot. The v1→v2 migration
89	  splits legacy stacked gear into separate slots until inventory capacity is
90	  exhausted; current malformed stacks are clamped to one.
91	- Note save-format/worldgen impact in PRs.
92	
```

Dungeon chests are placed by worldgen, so their contents are **not** in `changes`/`blockEntities` until accessed; the engine fills them lazily on first access (loot seeded from `world.seed ^ index`) and records the index here. Because an emptied chest drops out of `blockEntities`, this set — not the chest's emptiness — is what prevents a re-roll on reload. The session-only set of _which_ indices are dungeon chests/spawners is not stored; it is re-derived from the seed each load via `collectDungeonSites` (`lib/world/generation.ts`). Handled by `serializeLootedChests` / `readLootedChests` in `lib/game/save.ts`\n- `stats` (optional) — gameplay **statistics** as `{ id, value }[]` (blocks mined, mobs killed, items crafted/enchanted, potions drunk, fish caught, distance travelled, time played, …). Counters live in `state.stats` (a `Map<string, number>`) and are folded in at the engine's single emit chokepoint (`recordEvent`/`recordTick`, `lib/game/engine/systems/advancements.ts`). Like `xp` and **unlike `effects`**, they are a long-term record and are **kept across death**. Only positive values are written; `restoreStats` (`lib/game/save.ts`) drops non-string ids and negative/garbage values. The id set is open (per-recipe `crafted_<id>` and per-block `*_mined` counters), so there's no allow-list\n- `advancements` (optional) — the unlocked **advancement** ids as `string[]`. Unlock is uniform — `state.stats.get(stat) >= threshold` for each declarative entry in `ADVANCEMENTS` (`lib/game/engine/systems/advancements.ts`) — and ids accumulate in `state.advancements` (a `Set`). Also **kept across death**; `restoreAdvancements` (`lib/game/save.ts`) keeps non-empty strings only, de-duplicated. Unknown persisted ids (e.g. a renamed entry) are harmless: they round-trip but never re-unlock, since `evaluateAdvancements` only iterates the registry"
}

1	# Changelog
2	
3	All notable changes to this project are documented in this file.
4	
5	## [Unreleased]
6	
7	## [0.11.0] - 2026-06-28
8	
9	### Fixed
10	
11	- **Equipped armor no longer occupies a hotbar/inventory slot.** Armor was equipped _by reference_ — the worn piece stayed in the inventory and kept occupying its slot (and rendered twice: once in the hotbar/grid, once in the armor column). Armor now has **dedicated storage**: equipping **moves** the piece out of the inventory into its armor slot (freeing the slot, ending the double-render), and unequipping returns it to a free inventory slot (refused when the inventory is full, so nothing is lost). `EquippedArmor` now holds the worn `InventorySlot` instances; the defense/durability/Mending seams read them directly (no more find-by-id, which also removes the duplicate-piece ambiguity). The save schema bumps **v11 → v12** — `migrateSaveV11toV12` moves each legacy by-id equip out of `inventorySlots` into the armor record (preserving durability/enchantments/customName) — with **no worldgen change** (`WORLDGEN_VERSION` stays 8)
12	
13	### Added
14	
15	- **Three new potions — Haste, Resistance & Leaping**: brewed at the brewing stand from existing reagents (Gold Ore / Leather / Sliver Ore), on **zero new assets** and **no save-format or worldgen change**. **Haste** (`EFFECT_HASTE_MULTIPLIER` 1.4) speeds up mining (`mining.ts`); **Resistance** (`EFFECT_RESISTANCE_MULTIPLIER` 0.8) softens incoming **combat** damage only — lava/poison/void are untouched — at the `applyDamageWithArmor` seam; **Leaping** (Jump Boost, `EFFECT_JUMP_BOOST_VELOCITY` 2.0) raises the jump in `playerMotion.ts` (tuned to stay under the fall-damage threshold). Each is an engine-only buff with a procedurally-painted HUD icon (no renderer change); the three effect ids ride the existing top-level `effects` field (added to `VALID_EFFECT_IDS`), so no save bump
16	- **Tool & armor enchantments — Fortune & Feather Falling**: on **zero new assets** and **no save-format or worldgen change**. **Fortune** (tools) rolls up to `FORTUNE_BONUS_PER_LEVEL` (1) extra of an **ore** per level when you mine it (gated to the ore blocks in `rollBlockDrops`, read off the mining tool). **Feather Falling** (boots only, via the `itemIds` allow-list) cuts fall damage by `FEATHER_FALLING_REDUCE_PER_LEVEL` (0.15) per level — capped at `FEATHER_FALLING_MAX_REDUCTION` (0.8) — read off the worn boots at the `tickPlayerMotion` fall seam. Both ride the existing per-slot `enchantments` (added to `VALID_ENCHANT_IDS`), so no save bump
17	- **Weapon enchantments — Knockback & Looting**: two new enchantments for any weapon, on **zero new assets** and **no save-format or worldgen change**. **Knockback** adds `KNOCKBACK_PER_LEVEL` (0.4) to the melee shove per level (the base shove is now the named `MELEE_KNOCKBACK_IMPULSE` (0.75), no longer a magic number), read off the held weapon at the `tryAttackMob` seam. **Looting** rolls up to `LOOTING_BONUS_PER_LEVEL` (1) extra of each mob drop per level on a **melee** kill (the killing weapon's level is forwarded through the kill callback, so arrows/thrown spears/explosions apply none), in `rollMobDrops`. Both ride the existing per-slot `enchantments` (added to `VALID_ENCHANT_IDS`), so no save bump
18	- **Bow enchantments — Power & Punch**: two new enchantments for the existing enchanting table, on **zero new assets** and **no save-format or worldgen change**. **Power** adds `POWER_DAMAGE_PER_LEVEL` (2) arrow damage per level and **Punch** adds `PUNCH_KNOCKBACK_PER_LEVEL` (0.25) arrow knockback per level, both read off the held bow at the single `tryFireBow` seam. They're **bow-only**: `EnchantmentDef` gains an optional `itemIds` allow-list (`canEnchant` honors it on top of the item-kind gate), so these don't appear on swords (which are also `weapon`). Their ids ride the existing per-slot `enchantments` (added to `VALID_ENCHANT_IDS`), so no save bump (`WORLDGEN_VERSION` stays 8)
19	- **Equipped armor now shows on the player** in third-person (press **V**). Each worn piece renders as a procedurally-built voxel "shell" over the matching body part (helmet → head, face mask → visor, neck protection → collar, chestplate → torso + shoulder caps, leggings → upper legs, boots → feet), parented to the limb pivots so it animates with the walk/attack gait, and tinted toward the ore it's crafted from. Renderer-only (`playerModel.ts` builds the shells, `playerVisuals.ts` toggles each by what's worn) — zero new assets, no engine/save change
20	- **Grindstone**: a new crafted station (**2 cobble + 2 planks**) — the anvil's inverse, on **zero new assets** (a procedurally-painted stone-wheel block, reusing existing synth) and **no save-format or worldgen change**. Right-click to open the grindstone panel; with **enchanted** gear selected in the hotbar, **Remove enchantments** strips them all and refunds **`GRINDSTONE_REFUND_XP_PER_LEVEL` (5) XP per level** removed. Pure logic in `lib/game/grindstone.ts`, a new `grindstoneStrip` command, and a `GrindstoneColumn` panel; the `Grindstone` `BlockId` is **appended** (id 54), craft-only, so no worldgen baseline change
21	- **Anvil**: a new crafted station (**3 gold ore + 4 cobble**) for maintaining and consolidating gear, on **zero new assets** (a procedurally-painted dark-iron block, reusing existing synth for its clang). The save schema bumps **v10 → v11** (additive `customName`), with **no worldgen change** (`WORLDGEN_VERSION` stays 8)
22	  - Right-click to open the anvil panel, which — like the enchanting table — acts on the **selected hotbar** tool/weapon/armor and spends XP levels. Three actions: **Combine** a duplicate (adds the two durability bars + a bonus and **merges enchantments** at the higher level, consuming the duplicate); **Repair** with the item's tier material (e.g. diamond ore for a diamond sword, cobble for a stone pickaxe) for a chunk of durability without a second copy; and **Rename** (the custom name shows everywhere the item's label does)
23	  - **Architecture**: the new `Anvil` `BlockId` is **appended** (id 53) so saved block ids never shift, and it's craft-only so no worldgen baseline changes. Pure logic lives in `lib/game/anvil.ts` (`combineSlots`, `mergeEnchantments`, `findSacrificeIndex`, `materialRepair`, `sanitizeCustomName`), driven by new `anvilCombine`/`anvilRepair`/`anvilRename` commands. The anvil station id is threaded through the **engine/UI** `craftingStation` union only (not `Recipe.station`); the panel is a new `AnvilColumn`. A per-item `customName` (additive v11 field, durable gear only, trimmed + capped) persists the rename, surfaced everywhere via a shared `displayName` helper
24	- **Mending enchantment**: a fifth enchantment for the existing enchanting table, on **zero new assets** and **no worldgen change**. Applied to any durable tool/weapon/armor (like Unbreaking), it's **binary** (max level 1) and diverts the XP you pick up into **repairing** the gear you're holding or wearing instead of banking it — `MENDING_REPAIR_PER_XP` (2) durability per XP point, the held item first, then equipped armor. Implemented as one seam (`mendXp` in `enchantments.ts`) read once in `awardXp`, so XP earning, the HUD, and the sound still react to the gross pickup. The `mending` id rides the existing per-slot `enchantments`, so **no save-format bump** is needed for it
25	- **Hardcore mode**: a per-world **permadeath** toggle on the new-world form, all on **zero new assets**. The save schema bumps **v9 → v10** (additive `hardcore` + `gameOver`), with **no worldgen change**
26	  - **Locked to Survival + Hard.** A hardcore world forces the game mode to Survival and the difficulty to Hard and **disables both pause-menu switchers** (immutable for the world's life), so there's no flipping to Creative or lowering the difficulty to cheat death. Its hearts render in a darker **withered** variant (a recolour of the procedural heart sprite)
27	  - **Death is permanent → spectate the dead world.** On death there is no respawn: the player flips to a **Spectator** free camera over their dead world (mobs keep roaming but can't touch them) and a **Game Over** screen offers _Spectate World_ (minimizes so you can fly around), _Back to Worlds_, or _Delete World_. The `gameOver` flag persists, so reloading a dead world resumes spectating — it can never be played again
28	  - **Architecture**: `hardcore` is a per-world boolean (`WorldMeta` + a v10 `SaveData` field + `migrateSaveV9toV10`), resolved first in the engine so it overrides mode/difficulty. The two lethal damage seams divert to a new `GameEngine.triggerGameOver()` (sets `gameOver`, switches to spectator, leaves `isDead` cleared so the respawn screen never shows); `switchGameMode`/`switchDifficulty` no-op under hardcore (the death transition sets the mode directly); the shell force-saves on the `gameOver` event. New `GameOverScreen` component + a `GameShell` delete-world path
29	- **Difficulty**: the game gains Minecraft's difficulty axis — **Peaceful**, **Easy**, **Normal**, and **Hard** — orthogonal to the game mode, picked at world creation and switchable any time from the pause menu, all on **zero new assets**. The save schema bumps **v8 → v9** (additive), with **no worldgen change**
30	  - **Peaceful**: no hostile mobs spawn (and switching to Peaceful instantly despawns every hostile already around — a safe panic button, the boss included), health regenerates **twice as fast**, and an empty hunger bar never hurts you — full survival minus the danger, distinct from Creative
31	  - **Easy / Normal / Hard** scale two things together: **mob hit damage** (×0.5 / ×1 / ×1.5, applied at the strike — the per-mob templates are never mutated) and **hostile spawning** (cadence + the concurrent cap, ×16 → 8 / 16 / 24)
32	  - **Starvation** (new): an empty hunger bar now has a consequence — half a heart every few seconds, down to a difficulty-scaled floor. Easy stops at 5 hearts, Normal at half a heart, and **Hard can starve you to death**; Peaceful never starves. (This changes existing **Normal** worlds: previously a fully-empty hunger bar did nothing.)
33	  - **Architecture**: a new `lib/game/difficulties.ts` holds the `Difficulty` union plus **accessor functions** (`hostilesSpawn`, `mobDamageMultiplier`, `hostileSpawnIntervalScale`, `hostileCapScale`, `regenIntervalScale`, `starves`/`starvationFloorHp`) that each system reads, mirroring the game-mode predicate pattern. Difficulty rides the same per-world plumbing as `gameMode` (`WorldMeta` + a v9 `SaveData` field + `migrateSaveV8toV9`); the spawn gate keys **only** on difficulty and the damage/threat gate **only** on game mode, so the two axes stay independent (Peaceful stops spawns even in Creative; Creative is invulnerable even on Hard). New `setDifficulty` command (`GameEngine.switchDifficulty`); new `STARVATION_INTERVAL_SECONDS`/`STARVATION_HP` tunables
34	- **Game modes**: the game gains Minecraft's four ways to play — **Survival** (the original), **Creative**, **Adventure**, and **Spectator** — as a single per-player axis you pick at world creation and can switch any time from the pause menu, all on **zero new assets**. The save schema bumps **v7 → v8** (additive), with **no worldgen change**
35	  - **Creative**: fly (double-tap **Space**, then Space/crouch to rise/descend), take no damage and never hunger, break any block instantly, and place blocks without consuming them. The recipe book is replaced by a searchable **palette of every item** — click one to pull a stack into your inventory. The survival HUD bars hide
36	  - **Adventure**: survival in every respect (damage, hunger, mobs, combat, doors/chests/trading) **except you can't break or place blocks**, so a built world stays intact
37	  - **Spectator**: a free camera — fly through terrain (noclip), invisible and invulnerable, with no interaction at all; hostiles ignore you entirely and the HUD/hotbar hide
38	  - **Switching** modes mid-game (pause menu) refills your bars and clears any pending hazard damage, sets flight to match the mode, and lifts you out of a wall when leaving Spectator — so it's always safe to flip between them
39	  - **Architecture**: a new `lib/game/gameModes.ts` holds the `GameMode` union plus **intent predicates** (`takesDamage`, `canEditBlocks`, `freeBuild`, `canInteract`, `canFly`, `isNoclip`, `usesInventory`, `mobsThreaten`) that each system gates on, rather than comparing the raw id — so the four modes share one set of seams (the damage chokepoints in `playerLife.ts`, the block edits in `mining.ts`, aggro in `mobAI.ts`, flight/noclip in `playerMotion.ts`). `gameMode` rides the existing per-world `worldType` plumbing (`WorldMeta` + a v8 `SaveData` field + `migrateSaveV7toV8`); unlike `worldType` it's the _current_, switchable value, and the transient `isFlying` is never serialized. New `setGameMode`/`toggleFlight`/`creativeGiveItem` commands; new `FLY_SPEED`/`FLY_DOUBLE_TAP_WINDOW_SECONDS` tunables
40	
41	### Changed
42	
43	- **Pause menu reorganized into tabs**: the Esc menu had grown into one long scrolling column (action buttons, the game-mode grid, volume sliders, the skin picker, and a 12-row controls list). It's now split into **Game** (save / load / reset / quit + game mode), **Options** (sound + appearance), and **Controls** (the key reference), with **Back to Game** pinned on top. Pure presentation — no save-format, worldgen, or behavior change (`components/game/PauseMenu.tsx`).
44	
45	## [0.10.0] - 2026-06-21
```
